## 3. Exhaustive Chapter-by-Chapter Breakdown (4-Way Aligned: Gofatho Latin • Gofatho Fidel • Amharic • English)

### Gr8_Ch1 — Shemppo 1: Ooso Woga | ሼምፕፖ 1፦ ኦኦሶ ዎገ | ምዕራፍ 1፦ የሥራ ሥነ-ምግባር | Chapter 1: Work Ethics and Professional Integrity
- **Pages:** Book pp. 1–22 (PDF pp. 7–28) • **Source:** [Grade 8 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/160NHNy-4orPcAIk-L5t6tOEtZoWemz_-/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Ooso Woga* (ኦኦሶ ዎገ) — የሥራ ሥነ-ምግባር / Work Ethics (Judicial, Medical, and Educational Ethics) (pp. 9–11 (Book pp. 3–5))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Alame yooushon dumma dumma ooso macaratan oothiya oosanchchati de7oosona. | አለሜ ዮኦኡሾን ዱምመ ዱምመ ኦኦሶ መጨረተን ኦኦꬂየ ኦኦሰንችቻቲ ዴዖኦሶነ። | በዓለም ዙሪያ በተለያዩ የሥራ መስኮች የሚሠሩ ሠራተኞች አሉ። | Around the world, there are workers employed in various professional fields. |
| Pirdda immiya daannay pa ma7owa ma77idi, tishshi gida guuggen, uyidi mathottonashin pa ooso wogaa naagidi pirdday suure gidida, makkalatethi baynnanne lahey baynna pirdda immanaas koshshees. | ፒርድደ ኢምሚየ ደአንነይ ፐ መዖወ መዕዒዲ፣ ቲሽሺ ጊደ ጉኡግጌን፣ ኡዪዲ መꬆትቶነሺን ፐ ኦኦሶ ዎገአ ነአጊዲ ፒርድደይ ሱኡሬ ጊዲደ፣ መክከለቴꬂ በይንነንኔ ለሄይ በይንነ ፒርድደ ኢምመነአስ ኮሽሼእስ። | ፍርድ የሚሰጥ ዳኛ ልብሱን ለብሶ፣ በተረጋጋ አእምሮ፣ ጠጥቶ ሳይሰክር የሥራ ሥነ-ምግባሩን ጠብቆ ትክክለኛ፣ አድልዎ የሌለበትና እንከን የለሽ ፍርድ መስጠት ይገባዋል። | A presiding judge must wear proper attire, maintain a calm mind without intoxication, uphold judicial ethics, and render fair, impartial, and unblemished judgment. |
| Payyatethaa keethan oothiya aakimey pantta ooso ma7owa ma77anaas koshshees; hargganchchati odida hargge xuura naaganaas koshshees. | ፐይየቴꬃአ ኬእꬃን ኦኦꬂየ አአኪሜይ ፐንትተ ኦኦሶ መዖወ መዕዓነአስ ኮሽሼእስ፤ ሀርግገንችቻቲ ኦዲደ ሀርግጌ ጡኡረ ነአገነአስ ኮሽሼእስ። | በጤና ተቋም የሚሠራ ሐኪም የሥራ ልብሱን መልበስና ታካሚዎች የነገሩትን የሕመም ምሥጢር መጠበቅ ይገባዋል። | A doctor working in a health facility must wear professional attire and protect the confidentiality of patients' medical conditions. |
| Timirtte keethaa ooso minthidi poloy issi gade ikkonoome, deretetha, timirttenne polotika dichchaas bertte. | ቲሚርትቴ ኬእꬃአ ኦኦሶ ሚንꬂዲ ፖሎይ ኢስሲ ገዴ ኢክኮኖኦሜ፣ ዴሬቴꬃ፣ ቲሚርትቴንኔ ፖሎቲከ ዲችቻአስ ቤርትቴ። | የትምህርት ቤት ሥራን አጠናክሮ መፈጸም ለአንድ አገር ኢኮኖሚያዊ፣ ማኅበራዊ፣ ትምህርታዊና ፖለቲካዊ ዕድገት ቁልፍ ነው። | Diligently fulfilling educational work is foundational for a country's economic, social, educational, and political development. |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Haasaya guussay issi asi pa huuphen gidin haratara qoppidabata, tobbidabatanne qofa laamerettidabata ubbaa doonara woykko odettidi qonccisikko haasaya geetettees. Haasaya hiillay moode haasayanne moode gidonna haasaya geetettidi nam77an shaakettees.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሀአሰየ ጉኡስሰይ ኢስሲ አሲ ፐ ሁኡጴን ጊዲን ሀረተረ ቆፕፒደበተ፣ ቶብቢደበተንኔ ቆፈ ለአሜሬትቲደበተ ኡብበአ ዶኦነረ ዎይክኮ ኦዴትቲዲ ቆንጭጪሲክኮ ሀአሰየ ጌእቴትቴእስ። ሀአሰየ ሂኢልለይ ሞኦዴ ሀአሰየንኔ ሞኦዴ ጊዶንነ ሀአሰየ ጌእቴትቲዲ ነምዕዓን ሻአኬትቴእስ።
- **Amharic:** ንግግር ማለት አንድ ሰው በግሉም ሆነ ከሌሎች ጋር ያሰበውንና የተወያየውን በአንደበት ሲገልጽ ነው። የንግግር ክህሎት መደበኛ ንግግር (moode haasaya) እና ኢ-መደበኛ ንግግር (moode gidonna haasaya) ተብሎ በሁለት ይከፈላል።
- **English:** Speech (Haasaya) means expressing one's thoughts and discussions orally with others; speaking skill is divided into formal speech (moode haasaya) and informal speech (moode gidonna haasaya).
- **Gofatho Latin:** Aaho Ooso: A. Ooso wogaw, B. Heera geeshshatethaa naago, C. Timirtte, D. Biittay di7on meecettonna mela naago, E. Medheta haarota naago.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** አአሆ ኦኦሶ፦ አ። ኦኦሶ ዎገው፣ ብ። ሄእረ ጌእሽሻቴꬃአ ነአጎ፣ ጭ። ቲሚርትቴ፣ ድ። ቢኢትተይ ዲዖን ሜእጬትቶንነ ሜለ ነአጎ፣ እ። ሜꬌተ ሀአሮተ ነአጎ።
- **Amharic:** የቡድን ውይይት ርዕሶች፦ ሀ. የሥራ ሥነ-ምግባር፣ ለ. የአካባቢ ንጽሕና ጥበቃ፣ ሐ. ትምህርት፣ መ. የአፈር መሸርሸር መከላከል፣ ሠ. የተፈጥሮ ሀብት ጥበቃ።
- **English:** Oral Discussion Topics: A. Work ethics, B. Environmental sanitation, C. Education, D. Preventing soil erosion, E. Conserving natural resources.

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **ooso** | ኦኦሶ | `N` | allale | ሥራ / ተግባር | work / action / task |
| **suure** | ሱኡሬ | `ADJ` | luule | ትክክለኛ / ቀጥተኛ | fair / straight / upright |
| **payyatetha** | ፐይየቴꬃ | `N` | ifila | ጤንነት / ጤና | health / well-being |
| **dano** | ደኖ | `N` | qoho | አደጋ / ጉዳት | accident / hazard / harm |
| **woga** | ዎገ | `N` | maara | ሥርዓት / ባህል / ደንብ | ethics / custom / rule |
| **bonchcho** | ቦንችቾ | `N` | ishaliso | ክብር / እንክብካቤ | respect / honor / care |
| **go77a** | ጎዕዓ | `N` | maade | ጥቅም / ድጋፍ | benefit / usefulness / aid |
| **uuga** | ኡኡገ | `QUANT` | guutha | ጥቂት / የተወሰኑ | a few / some |
| **phalqqe** | ጳልቅቄ | `ADJ/N` | misennabaa | ተቃራኒ / የማይመሳሰል | opposite / contrary |
| **bala** | በለ | `N` | mooro | ስህተት / ጥፋት | error / fault / transgression |
| **xuura** | ጡኡረ | `N` | geema qofa | ምሥጢር | secret / confidentiality |
| **mootanchcha** | ሞኦተንችቻ | `N` | pirdda koyiya ase | ተሟጋች / ባለጉዳይ | litigant / disputant in court |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** 1. Sunthi (Nouns & 7 Subclasses): Buzo sunthi (Proper: Baqulo, Zentto, Sawula), Kothe sunthi (Common: na7a, boori, tamaare), Shiiqo sunthi (Collective: dago, wude, dere asa), Beettonabata sunthi (Abstract: era, cincca, siiqo, ufayssa), Miishe sunthi (Material: saaxine, shuchchi, oyde, mithi), Taybettiya sunthi (Countable), Taybettonna sunthi (Uncountable: haathe, maathi, dhiille).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 1። ሱንꬂ (ኖኡንስ & 7 ሱብጭለስሴስ)፦ ቡዞ ሱንꬂ (ፕሮፔር፦ በቁሎ፣ ዜንትቶ፣ ሰዉለ)፣ ኮꬄ ሱንꬂ (ጮምሞን፦ ነዓ፣ ቦኦሪ፣ ተመአሬ)፣ ሺኢቆ ሱንꬂ (ጮልሌጭቲቬ፦ ደጎ፣ ዉዴ፣ ዴሬ አሰ)፣ ቤእትቶነበተ ሱንꬂ (አብስትረጭት፦ እረ፣ ጪንጭጨ፣ ሲኢቆ፣ ኡፈይስሰ)፣ ሚኢሼ ሱንꬂ (መቴሪአል፦ ሰአጢኔ፣ ሹችቺ፣ ኦይዴ፣ ሚꬂ)፣ ተይቤትቲየ ሱንꬂ (ጮኡንተብሌ)፣ ተይቤትቶንነ ሱንꬂ (ኡንጮኡንተብሌ፦ ሀአꬄ፣ መአꬂ፣ ꬊኢልሌ)።
- **Amharic:** 1. ስም (Sunthi) እና 7 ንዑሳን ክፍሎች፦ የተጸውኦ ስም (Buzo)፣ የወል ስም (Kothe)፣ የጥቅል ስም (Shiiqo)፣ የረቂቅ ስም (Beettonabata)፣ የቁስ ስም (Miishe)፣ ተቆጣሪ ስም (Taybettiya) እና ኢ-ተቆጣሪ ስም (Taybettonna)።
- **English:** 1. Nouns (Sunthi) and 7 Subclasses: Proper (Buzo), Common (Kothe), Collective (Shiiqo), Abstract (Beettonabata), Material/Concrete (Miishe), Countable (Taybettiya), and Uncountable/Mass (Taybettonna).
- **Gofatho Latin:** 2. Sunqqo (Adjectives & 5 Subclasses): Keena sunqqo (Quantity: daro, guutha), Mala sunqqo (Color/Appearance: aarusse macci, karretha boori, wancco dorssa), Kanddo sunqqo (Character: lo77o asttamaare, keeha kaappo, mino zal77anchcha), Medheta sunqqo (Shape: luulo mithi, wobbe kado), Dabbothiya sunqqo (Affiliation: Goofa dere asata).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 2። ሱንቅቆ (አድጄጭቲቬስ & 5 ሱብጭለስሴስ)፦ ኬእነ ሱንቅቆ (ቁአንቲትይ፦ ደሮ፣ ጉኡꬃ)፣ መለ ሱንቅቆ (ጮሎር/አፕፔአረንጬ፦ አአሩስሴ መጭጪ፣ ከርሬꬃ ቦኦሪ፣ ወንጭጮ ዶርስሰ)፣ ከንድዶ ሱንቅቆ (ቻረጭቴር፦ ሎዕዖ አስትተመአሬ፣ ኬእሀ ከአፕፖ፣ ሚኖ ዘልዕዓንችቻ)፣ ሜꬌተ ሱንቅቆ (ሻፔ፦ ሉኡሎ ሚꬂ፣ ዎብቤ ከዶ)፣ ደብቦꬂየ ሱንቅቆ (አፍፊሊአቲኦን፦ ጎኦፈ ዴሬ አሰተ)።
- **Amharic:** 2. ቅጽል (Sunqqo) እና 5 ንዑሳን ክፍሎች፦ የመጠን ቅጽል (Keena)፣ የቀለም/መልክ ቅጽል (Mala)፣ የባህሪ ቅጽል (Kanddo)፣ የቅርጽ ቅጽል (Medheta) እና የዝምድና/ወገን ቅጽል (Dabbothiya)።
- **English:** 2. Adjectives/Modifiers (Sunqqo) and 5 Subclasses: Quantity (Keena), Color/Appearance (Mala), Character/Quality (Kanddo), Shape/Form (Medheta), and Relational/Affiliation (Dabbothiya).
- **Gofatho Latin:** 3. Peesho (Verbs & Tense): Tumi ubba wode maxaafiya nabbabees (Ha77i wode / Present); Tumi zine maxaafa nabbabis (Aadhdhida wode / Past); Tumi wontto maxaafe nabbabana (Sintha wode / Future).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 3። ፔእሾ (ቬርብስ & ቴንሴ)፦ ቱሚ ኡብበ ዎዴ መጠአፊየ ነብበቤእስ (ሀዕዒ ዎዴ / ፕሬሴንት)፤ ቱሚ ዚኔ መጠአፈ ነብበቢስ (አአꬍꬊደ ዎዴ / ፐስት)፤ ቱሚ ዎንትቶ መጠአፌ ነብበበነ (ሲንꬃ ዎዴ / ፉቱሬ)።
- **Amharic:** 3. ግስና ጊዜያት (Peesho)፦ ቱሚ ሁልጊዜ መጽሐፍ ያነባል (የአሁን ጊዜ)፤ ቱሚ ትናንት መጽሐፍ አነበበ (ኃላፊ ጊዜ)፤ ቱሚ ነገ መጽሐፍ ያነባል (የወደፊት ጊዜ)።
- **English:** 3. Verbs and Tense (Peesho): Present/Habitual (-ees/-awus), Past/Perfective (-is/-asu), and Future (-ana).
- **Gofatho Latin:** 4. Peeqo (Adverbs), Gugame (Sigetho Postpositions & Gasetho Conjunctions) & Tura (5 Phrase Types): Wode peeqo (zine, wontto), Besse peeqo (karen, zagon), Keena peeqo (daro, loythidi), Hanota peeqo (woxxishe, tuuqqi); Sigetho (-ppe, -ko, -s, mela); Gasetho (-nne, woykko, gidoppe attin); Suntha tura, Sunqqo tura, Peesho tura, Peeqo tura, Gugame tura.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 4። ፔእቆ (አድቬርብስ)፣ ጉገሜ (ሲጌꬆ ፖስትፖሲቲኦንስ & ገሴꬆ ጮንጁንጭቲኦንስ) & ቱረ (5 ጵረሴ ትይፔስ)፦ ዎዴ ፔእቆ (ዚኔ፣ ዎንትቶ)፣ ቤስሴ ፔእቆ (ከሬን፣ ዘጎን)፣ ኬእነ ፔእቆ (ደሮ፣ ሎይꬂዲ)፣ ሀኖተ ፔእቆ (ዎጥጢሼ፣ ቱኡቅቂ)፤ ሲጌꬆ (-ፕፔ፣ -ኮ፣ -ስ፣ ሜለ)፤ ገሴꬆ (-ንኔ፣ ዎይክኮ፣ ጊዶፕፔ አትቲን)፤ ሱንꬃ ቱረ፣ ሱንቅቆ ቱረ፣ ፔእሾ ቱረ፣ ፔእቆ ቱረ፣ ጉገሜ ቱረ።
- **Amharic:** 4. ተውሳከ ግስ (Peeqo)፣ መስተዋድድና አያያዥ (Gugame) እና 5 የሐረግ ዓይነቶች (Tura)፦ የጊዜ፣ የቦታ፣ የመጠንና የሁኔታ ተውሳከ ግሶች፤ መስተዋድድ (Sigetho)፣ አያያዥ (Gasetho) እና 5 የሐረግ ክፍሎች።
- **English:** 4. Adverbs (Peeqo: Time, Place, Degree, Manner), Function Words (Gugame: Postpositions & Conjunctions), and 5 Phrase Types (Tura: NP, AdjP, VP, AdvP, PP).

#### E. Proverbs, Idioms, Riddles & Oral Literature (`Leemiso / Tosse / Aaqeta / Sedhemenkke / Qan77ishe`)
| Category | Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Aaqeta / Tosse (Idiom/Proverb in Meeze 1, p. 27) | **Hayqoy asa na7as aawu mithi.** | ሀይቆይ አሰ ነዓስ አአዉ ሚꬂ። | ሞት ለሰው ልጅ የማይቀር የአባት ዕዳ (የተፈጥሮ ግዴታ) ነው። | Death is an inevitable natural duty ('father's tree') for human beings. |
| Tosse (Proverbial Comparison in Meeze 1, p. 27) | **Na7a caalappe parsso caali lo77o.** | ነዓ ጨአለፕፔ ፐርስሶ ጨአሊ ሎዕዖ። | ከልጅ ጫጫታ የቢራ (ቦርዴ) ሽታ ይሻላል። | The aroma of traditional beer is better than noisy commotion. |

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 1 (Tuma/Worddo, p. 11): 4. Aakime keethan oothiya aakimeti hargganchcha xuura naaganaas bessees. -> Zaaro: Tuma.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 1 (ቱመ/ዎርድዶ፣ ፕ። 11)፦ 4። አአኪሜ ኬእꬃን ኦኦꬂየ አአኪሜቲ ሀርግገንችቻ ጡኡረ ነአገነአስ ቤስሴእስ። -> ዘአሮ፦ ቱመ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 1 (እውነት/ሐሰት፣ ገጽ 11)፦ 4. በሕክምና ተቋም የሚሠሩ ሐኪሞች የታካሚውን ምሥጢር መጠበቅ አለባቸው። -> መልስ፦ እውነት (Tuma)።
- **English:** Exercise 1 (True/False, p. 11): 4. Doctors working in a medical facility must protect patients' confidentiality. -> Answer: True (Tuma).
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 1 (Ooratha Qaala Kuntho, p. 14): 1. Pirdda keetha daannati mootanchchatas [suure pirdda] immanaas bessees.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 1 (ኦኦረꬃ ቀአለ ኩንꬆ፣ ፕ። 14)፦ 1። ፒርድደ ኬእꬃ ደአንነቲ ሞኦተንችቻተስ [ሱኡሬ ፒርድደ] ኢምመነአስ ቤስሴእስ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 1 (ባዶ ቦታ መሙላት፣ ገጽ 14)፦ 1. የፍርድ ቤት ዳኞች ለተሟጋቾች [ትክክለኛ ፍርድ] መስጠት ይገባቸዋል።
- **English:** Exercise 1 (Vocabulary Fill-in-the-Blank, p. 14): 1. Court judges must render [fair judgment (suure pirdda)] to litigants.
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 1 (Qawosa Sunthi, p. 17): 2. 'Wude' giya qaalay awussa suntha kifile giddon moodettii? -> Zaaro: Shiiqo sunthi.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 1 (ቀዎሰ ሱንꬂ፣ ፕ። 17)፦ 2። 'ዉዴ' ጊየ ቀአለይ አዉስሰ ሱንꬃ ኪፊሌ ጊድዶን ሞኦዴትቲኢ? -> ዘአሮ፦ ሺኢቆ ሱንꬂ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 1 (ሰዋስው፣ ገጽ 17)፦ 2. 'Wude' (መንጋ) የሚለው ቃል በየትኛው የስም ክፍል ይመደባል? -> መልስ፦ የጥቅል ስም (Shiiqo sunthi)።
- **English:** Exercise 1 (Grammar Nouns, p. 17): 2. Under which noun subclass is the word 'Wude' (herd/flock) classified? -> Answer: Collective noun (Shiiqo sunthi).

### Gr8_Ch2 — Shemppo 2: Woga Buqurata | ሼምፕፖ 2፦ ዎገ ቡቁረተ | ምዕራፍ 2፦ ባህላዊና ታሪካዊ ቅርሶች | Chapter 2: Cultural and Historical Heritage Artifacts
- **Pages:** Book pp. 23–39 (PDF pp. 29–45) • **Source:** [Grade 8 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/160NHNy-4orPcAIk-L5t6tOEtZoWemz_-/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Woga Buqurata (Sofomere Gonggolo, Laalibela Bataskkaana, Jogole Goda)* (ዎገ ቡቁረተ (ሶፎሜሬ ጎንግጎሎ፣ ለአሊቤለ በተስክከአነ፣ ጆጎሌ ጎደ)) — ባህላዊ ቅርሶች (የሶፍ ዑመር ዋሻ፣ የላሊበላ ውቅር አብያተ ክርስቲያናት፣ የሐረር ጀጎል ግንብ) / Cultural Heritage Sites (Sof Omar Cave, Lalibela Churches, Harar Jugol Wall) (pp. 36–39 (Book pp. 30–33))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Nu mayzappe woykko yeletaappe yeletaakko aadhdhishe yeda dumma dumma buqurati woga buqurata geetettees. | ኑ መይዘፕፔ ዎይክኮ ዬሌተአፕፔ ዬሌተአክኮ አአꬍꬊሼ ዬደ ዱምመ ዱምመ ቡቁረቲ ዎገ ቡቁረተ ጌእቴትቴእስ። | ከቅድመ አያቶቻችን ወይም ከትውልድ ወደ ትውልድ ሲተላለፉ የመጡ የተለያዩ ሀብቶች ባህላዊ ቅርሶች ይባላሉ። | Various treasures passed down from our ancestors from generation to generation are called cultural heritage artifacts (woga buqurata). |
| Laalibela bataskkaanay issi shuchchappe woykko zaallappe woocettidi kawo Laalibela woden 12tho xeetu laythan keexettida dumma bataskkaana. | ለአሊቤለ በተስክከአነይ ኢስሲ ሹችቻፕፔ ዎይክኮ ዘአልለፕፔ ዎኦጬትቲዲ ከዎ ለአሊቤለ ዎዴን 12ꬆ ጤእቱ ለይꬃን ኬእጤትቲደ ዱምመ በተስክከአነ። | የላሊበላ ቤተ ክርስቲያን በንጉሥ ላሊበላ ዘመን በ12ኛው መቶ ክፍለ ዘመን ከአንድ ወጥ ቋጥኝ ተፈልፍሎ የተሠራ ልዩ ቤተ ክርስቲያን ነው። | The Lalibela churches were uniquely carved out of monolithic rock in the 12th century during the reign of King Lalibela. |
| Jogole goday Harare katamaa yuushon 16tho xeetu laythan direttida goda gidishin ichchashu گita wulatinne 82 isilaama woosa keethati (masggideti) de7oosona. | ጆጎሌ ጎደይ ሀረሬ ከተመአ ዩኡሾን 16ꬆ ጤእቱ ለይꬃን ዲሬትቲደ ጎደ ጊዲሺን ኢችቻሹ گኢተ ዉለቲንኔ 82 ኢሲለአመ ዎኦሰ ኬእꬃቲ (መስግጊዴቲ) ዴዖኦሶነ። | የጀጎል ግንብ በሐረር ከተማ ዙሪያ በ16ኛው ክፍለ ዘመን የተገነባ ግንብ ሲሆን አምስት ትላልቅ በሮችና 82 መስጊዶች አሉት። | The Jugol Wall surrounding the city of Harar was built in the 16th century and contains five historic gates and 82 mosques. |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Qan77ishe Kaassa Woga: Qan77ishe wothiya uray 'Qan77ishee' giikko zaariya uray 'Qashe' gidi zaarees. Zaaro immonna attikko 'Mokke' gidi gade immees.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ቀንዕዒሼ ከአስሰ ዎገ፦ ቀንዕዒሼ ዎꬂየ ኡረይ 'ቀንዕዒሼእ' ጊኢክኮ ዘአሪየ ኡረይ 'ቀሼ' ጊዲ ዘአሬእስ። ዘአሮ ኢምሞንነ አትቲክኮ 'ሞክኬ' ጊዲ ገዴ ኢምሜእስ።
- **Amharic:** የቃንጪሼ ጨዋታ ደንብ፦ ጠያቂው 'ቃንጪሼ' ሲል መላሹ 'ቃሼ' ብሎ ይመልሳል። መልሱን ካላወቀ 'ሞኬ' ብሎ አገር ከሰጠ በኋላ መልሱ ይነገረዋል።
- **English:** Qan77ishe Dialogue Protocol: When the challenger says 'Qan77ishee?', the respondent replies 'Qashe'. If unable to counter the couplet, the respondent says 'Mokke' and grants a town before hearing the verse.

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **woga buqura** | ዎገ ቡቁረ | `NP` | beni mayza shalo | ባህላዊ ቅርስ | cultural heritage / historical artifact |
| **gonggolo** | ጎንግጎሎ | `N` | shuchcha oocho | ዋሻ | cave / cavern |
| **bataskkaana** | በተስክከአነ | `N` | woosa keetha | ቤተ ክርስቲያን | church / temple |
| **zaalla** | ዘአልለ | `N` | gita shuchcha | ወጥ ቋጥኝ ድንጋይ | monolithic bedrock / cliff rock |
| **geeshshidi** | ጌእሽሺዲ | `ADV` | loythidi / qita kessidi | 1) በሚገባ/አጥርቶ (loythidi)፤ 2) አጽድቶ (qita kessidi) | 1) clearly/fluently; 2) cleanly/removing dirt (contextual polysemy) |
| **geella** | ጌእልለ | `ADJ` | makkalla / wobbe | 1) ጠማማ/ቅን ያልሆነ (makkalla)፤ 2) ጎባጣ እንጨት (wobbe) | 1) crooked/dishonest; 2) bent/curved wood (contextual polysemy) |
| **pengge** | ፔንግጌ | `N` | wula / qaada | 1) በር (wula)፤ 2) የሥራ ዕድል (ooso qaada) | 1) door/gate; 2) opportunity/opening (contextual polysemy) |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** 1. Palanthiya Adarsso (Argumentative Essay, Xaafo Hiilla, pp. 42–44): Adarssoy oyddan shaakettees: Palanthiya adarsso, Qoncciso adarsso, Qaari beso adarsso, Hayso (Paatetha) adarsso.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 1። ፐለንꬂየ አደርስሶ (አርጉሜንተቲቬ እስሰይ፣ ጠአፎ ሂኢልለ፣ ፕፕ። 42–44)፦ አደርስሶይ ኦይድደን ሻአኬትቴእስ፦ ፐለንꬂየ አደርስሶ፣ ቆንጭጪሶ አደርስሶ፣ ቀአሪ ቤሶ አደርስሶ፣ ሀይሶ (ፐአቴꬃ) አደርስሶ።
- **Amharic:** 1. አመዛዛኝ/አከራካሪ ድርሰት (Palanthiya Adarsso)፦ ድርሰት በአራት ይከፈላል፦ አከራካሪ፣ ሥዕላዊ (ገላጭ)፣ አብራሪ እና ተራኪ ድርሰት።
- **English:** 1. Argumentative Essay & 4 Essay Types: Argumentative (Palanthiya), Descriptive (Qoncciso), Expository (Qaari beso), and Narrative (Hayso/Paatetha).
- **Gofatho Latin:** 2. Ha77i Wode Qonccisiya Meega Qofa (Present/Habitual Tense Declarative Sentences, p. 45): Mallas: Na7ay ubba wode tamaare soo wonttara bees. Pooliseti katama giddon de7iya metota teqqoosona.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 2። ሀዕዒ ዎዴ ቆንጭጪሲየ ሜእገ ቆፈ (ፕሬሴንት/ሀቢቱአል ቴንሴ ዴጭለረቲቬ ሴንቴንጬስ፣ ፕ። 45)፦ መልለስ፦ ነዓይ ኡብበ ዎዴ ተመአሬ ሶኦ ዎንትተረ ቤእስ። ፖኦሊሴቲ ከተመ ጊድዶን ዴዒየ ሜቶተ ቴቅቆኦሶነ።
- **Amharic:** 2. የአሁን ጊዜ ዓረፍተ ነገር (Ha77i Wode)፦ ምሳሌ፦ ልጁ ሁልጊዜ ጠዋት ወደ ትምህርት ቤት ይሄዳል። ፖሊሶች በከተማ ውስጥ ያሉ ችግሮችን ይከላከላሉ።
- **English:** 2. Present/Habitual Tense Sentences (Ha77i Wode): Expresses ongoing or habitual actions using -ees (3MS), -awus (3FS), -oosona (3PL).

#### E. Proverbs, Idioms, Riddles & Oral Literature (`Leemiso / Tosse / Aaqeta / Sedhemenkke / Qan77ishe`)
| Category | Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |

| Qan77ishe (Poetic Duel, pp. 31–32) | **Qan77ishe 1: Barzaapeppe luuloy baawashin tuussi eqqenna. -> Zaaro: Ne aawappe durey baawashin dokke ashshenna.** | ቀንዕዒሼ 1፦ በርዘአፔፕፔ ሉኡሎይ በአወሺን ቱኡስሲ እቅቄንነ። -> ዘአሮ፦ ኔ አአወፕፔ ዱሬይ በአወሺን ዶክኬ አሽሼንነ። | ቃንጪሼ 1፦ ከባህርዛፍ የቀና የለም ግን ምሰሶ አይቆምም። -> መልስ፦ ከአባትህ የከበረ (ሀብታም) የለም ግን ጥጃ አያሳድግም። | Qan77ishe Poetic Duel 1: Nothing is straighter than eucalyptus, yet it cannot stand as a center pole -> No one is richer than your father, yet he never spares a calf. |
| Qan77ishe (Poetic Duel, p. 32) | **Qan77ishe 2: Qan77ishey qasheko lage -> Zaaro: Qacini harppa lage.** | ቀንዕዒሼ 2፦ ቀንዕዒሼይ ቀሼኮ ለጌ -> ዘአሮ፦ ቀጪኒ ሀርፕፐ ለጌ። | ቃንጪሼ 2፦ ቃንጪሼ የቃሼ ወዳጅ ነው -> መልስ፦ መፋቂያ ድንጋይ የቆዳ ወዳጅ ነው። | Qan77ishe Poetic Duel 2: Qan77ishe is the companion of Qashe -> The scraping blade is the companion of the hide. |
| Sedhemenkke (Riddle, p. 34) | **Sedhemenkke 1: Xeeray duge; dulley pude haniday aybeekko era wodha. -> Zaaro: Miiza goyna.** | ሴꬌሜንክኬ 1፦ ጤእረይ ዱጌ፤ ዱልሌይ ፑዴ ሀኒደይ አይቤእክኮ እረ ዎꬋ። -> ዘአሮ፦ ሚኢዘ ጎይነ። | እንቆቅልሽ 1፦ ጫፉ ወደ ታች፣ መነሻው ወደ ላይ የሆነው ምንድን ነው? -> መልስ፦ የላም ጭራ። | Sedhemenkke Riddle 1: Its tip points downward and its base points upward — what is it? -> Answer: A cow's tail. |
| Sedhemenkke (Riddle, p. 34) | **Sedhemenkke 2: Timirtte tamaaribennashin digire tookkis; kushen saatey baawashin saate eriyaba era wodha. -> Zaaro: Awure kutto.** | ሴꬌሜንክኬ 2፦ ቲሚርትቴ ተመአሪቤንነሺን ዲጊሬ ቶኦክኪስ፤ ኩሼን ሰአቴይ በአወሺን ሰአቴ እሪየበ እረ ዎꬋ። -> ዘአሮ፦ አዉሬ ኩትቶ። | እንቆቅልሽ 2፦ ትምህርት ሳይማር ዲግሪ የተሸከመ፣ በእጁ ሰዓት ሳይኖረው ሰዓት የሚያውቅ ምንድን ነው? -> መልስ፦ አውራ ዶሮ። | Sedhemenkke Riddle 2: Never went to school yet wears a crown/degree; has no watch on its hand yet knows the time -> Answer: A rooster. |
| Sedhemenkke (Riddles, pp. 34–35) | **Sedhemenkke 3–10 (pp. 34–35): (1) Gita beshe giddon guutha onggochcho -> Inxxarssa / Agina; (2) Dulley tama kayees, xeeray awa kayees -> Keetha tuussa; (3) Gede bishin de7ees, haa yishin de7onaba -> Shaafa haatha; (4) Woxxishin woxxees, eqqishin eqqees -> Kuya; (8) Omarssi pude, wontta duge -> Pengge sanqa; (10) Eqqiyade qanthi, uttiyade aduqqiyaba -> Kana.** | ሴꬌሜንክኬ 3–10 (ፕፕ። 34–35)፦ (1) ጊተ ቤሼ ጊድዶን ጉኡꬃ ኦንግጎችቾ -> ኢንጥጠርስሰ / አጊነ፤ (2) ዱልሌይ ተመ ከዬእስ፣ ጤእረይ አወ ከዬእስ -> ኬእꬃ ቱኡስሰ፤ (3) ጌዴ ቢሺን ዴዔእስ፣ ሀአ ዪሺን ዴዖነበ -> ሻአፈ ሀአꬃ፤ (4) ዎጥጢሺን ዎጥጤእስ፣ እቅቂሺን እቅቄእስ -> ኩየ፤ (8) ኦመርስሲ ፑዴ፣ ዎንትተ ዱጌ -> ፔንግጌ ሰንቀ፤ (10) እቅቂየዴ ቀንꬂ፣ ኡትቲየዴ አዱቅቂየበ -> ከነ። | እንቆቅልሽ 3–10 (ገጽ 34–35)፦ (1) በትልቅ ምጣድ ውስጥ ትንሽ እንጎቻ -> ምላስ/ጨረቃ፤ (2) ስሩ እሳት ይሞቃል ጫፉ ፀሐይ ይሞቃል -> የቤት ምሰሶ፤ (3) ሲሄድ አለ ሲመጣ የለም -> የወንዝ ውኃ፤ (4) ስትሮጥ የሚሮጥ ስትቆም የሚቆም -> ጥላ፤ (8) ማታ ወደ ላይ ጠዋት ወደ ታች -> የበር መቀርቀሪያ፤ (10) ሲቆም አጭር ሲቀመጥ ረጅም -> ውሻ። | Sedhemenkke Riddles 3–10 (pp. 34–35): (1) Small flatbread in a big pan -> Tongue/Moon; (2) Base warms by fire, top warms in sun -> Center house pole; (3) Goes forward, never returns -> River water; (4) Runs when you run, stands when you stand -> Shadow; (8) Up at night, down in morning -> Door bar; (10) Short standing, tall sitting -> Dog. |

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 1 (Tuma/Worddo, p. 40): 1. Sofomere gonggoloy Oroomo killile giddon beettiya daro malalisiya gonggolo. -> Zaaro: Tuma.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 1 (ቱመ/ዎርድዶ፣ ፕ። 40)፦ 1። ሶፎሜሬ ጎንግጎሎይ ኦሮኦሞ ኪልሊሌ ጊድዶን ቤእትቲየ ደሮ መለሊሲየ ጎንግጎሎ። -> ዘአሮ፦ ቱመ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 1 (እውነት/ሐሰት፣ ገጽ 40)፦ 1. የሶፍ ዑመር ዋሻ በኦሮሚያ ክልል ውስጥ የሚገኝ እጅግ አስደናቂ ዋሻ ነው። -> መልስ፦ እውነት (Tuma)።
- **English:** Exercise 1 (True/False, p. 40): 1. Sof Omar Cave is a remarkable cave located in the Oromia Region. -> Answer: True (Tuma).
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 3 (Dooro Oysha, p. 41): 5. Laalibela bataskkaanay awussa kawo woden oosettidee? -> Zaaro: Kawo Laalibela woden (12tho xeetu laythan).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 3 (ዶኦሮ ኦይሻ፣ ፕ። 41)፦ 5። ለአሊቤለ በተስክከአነይ አዉስሰ ከዎ ዎዴን ኦኦሴትቲዴእ? -> ዘአሮ፦ ከዎ ለአሊቤለ ዎዴን (12ꬆ ጤእቱ ለይꬃን)።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 3 (ምርጫ፣ ገጽ 41)፦ 5. የላሊበላ ቤተ ክርስቲያን በየትኛው ንጉሥ ዘመን ተሠራ? -> መልስ፦ በንጉሥ ላሊበላ ዘመን (በ12ኛው ክፍለ ዘመን)።
- **English:** Exercise 3 (Multiple Choice, p. 41): 5. During which king's reign were the Lalibela churches built? -> Answer: During the reign of King Lalibela (12th century).

### Gr8_Ch3 — Shemppo 3: Asatetha Kanddo | ሼምፕፖ 3፦ አሰቴꬃ ከንድዶ | ምዕራፍ 3፦ ሰብአዊ ባህሪና ሥነ-ምግባር | Chapter 3: Human Character, Personal Conduct, and Civic Ethics
- **Pages:** Book pp. 40–49 (PDF pp. 46–55) • **Source:** [Grade 8 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/160NHNy-4orPcAIk-L5t6tOEtZoWemz_-/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Asatetha Kanddo* (አሰቴꬃ ከንድዶ) — ሰብአዊ ባህሪና መልካም ሥነ-ምግባር / Human Character and Good Personal Conduct (pp. 50–51 (Book pp. 44–45))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Asatetha kanddoy asa na7i biittan de7ishe issoy issuwara lo77o gahetethi medhdhidi dumma dumma oosota polanaas maaddiya lo77o hanota guussi. | አሰቴꬃ ከንድዶይ አሰ ነዒ ቢኢትተን ዴዒሼ ኢስሶይ ኢስሱወረ ሎዕዖ ገሄቴꬂ ሜꬍꬊዲ ዱምመ ዱምመ ኦኦሶተ ፖለነአስ መአድዲየ ሎዕዖ ሀኖተ ጉኡስሲ። | ሰብአዊ ሥነ-ምግባር ማለት የሰው ልጅ በምድር ላይ ሲኖር እርስ በርሱ መልካም ግንኙነት ፈጥሮ የተለያዩ ሥራዎችን ለማከናወን የሚረዳ መልካም ሁኔታ ማለት ነው። | Human character (asatetha kanddo) refers to the positive conduct that enables people living on earth to build good relationships and accomplish various tasks together. |
| Moorettida asatetha kanddoti: uyi mathotethi, pantta soo asa bonchchonna ago, machcho bilethi, kawo giira wodera ciggonna ago. | ሞኦሬትቲደ አሰቴꬃ ከንድዶቲ፦ ኡዪ መꬆቴꬂ፣ ፐንትተ ሶኦ አሰ ቦንችቾንነ አጎ፣ መችቾ ቢሌꬂ፣ ከዎ ጊኢረ ዎዴረ ጪግጎንነ አጎ። | የተበላሹ ባህሪያት፦ ጠጥቶ መስከር፣ ቤተሰብን አለማክበር፣ ቃል ኪዳን ማፍረስና የመንግሥት ግብርን በወቅቱ አለመክፈል። | Corrupted behaviors include drunkenness, disrespecting one's family, breaking marital/social trust, and failing to pay government taxes on time. |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Aasetho Oda vs. Sitta Oda (pp. 47–49): Aasetho oday nam77u woykko daro asati wolira qofa laamerettishe haasayiya haasaya (dialogue); Sitta oday qassi issi asi haratas shiishiya moode haasaya (monologue/address).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** አአሴꬆ ኦደ ቭስ። ሲትተ ኦደ (ፕፕ። 47–49)፦ አአሴꬆ ኦደይ ነምዕዑ ዎይክኮ ደሮ አሰቲ ዎሊረ ቆፈ ለአሜሬትቲሼ ሀአሰዪየ ሀአሰየ (ዲአሎጉእ)፤ ሲትተ ኦደይ ቀስሲ ኢስሲ አሲ ሀረተስ ሺኢሺየ ሞኦዴ ሀአሰየ (ሞኖሎጉእ/አድድሬስስ)።
- **Amharic:** የምልልስ ንግግር (Aasetho oda) እና ቀጥተኛ ንግግር (Sitta oda)፦ ምልልስ በሁለት ወይም ከዚያ በላይ ሰዎች መካከል የሚደረግ የሐሳብ ልውውጥ ሲሆን ቀጥተኛ ንግግር ደግሞ አንድ ተናጋሪ ለታዳሚዎች የሚያቀርበው ንግግር ነው።
- **English:** Interactive Dialogue (Aasetho Oda) vs. Direct Address (Sitta Oda): Aasetho oda is an interactive conversation between two or more speakers, whereas sitta oda is a prepared speech delivered by a single speaker.

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **keetha-ooso** | ኬእꬃ-ኦኦሶ | `COMP.N` | soo ooso | የቤት ሥራ | housework / homework (Keethe + ooso) |
| **haatha-bule** | ሀአꬃ-ቡሌ | `COMP.N` | haathaufuwa | የውኃ አረፋ / ማዕበል | water bubble / foam (Haathe + bule) |
| **dono-oto** | ዶኖ-ኦቶ | `COMP.N` | dono shoorbba | የድንች ወጥ / ማብሰያ ምንቸት | potato stew / potato pot (Dono + oto) |
| **zo7o-asho** | ዞዖ-አሾ | `COMP.N` | asho | ቀይ ሥጋ | red/lean meat (Zo7o + asho) |
| **goshsha-boori** | ጎሽሻ-ቦኦሪ | `COMP.N` | goyiya boora | የእርሻ በሬ | plow ox (Goshsha + boori) |
| **maata-keethi** | መአተ-ኬእꬂ | `COMP.N` | woga keetha | የሣር ቤት (ጎጆ) | thatched grass house (Maata + keethi) |
| **tukke-haythi** | ቱክኬ-ሀይꬂ | `COMP.N` | tukke | የቡና ቅጠል | coffee leaf used for traditional infusion (Tukke + haythi) |
| **woosa-keethi** | ዎኦሰ-ኬእꬂ | `COMP.N` | bataskkaana | ቤተ ክርስቲያን / የጸሎት ቤት | house of prayer / church (Woosa + keethi) |
| **awa-ayfe** | አወ-አይፌ | `COMP.N` | awa | የፀሐይ ዓይን (ፀሐይ) | the sun / sun's disk (Awa + ayfe) |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** 1. Waatha Qaala (Compound Words, p. 53): Nam77u pana dandda7ida qaalati wolira gahettidi hara ooratha qaala medhdhiya wode waatha qaala geetettees (Mallas: shambba-haluwa, aaho-ooso, tukke-xaaro, para-haythe).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 1። ወአꬃ ቀአለ (ጮምፖኡንድ ዎርድስ፣ ፕ። 53)፦ ነምዕዑ ፐነ ደንድደዒደ ቀአለቲ ዎሊረ ገሄትቲዲ ሀረ ኦኦረꬃ ቀአለ ሜꬍꬊየ ዎዴ ወአꬃ ቀአለ ጌእቴትቴእስ (መልለስ፦ ሻምብበ-ሀሉወ፣ አአሆ-ኦኦሶ፣ ቱክኬ-ጠአሮ፣ ፐረ-ሀይꬄ)።
- **Amharic:** 1. ጥምር ቃላት (Waatha Qaala)፦ ሁለት ራሳቸውን የቻሉ ነጠላ ቃላት ተጣምረው አዲስ ትርጉም ያለው ቃል ሲፈጥሩ ጥምር ቃል ይባላል።
- **English:** 1. Compound Words (Waatha Qaala): Formed when two independent stems join (often hyphenated) to create a new lexical unit with specialized meaning.
- **Gofatho Latin:** 2. Pexo Qaalinne Masara Qaala (Singular and Plural Nouns, pp. 54–55): Pexo qaali issibaa xalla qonccisees (Mallas: kafo, keethe, mithi, boora); Masara qaali nam77uppe bolla gididabata '-ta/-ti' some ekkidi qonccisees (Mallas: kafota, keetheta, mithata, boorata).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 2። ፔጦ ቀአሊንኔ መሰረ ቀአለ (ሲንጉለር አንድ ፕሉረል ኖኡንስ፣ ፕፕ። 54–55)፦ ፔጦ ቀአሊ ኢስሲበአ ጠልለ ቆንጭጪሴእስ (መልለስ፦ ከፎ፣ ኬእꬄ፣ ሚꬂ፣ ቦኦረ)፤ መሰረ ቀአሊ ነምዕዑፕፔ ቦልለ ጊዲደበተ '-ተ/-ቲ' ሶሜ እክኪዲ ቆንጭጪሴእስ (መልለስ፦ ከፎተ፣ ኬእꬄተ፣ ሚꬃተ፣ ቦኦረተ)።
- **Amharic:** 2. ነጠላ ቃል (Pexo Qaala) እና ብዙ ቁጥር ቃል (Masara Qaala)፦ ነጠላ ቃል አንድን ነገር ብቻ ሲያመለክት፣ ብዙ ቁጥር ደግሞ '-ta/-ti' ቅጥያ ወስዶ ከሁለት በላይ የሆኑትን ያመለክታል (ለምሳሌ፦ kafo -> kafota)።
- **English:** 2. Singular (Pexo) vs. Plural (Masara) Nouns: Singular nouns denote a single entity, while plural nouns attach the plural suffix -ta (ACC) / -ti (NOM) (e.g., kafo 'bird' -> kafota 'birds').

#### E. Proverbs, Idioms, Riddles & Oral Literature (`Leemiso / Tosse / Aaqeta / Sedhemenkke / Qan77ishe`)
| Category | Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Aaqeta / Waatha Qaala Idiom (p. 53) | **Uray para-haythe gididi sinthe xeellees.** | ኡረይ ፐረ-ሀይꬄ ጊዲዲ ሲንꬄ ጤእልሌእስ። | ሰውየው እንደ ፈረስ ጆሮ ቀጥ ብሎ ወደ ፊት ይመለከታል (በንቃት ያዳምጣል/ይመለከታል)። | The man looks straight ahead with alert attention like a horse's ear (idiomatic compound 'para-haythe'). |

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 1 (Nabbabo Wode Kuntho, p. 50): 1. Lo77o asatetha [kanddoy] issi dere asas de7ikko biitta dichchaas daro maaddees.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 1 (ነብበቦ ዎዴ ኩንꬆ፣ ፕ። 50)፦ 1። ሎዕዖ አሰቴꬃ [ከንድዶይ] ኢስሲ ዴሬ አሰስ ዴዒክኮ ቢኢትተ ዲችቻአስ ደሮ መአድዴእስ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 1 (ባዶ ቦታ መሙላት፣ ገጽ 50)፦ 1. መልካም ሰብአዊ [ሥነ-ምግባር] ለአንድ አገር ሕዝብ ካለው ለአገር ዕድገት እጅግ ይጠቅማል።
- **English:** Exercise 1 (Reading Cloze, p. 50): 1. If a community possesses good personal [character (kanddoy)], it greatly aids national development.
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 1 (Pexo -> Masara, p. 55): 1. Kafo -> Kafota; 2. Mithi -> Mithata; 3. Boora -> Boorata; 4. Tamaare -> Tamaareta.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 1 (ፔጦ -> መሰረ፣ ፕ። 55)፦ 1። ከፎ -> ከፎተ፤ 2። ሚꬂ -> ሚꬃተ፤ 3። ቦኦረ -> ቦኦረተ፤ 4። ተመአሬ -> ተመአሬተ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 1 (ነጠላ ወደ ብዙ ቁጥር፣ ገጽ 55)፦ 1. Kafo -> Kafota (ወፎች)፤ 2. Mithi -> Mithata (ዛፎች)፤ 3. Boora -> Boorata (በሬዎች)፤ 4. Tamaare -> Tamaareta (ተማሪዎች)።
- **English:** Exercise 1 (Singular to Plural Transformation, p. 55): 1. Kafo -> Kafota; 2. Mithi -> Mithata; 3. Boora -> Boorata; 4. Tamaare -> Tamaareta.

### Gr8_Ch4 — Shemppo 4: Qantha Medh-Darsso | ሼምፕፖ 4፦ ቀንꬃ ሜꬍ-ደርስሶ | ምዕራፍ 4፦ አጭር ልቦለድ | Chapter 4: Short Fiction and Narrative Structure
- **Pages:** Book pp. 50–66 (PDF pp. 56–72) • **Source:** [Grade 8 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/160NHNy-4orPcAIk-L5t6tOEtZoWemz_-/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Amon Attida Gita Amo (Qantha Medh-Darsso)* (አሞን አትቲደ ጊተ አሞ (ቀንꬃ ሜꬍ-ደርስሶ)) — በምኞት የቀረ ትልቅ ምኞት (አጭር ልቦለድ) / A Great Dream Left Unfulfilled (Short Story of Teacher Kule Kubaari and His Mother Maalle Atarqqa) (pp. 58–61 (Book pp. 52–55))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Kuley Manddoye Sawulan 1975 M.L heeran Dargge kawotethay Tophphiya aysiya wode yelettis. | ኩሌይ መንድዶዬ ሰዉለን 1975 ም።ል ሄእረን ደርግጌ ከዎቴꬃይ ቶጵጪየ አይሲየ ዎዴ ዬሌትቲስ። | ኩሌ በማንዶዬ ሳውላ በ1975 ዓ.ም. አካባቢ በደርግ መንግሥት ዘመን ተወለደ። | Kule was born in Manddoye Sawla around 1975 E.C. during the Derg regime in Ethiopia. |
| Maalle Atarqqa pa na7aa dicha tamaarsada asa udanaw dumma dumma asa keethan gaach gidada afila meeccashe, beshe wothashe, mithi maxa bayzashe undenna metoota aathasu. | መአልሌ አተርቅቀ ፐ ነዓአ ዲቻ ተመአርሰደ አሰ ኡደነው ዱምመ ዱምመ አሰ ኬእꬃን ገአች ጊደደ አፊለ ሜእጭጨሼ፣ ቤሼ ዎꬃሼ፣ ሚꬂ መጠ በይዘሼ ኡንዴንነ ሜቶኦተ አአꬃሱ። | ወይዘሮ አታርቃ ልጇን አሳድጋና አስተምራ ሰው ለማድረግ በሰው ቤት ልብስ እያጠበች፣ ምጣድ እየጣደችና እንጨት ለቅማ እየሸጠች ብዙ መከራ አሳለፈች። | His widowed mother Maalle Atarqqa endured immense hardship washing clothes, baking flatbread in others' homes, and gathering firewood to sell so that her son Kule could study and succeed. |
| Qoppennan astamaare Kule Kubaari koyirida zhima conchaynne usha uyethay loddan loddan xapho yeddidi pa aayes qoppida gita amuwa meto bolla wodhdhis. | ቆፕፔንነን አስተመአሬ ኩሌ ኩበአሪ ኮዪሪደ ዢመ ጮንቻይንኔ ኡሻ ኡዬꬃይ ሎድደን ሎድደን ጠፆ ዬድዲዲ ፐ አአዬስ ቆፕፒደ ጊተ አሙወ ሜቶ ቦልለ ዎꬍꬊስ። | ሳይታሰብ መምህር ኩሌ ኩባሪ የጀመረው የጫት መቃምና የመጠጥ ልማድ ቀስ በቀስ ሥር ሰዶ ለእናቱ ያሰበውን ትልቅ ምኞት አጨናገፈበት። | Tragically, after graduating and becoming a teacher in Arba Minch, Kule fell into khat-chewing and alcohol addiction, squandering his salary while his devoted mother fell ill before he could fulfill his promise to her. |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Ooso Kaaloda Maara (Procedural Discourse, pp. 57–58): Koyro, hayssa kaallidi, nam77antho, heedzantho, wurssethan — Mallas: Tukke katho maara, Maathi yeessidi pila doyssi kessana maara, Badala zeridi shaalen gelssana maara.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ኦኦሶ ከአሎደ መአረ (ፕሮጬዱረል ዲስጮኡርሴ፣ ፕፕ። 57–58)፦ ኮይሮ፣ ሀይስሰ ከአልሊዲ፣ ነምዕዓንꬆ፣ ሄእꬓንꬆ፣ ዉርስሴꬃን — መልለስ፦ ቱክኬ ከꬆ መአረ፣ መአꬂ ዬእስሲዲ ፒለ ዶይስሲ ኬስሰነ መአረ፣ በደለ ዜሪዲ ሻአሌን ጌልስሰነ መአረ።
- **Amharic:** የሥራ ቅደም ተከተል አገላለጽ (ገጽ 57–58)፦ መጀመሪያ (koyro)፣ ከዚህ ቀጥሎ (hayssa kaallidi)፣ በመጨረሻም (wurssethan) በማለት የቡና አፈላል፣ የወተት መናጥና የበቆሎ እርሻ ቅደም ተከተልን ማስረዳት።
- **English:** Procedural Discourse Markers (pp. 57–58): Using sequence connectors (koyro 'first', hayssa kaallidi 'next', wurssethan 'finally') to explain traditional coffee preparation, churning milk into butter, and maize cultivation.

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **yi7o** | ዪዖ | `N` | yela aawaynne aayiya baynna na7a | ወላጅ አልባ ልጅ | orphan child |
| **unddenna** | ኡንድዴንነ | `ADJ/QUANT` | daro | ብዙ / በርካታ | many / numerous / countless |
| **kayo** | ከዮ | `N` | ufayssa dhayo | ሐዘን / ትካዜ | grief / sorrow / sadness |
| **yeemottis** | ዬእሞትቲስ | `V` | waayettis | ተሰቃየ / ተቸገረ | suffered / struggled greatly |
| **katidi** | ከቲዲ | `CNV` | zemppidi | ተመሥርቶ / ተደግፎ | based on / relying upon |
| **loddan loddan** | ሎድደን ሎድደን | `ADV` | leppi gidi | ቀስ በቀስ | gradually / little by little |
| **haphphidi** | ሀጵጪዲ | `CNV` | shiishidi | አቅፎ / ሰብስቦ | embracing / gathering together |
| **loohis** | ሎኦሂስ | `V` | luxxis / eris | ሠለጠነ / ተማረ | trained / graduated / learned |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** 1. Qantha Medh-Darsso Yarata (Elements of Short Fiction, pp. 61–62): (a) Taarike (Story), (b) Gaasonne Muruuta (Plot / Cause-and-Effect), (c) Odaawa (Character), (d) Beswode (Setting: Besse + Wode), (e) Dom77e (Conflict), (f) Ayfe Qofa (Theme).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 1። ቀንꬃ ሜꬍ-ደርስሶ የረተ (እሌሜንትስ ኦፍ ሾርት ፊጭቲኦን፣ ፕፕ። 61–62)፦ (አ) ተአሪኬ (ስቶርይ)፣ (ብ) ገአሶንኔ ሙሩኡተ (ፕሎት / ጨኡሴ-አንድ-እፍፌጭት)፣ (ጭ) ኦደአወ (ቻረጭቴር)፣ (ድ) ቤስዎዴ (ሴትቲንግ፦ ቤስሴ + ዎዴ)፣ (እ) ዶምዕዔ (ጮንፍሊጭት)፣ (ፍ) አይፌ ቆፈ (ꬄሜ)።
- **Amharic:** 1. የአጭር ልቦለድ አላባዊያን (ገጽ 61–62)፦ (ሀ) ታሪክ (Taarike)፣ (ለ) ሴራ/ምክንያትና ውጤት (Gaasonne Muruuta)፣ (ሐ) ገጸ-ባህሪ (Odaawa)፣ (መ) መቼት (Beswode)፣ (ሠ) ግጭት (Dom77e)፣ (ረ) ጭብጥ (Ayfe Qofa)።
- **English:** 1. Elements of Short Fiction (pp. 61–62): Story (Taarike), Plot/Causality (Gaasonne Muruuta), Character (Odaawa), Setting (Beswode), Conflict (Dom77e), and Theme (Ayfe Qofa).
- **Gofatho Latin:** 2. Meega Qofa Shaahota: Suntha Tura (NP Subject) & Peesho Tura (VP Predicate) (pp. 70–72): Malla: 'Maallee Amanu7eela Anileyya shuchcha keethe keexasu.' -> Suntha tura: Maallee Amanu7eela Anileyya; Peesho tura: shuchcha keethe keexasu.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 2። ሜእገ ቆፈ ሻአሆተ፦ ሱንꬃ ቱረ (ንፕ ሱብጄጭት) & ፔእሾ ቱረ (ቭፕ ፕሬዲጨቴ) (ፕፕ። 70–72)፦ መልለ፦ 'መአልሌእ አመኑዔእለ አኒሌይየ ሹችቻ ኬእꬄ ኬእጠሱ።' -> ሱንꬃ ቱረ፦ መአልሌእ አመኑዔእለ አኒሌይየ፤ ፔእሾ ቱረ፦ ሹችቻ ኬእꬄ ኬእጠሱ።
- **Amharic:** 2. የዓረፍተ ነገር ክፍሎች፦ ስማዊ ሐረግ (Suntha tura) እና ግሳዊ ሐረግ (Peesho tura) (ገጽ 70–72)፦ ምሳሌ፦ 'ወ/ሮ አማኑኤላ አኒለያ የድንጋይ ቤት ሠራች።' -> ስማዊ ሐረግ፦ Maallee Amanu7eela Anileyya፤ ግሳዊ ሐረግ፦ shuchcha keethe keexasu።
- **English:** 2. Sentence Constituents: Noun Phrase Subject (Suntha Tura) & Verb Phrase Predicate (Peesho Tura) (pp. 70–72).

#### E. Proverbs, Idioms, Riddles & Oral Literature (`Leemiso / Tosse / Aaqeta / Sedhemenkke / Qan77ishe`)
| Category | Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Aaqeta (Idiom, p. 63) | **Haathan eqqida mithi udis.** | ሀአꬃን እቅቂደ ሚꬂ ኡዲስ። | በውኃ ውስጥ እንደቆመ ዛፍ ኑሮው ተናጋ (ተቸገረ)። | His/her life became like a tree standing in floodwater (precarious and troubled). |
| Aaqeta (Idiom, p. 63) | **Ushacha kushen oykkidi tamaares.** | ኡሻቻ ኩሼን ኦይክኪዲ ተመአሬስ። | በቀኝ እጁ ይዞ (በከፍተኛ ትኩረትና ትጋት) ይማራል። | He studies holding it in his right hand (with utmost diligence and priority). |
| Aaqeta (Idiom, p. 63) | **Ache saxxada dichchasu.** | አቼ ሰጥጠደ ዲችቻሱ። | ጥርሷን ነክሳ (በጽናት ተቋቁማ) አሳደገችው። | She gritted her teeth (persevered through hardship) and raised him. |
| Aaqeta (Idiom, p. 64) | **Zine issi doonan cuttidi qachidi oday sa7i wonttishin mana bangga gidis.** | ዚኔ ኢስሲ ዶኦነን ጩትቲዲ ቀቺዲ ኦደይ ሰዒ ዎንትቲሺን መነ በንግገ ጊዲስ። | ትናንት በአንድ አፍ ተስማምተው የቋጩት ነገር ሲነጋ እንደ ማና ገብስ ተበታተነ። | What they agreed upon unanimously yesterday scattered in disorder like spilled barley by morning. |

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 2 (Tuma/Worddo, p. 63): 5. Maalle Atarqqa kushe shica maccasi gidiya gisho pa na7a waayettada dichasu. -> Zaaro: Worddo ('kushe shica' guussi dure guussi; Maalle Atarqqa manqqo maccasi).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 2 (ቱመ/ዎርድዶ፣ ፕ። 63)፦ 5። መአልሌ አተርቅቀ ኩሼ ሺጨ መጭጨሲ ጊዲየ ጊሾ ፐ ነዓ ወአዬትተደ ዲቻሱ። -> ዘአሮ፦ ዎርድዶ ('ኩሼ ሺጨ' ጉኡስሲ ዱሬ ጉኡስሲ፤ መአልሌ አተርቅቀ መንቅቆ መጭጨሲ)።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 2 (እውነት/ሐሰት፣ ገጽ 63)፦ 5. ወ/ሮ አታርቃ ሀብታም (kushe shica) ስለነበረች ልጇን በመከራ አሳደገች። -> መልስ፦ ሐሰት (Worddo)።
- **English:** Exercise 2 (True/False, p. 63): 5. Because Maalle Atarqqa was a wealthy woman (kushe shica), she raised her son in hardship. -> Answer: False (Worddo — she was poor, not wealthy).
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 3 (Aaqe Birshshethi, p. 63): 1. 'Maalle Atarqqo duussi haathan eqqida mithi udis.' Haathan eqqida mithi guussi: -> Zaaro: Duussay metoninne waayen kumidayssa.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 3 (አአቄ ቢርሽሼꬂ፣ ፕ። 63)፦ 1። 'መአልሌ አተርቅቆ ዱኡስሲ ሀአꬃን እቅቂደ ሚꬂ ኡዲስ።' ሀአꬃን እቅቂደ ሚꬂ ጉኡስሲ፦ -> ዘአሮ፦ ዱኡስሰይ ሜቶኒንኔ ወአዬን ኩሚደይስሰ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 3 (የፈሊጣዊ ንግግር ፍቺ፣ ገጽ 63)፦ 1. 'በውኃ ላይ የቆመ ዛፍ' ማለት ምን ማለት ነው? -> መልስ፦ በችግርና በሥጋት የተሞላ ኑሮ።
- **English:** Exercise 3 (Idiom Meaning, p. 63): 1. What does 'a tree standing in water' mean in 'Maalle Atarqqo duussi haathan eqqida mithi udis'? -> Answer: A life full of instability and hardship.

### Gr8_Ch5 — Shemppo 5: Dere Asa | ሼምፕፖ 5፦ ዴሬ አሰ | ምዕራፍ 5፦ የሕዝብ ቁጥርና ስርጭት | Chapter 5: Population Growth, Census Distribution, and Demography
- **Pages:** Book pp. 67–81 (PDF pp. 73–87) • **Source:** [Grade 8 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/160NHNy-4orPcAIk-L5t6tOEtZoWemz_-/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Dere Asaa (Tophphiya Dere Asa Qoodanne Dharccetethaa)* (ዴሬ አሰአ (ቶጵጪየ ዴሬ አሰ ቆኦደንኔ ꬋርጭጬቴꬃአ)) — የኢትዮጵያ የሕዝብ ቁጥር፣ ዕድገትና ክልላዊ ስርጭት / Population of Ethiopia: Growth, Regional Distribution, and Mortality Rates (pp. 75–79 (Book pp. 69–73))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Dere asa qoodaynne hayqo qooday dichchan luxida dereninne dicconna gadetan geeddari xeelliya wode keehidi dummatethi de7ees. | ዴሬ አሰ ቆኦደይንኔ ሀይቆ ቆኦደይ ዲችቻን ሉጢደ ዴሬኒንኔ ዲጭጮንነ ገዴተን ጌእድደሪ ጤእልሊየ ዎዴ ኬእሂዲ ዱምመቴꬂ ዴዔእስ። | የሕዝብ ብዛትና የሞት መጠን በበለጸጉ አገራትና በታዳጊ አገራት መካከል ሲነጻጸር ከፍተኛ ልዩነት አለው። | When population birth rates and mortality rates are compared between developed and developing countries, there is a major difference. |
| Tophphiya dere asa de7o heera dharccetethay kumethan gina gidenna. Gezze ayyerenne doollottiya heeran dere asaa dharccetethay dari ekkishin, gadha (bazzo) ayyere haaron guuxxidi beettees. | ቶጵጪየ ዴሬ አሰ ዴዖ ሄእረ ꬋርጭጬቴꬃይ ኩሜꬃን ጊነ ጊዴንነ። ጌዝዜ አይዬሬንኔ ዶኦልሎትቲየ ሄእረን ዴሬ አሰአ ꬋርጭጬቴꬃይ ደሪ እክኪሺን፣ ገꬋ (በዝዞ) አይዬሬ ሀአሮን ጉኡጥጢዲ ቤእትቴእስ። | የኢትዮጵያ የሕዝብ አሰፋፈር ስርጭት በሁሉም ቦታ እኩል አይደለም። በደጋና ወይና ደጋ አካባቢዎች የሕዝብ ጥግግት ከፍተኛ ሲሆን በቆላና በረሃማ አካባቢዎች ግን አነስተኛ ነው። | Population distribution across Ethiopia is uneven: population density is high in highland (gezze) and midland (doollo) zones, while low in lowland/arid (gadha/bazzo) zones. |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Haasayo Tobba (p. 75): 1. Tophphiya dere asa qoodanne hayqo geeddaridi shiishite. 2. Tophphiyan matan matan (shiiqidi) de7iya killille dere asata tobbidi shiishite.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሀአሰዮ ቶብበ (ፕ። 75)፦ 1። ቶጵጪየ ዴሬ አሰ ቆኦደንኔ ሀይቆ ጌእድደሪዲ ሺኢሺቴ። 2። ቶጵጪየን መተን መተን (ሺኢቂዲ) ዴዒየ ኪልሊልሌ ዴሬ አሰተ ቶብቢዲ ሺኢሺቴ።
- **Amharic:** የውይይት ጥያቄዎች (ገጽ 75)፦ 1. የኢትዮጵያን የሕዝብ ብዛትና የሞት መጠን አወዳድራችሁ አቅርቡ። 2. በኢትዮጵያ ተጠጋግተው የሚኖሩ የክልል ሕዝቦችን ስርጭት ተወያይታችሁ አቅርቡ።
- **English:** Oral Discussion & Presentation (p. 75): 1. Compare and present Ethiopia's population growth and mortality rates. 2. Discuss densely populated versus sparsely populated regions in Ethiopia.

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **gaammo** | ገአምሞ | `N` | asho miya wora do7a | አንበሳ (ሥጋ የሚበላ የዱር አውሬ) | lion (asho miya wora do7a) |
| **dorssa** | ዶርስሰ | `N` | maata miya soo mehe | በግ (ሣር የሚበላ የቤት እንስሳ) | sheep (maata miya soo mehe) |
| **mathi** | መꬂ | `N` | eessi immiya nhoo77o | ንብ (ማር የሚሰጥ ነፍሳት) | honeybee (eessi immiya nhoo77o) |
| **wola** | ዎለ | `N` | gita mithi | ዋርካ (ትልቅ ዛፍ) | sycamore fig tree (gita mithi) |
| **maahe** | መአሄ | `N` | ziirinqqo wora do7a | ነብር (ዥንጉርጉር የዱር አውሬ) | leopard (ziirinqqo wora do7a) |
| **yeleta** | ዬሌተ | `N` | phalqqe: hayqo | ልደት / ትውልድ (ተቃራኒ፦ hayqo) | birth / generation (antonym: hayqo 'death') |
| **gaxare** | ገጠሬ | `N` | phalqqe: katama | ገጠር (ተቃራኒ፦ katama) | rural area / countryside (antonym: katama 'city') |
| **gezze** | ጌዝዜ | `N` | phalqqe: gadha | ደጋ (ተቃራኒ፦ gadha) | highland zone (antonym: gadha 'lowland') |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** 1. Zodo Birshshethi (Literal / Denotative Dictionary Meaning, p. 84): Qaalas sitta woykko qabima birshshethi imo (Mallas: Puutto = afila daddanaw maaddiyaba; Shuchchi = minnoba, keetha oosos maaddiyaba).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 1። ዞዶ ቢርሽሼꬂ (ሊቴረል / ዴኖተቲቬ ዲጭቲኦነርይ ሜአኒንግ፣ ፕ። 84)፦ ቀአለስ ሲትተ ዎይክኮ ቀቢመ ቢርሽሼꬂ ኢሞ (መልለስ፦ ፑኡትቶ = አፊለ ደድደነው መአድዲየበ፤ ሹችቺ = ሚንኖበ፣ ኬእꬃ ኦኦሶስ መአድዲየበ)።
- **Amharic:** 1. ቀጥተኛ (የመዝገበ-ቃላት) ፍቺ (Zodo Birshshethi፣ ገጽ 84)፦ ለቃላት ቀጥተኛ ወይም የመዝገበ-ቃላት ትርጉም መስጠት (ምሳሌ፦ ጥጥ = ልብስ ለመሸመን የሚረዳ ነገር)።
- **English:** 1. Literal / Denotative Meaning (Zodo Birshshethi, p. 84): Giving the direct dictionary definition of a lexical item.
- **Gofatho Latin:** 2. Active to Passive Voice Transformation (Os-meqo -> Ost-meqo with -ett-, pp. 85–87): Mallas: 1. Shaashshota asho masu -> Ashoy metettis. 2. Lafaama soollo uukkasu -> Soolloy uukettis. 3. Woraabi boora shukkis -> Booray shukettis. 4. Madalchoy Bodollen badala harmis -> Badalay harmettis.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 2። አጭቲቬ ቶ ፐስሲቬ ቮኢጬ ትረንስፎርመቲኦን (ኦስ-ሜቆ -> ኦስት-ሜቆ ዊꬅ -እትት-፣ ፕፕ። 85–87)፦ መልለስ፦ 1። ሻአሽሾተ አሾ መሱ -> አሾይ ሜቴትቲስ። 2። ለፈአመ ሶኦልሎ ኡኡክከሱ -> ሶኦልሎይ ኡኡኬትቲስ። 3። ዎረአቢ ቦኦረ ሹክኪስ -> ቦኦረይ ሹኬትቲስ። 4። መደልቾይ ቦዶልሌን በደለ ሀርሚስ -> በደለይ ሀርሜትቲስ።
- **Amharic:** 2. ከድርጊት ወደ ተደራጊ ግስ መቀየር (ገጽ 85–87)፦ ምሳሌ፦ 1. ሻሾታ ሥጋ በላች -> ሥጋው ተበላ (Ashoy metettis)። 2. ወራቢ በሬ አረደ -> በሬው ታረደ (Booray shukettis)።
- **English:** 2. Active to Passive Voice Transformation (pp. 85–87): Promoting the direct object to nominative subject (-y) and attaching the passive suffix -ett- to the verb stem (e.g., shukkis 'slaughtered' -> shukettis 'was slaughtered').

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 3 (Peesho Laame, p. 81): Convert past-tense verbs (Polis, Tokkis, Bis, Zeris, Tamaaris) into present and future forms -> Pol-ees / Pol-ana, Tokk-ees / Tokk-ana, B-ees / B-aana.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 3 (ፔእሾ ለአሜ፣ ፕ። 81)፦ ጮንቬርት ፐስት-ቴንሴ ቬርብስ (ፖሊስ፣ ቶክኪስ፣ ቢስ፣ ዜሪስ፣ ተመአሪስ) ኢንቶ ፕሬሴንት አንድ ፉቱሬ ፎርምስ -> ፖል-እእስ / ፖል-አነ፣ ቶክክ-እእስ / ቶክክ-አነ፣ ብ-እእስ / ብ-አአነ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 3 (የግስ ጊዜ መቀየር፣ ገጽ 81)፦ ኃላፊ ግሶችን (Polis, Tokkis, Bis, Zeris) ወደ አሁንና ወደፊት ጊዜ መቀየር።
- **English:** Exercise 3 (Verb Tense Inflection, p. 81): Inflect past verbs (Polis, Tokkis, Bis, Zeris, Tamaaris) across present (-ees) and future (-ana) paradigms.
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 7 (Phalqqe Qaala Dabbotho, p. 82): Match antonyms: 1. Yeleta -> F. Hayqo; 3. Pashkkatethi -> D. Sahanchcha; 4. Gaxare -> B. Katama; 6. Maccata -> A. Addeta; 8. Gezze -> E. Gadha; 10. Gujjis -> I. Paccis.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 7 (ጳልቅቄ ቀአለ ደብቦꬆ፣ ፕ። 82)፦ መትች አንቶኝምስ፦ 1። ዬሌተ -> ፍ። ሀይቆ፤ 3። ፐሽክከቴꬂ -> ድ። ሰሀንችቻ፤ 4። ገጠሬ -> ብ። ከተመ፤ 6። መጭጨተ -> አ። አድዴተ፤ 8። ጌዝዜ -> እ። ገꬋ፤ 10። ጉጅጂስ -> ኢ። ፐጭጪስ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 7 (ተቃራኒ ቃላትን ማዛመድ፣ ገጽ 82)፦ 1. ልደት -> ሞት፤ 3. ጤንነት -> ታማሚ፤ 4. ገጠር -> ከተማ፤ 6. ሴቶች -> ወንዶች፤ 8. ደጋ -> ቆላ፤ 10. ጨመረ -> ጎደለ።
- **English:** Exercise 7 (Antonym Matching, p. 82): 1. Yeleta (birth) -> Hayqo (death); 4. Gaxare (rural) -> Katama (urban); 8. Gezze (highland) -> Gadha (lowland); 10. Gujjis (increased) -> Paccis (decreased).

### Gr8_Ch6 — Shemppo 6: Bazzotethi | ሼምፕፖ 6፦ በዝዞቴꬂ | ምዕራፍ 6፦ በረሃማነትና የአካባቢ መራቆት | Chapter 6: Desertification, Deforestation, and Environmental Protection
- **Pages:** Book pp. 82–94 (PDF pp. 88–100) • **Source:** [Grade 8 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/160NHNy-4orPcAIk-L5t6tOEtZoWemz_-/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Bazzotethi* (በዝዞቴꬂ) — በረሃማነት (የደን መጨፍጨፍና የአካባቢ መራቆት) / Desertification: Causes, Deforestation, Drought, and Ecological Impact (pp. 91–93 (Book pp. 85–87))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Bazzotethi guussi issi heeran de7iya woray woykko dumma dumma dozatinne medosati dhayiyade, haathay meliyade, biittay meecettiyadenne heeray kallo atishin bazzotethi medhdhettees. | በዝዞቴꬂ ጉኡስሲ ኢስሲ ሄእረን ዴዒየ ዎረይ ዎይክኮ ዱምመ ዱምመ ዶዘቲንኔ ሜዶሰቲ ꬋዪየዴ፣ ሀአꬃይ ሜሊየዴ፣ ቢኢትተይ ሜእጬትቲየዴንኔ ሄእረይ ከልሎ አቲሺን በዝዞቴꬂ ሜꬍꬌትቴእስ። | በረሃማነት ማለት በአንድ አካባቢ የሚገኝ ደን፣ ልዩ ልዩ ዕፅዋትና ፍጥረታት ሲጠፉ፣ ውኃ ሲደርቅና አፈር ተሸርሽሮ መሬቱ ራቁቱን ሲቀር የሚፈጠር ክስተት ነው። | Desertification occurs when an area's forests, diverse plants, and wildlife disappear, water sources dry up, and soil erodes, leaving the land barren. |
| Biitta bolla de7iya bazzotappe 72 kushey (72%) bazzoy de7ey dichchaa bolla de7iya gadetana. | ቢኢትተ ቦልለ ዴዒየ በዝዞተፕፔ 72 ኩሼይ (72%) በዝዞይ ዴዔይ ዲችቻአ ቦልለ ዴዒየ ገዴተነ። | በዓለም ላይ ከሚገኙ በረሃማ አካባቢዎች 72 በመቶው የሚገኘው በማደግ ላይ ባሉ አገራት ውስጥ ነው። | Seventy-two percent (72%) of the world's desertified lands are located in developing countries due to deforestation for firewood, timber, and farmland expansion. |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Moode Haasaya vs. Moode Gidonna Haasaya (p. 90): Hintte cugan cugan gididi 'Bazzotethi' giya kaara bolla qantha nootte oykkidi moode haasaya giigisidi kifile tamaaretas shiishite.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሞኦዴ ሀአሰየ ቭስ። ሞኦዴ ጊዶንነ ሀአሰየ (ፕ። 90)፦ ሂንትቴ ጩገን ጩገን ጊዲዲ 'በዝዞቴꬂ' ጊየ ከአረ ቦልለ ቀንꬃ ኖኦትቴ ኦይክኪዲ ሞኦዴ ሀአሰየ ጊኢጊሲዲ ኪፊሌ ተመአሬተስ ሺኢሺቴ።
- **Amharic:** መደበኛና ኢ-መደበኛ ንግግር (ገጽ 90)፦ በቡድን ሆናችሁ 'በረሃማነት' በሚል ርዕስ አጭር ማስታወሻ በመያዝ መደበኛ ንግግር አዘጋጅታችሁ ለክፍል ተማሪዎች አቅርቡ።
- **English:** Formal vs. Informal Speech (p. 90): In groups, prepare and deliver a formal speech using outline notes on the topic of 'Desertification (Bazzotethi)'.

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **bazzotethi** | በዝዞቴꬂ | `N` | woray dhayidi biittay melo | በረሃማነት / የመሬት መራቆት | desertification / land degradation |
| **xaraga tama** | ጠረገ ተመ | `NP` | wora xuuggiya tama | የሰደድ እሳት | wildfire / forest fire |
| **haathe (geema birshshethi)** | ሀአꬄ (ጌእመ ቢርሽሼꬂ) | `N (FIG)` | daro lo77o, injje | በጣም ጥሩ / ምቹ ባህሪ (በዘይቤያዊ ፍቺ) | very gentle, agreeable character (figurative meaning of 'water') |
| **eessi tigees (geema)** | እእስሲ ቲጌእስ (ጌእመ) | `VP (FIG)` | mal7ethidi odettees, cimmees | በጣፋጭ አንደበት ያታልላል / ያቆላምጣል | flatters sweetly / deceives with honeyed words (lit. drips honey from the mouth) |
| **haatha shooshi (geema)** | ሀአꬃ ሾኦሺ (ጌእመ) | `NP (FIG)` | mala lo77o | እጅግ ውብ / መልከ መልካም | strikingly beautiful / graceful (lit. water snake) |
| **dhiille baaris (geema)** | ꬊኢልሌ በአሪስ (ጌእመ) | `VP (FIG)` | si77i gis, odettenna | ጭጭ አለ / ምንም አልተናገረም | kept completely silent (lit. held flour in the mouth) |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meega Qofa Qommota (4 Functional Sentence Types, pp. 96–100): (1) Qoncciso meega qofa (Declarative: 'Tophphey dichcha bolla de7iya gadetappe issiniwu.'), (2) Oysha meega qofa (Interrogative: 'Ne aawa sunthay oonee?'), (3) Kiita meega qofa (Imperative: 'Keetha loytha naaga.'), (4) Malalo meega qofa (Exclamatory: 'Hashshu Hashshu!').
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእገ ቆፈ ቆምሞተ (4 ፉንጭቲኦነል ሴንቴንጬ ትይፔስ፣ ፕፕ። 96–100)፦ (1) ቆንጭጪሶ ሜእገ ቆፈ (ዴጭለረቲቬ፦ 'ቶጵጴይ ዲችቻ ቦልለ ዴዒየ ገዴተፕፔ ኢስሲኒዉ።')፣ (2) ኦይሻ ሜእገ ቆፈ (ኢንቴርሮገቲቬ፦ 'ኔ አአወ ሱንꬃይ ኦኦኔእ?')፣ (3) ኪኢተ ሜእገ ቆፈ (ኢምፔረቲቬ፦ 'ኬእꬃ ሎይꬃ ነአገ።')፣ (4) መለሎ ሜእገ ቆፈ (እጥጭለመቶርይ፦ 'ሀሽሹ ሀሽሹ!')።
- **Amharic:** አራቱ የዓረፍተ ነገር ዓይነቶች (ገጽ 96–100)፦ (1) ገላጭ/ነጋሪ ዓ.ነገር (Qoncciso)፣ (2) ጥያቄያዊ ዓ.ነገር (Oysha)፣ (3) ትዕዛዛዊ ዓ.ነገር (Kiita)፣ (4) አጋናኝ/አድናቆታዊ ዓ.ነገር (Malalo)።
- **English:** Four Functional Sentence Types (pp. 96–100): Declarative (Qoncciso), Interrogative (Oysha), Imperative (Kiita), and Exclamatory (Malalo).

#### E. Proverbs, Idioms, Riddles & Oral Literature (`Leemiso / Tosse / Aaqeta / Sedhemenkke / Qan77ishe`)
| Category | Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Geema Birshshethi / Simile (p. 95) | **Daashshi dhiille baarida asa mela si77i gis.** | ደአሽሺ ꬊኢልሌ በአሪደ አሰ ሜለ ሲዕዒ ጊስ። | ዳሺ ዱቄት እንደጎረሰ ሰው ጭጭ አለ። | Daashshi stayed completely silent like a person with a mouthful of flour. |
| Geema Birshshethi / Metaphor (p. 96) | **Ashshami wozanay shuchchi gidis.** | አሽሻሚ ዎዘነይ ሹችቺ ጊዲስ። | የአሻሚ ልብ ድንጋይ ሆነ (ጨከነ)። | Ashshami's heart turned to stone (became unyielding/hard-hearted). |

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 2 (Meega Qofa Qommo Dooro, p. 99): 1. 'Ne awude Tungga baanee?' -> Oysha meega qofa; 2. 'Ta ishay new habba habba!' -> Malalo meega qofa; 3. 'Tophphiya dere asa qooday xeetu mukulappe aadhdhiis.' -> Qoncciso meega qofa; 4. 'Na7aw! Loytha goya.' -> Kiita meega qofa.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 2 (ሜእገ ቆፈ ቆምሞ ዶኦሮ፣ ፕ። 99)፦ 1። 'ኔ አዉዴ ቱንግገ በአኔእ?' -> ኦይሻ ሜእገ ቆፈ፤ 2። 'ተ ኢሻይ ኔው ሀብበ ሀብበ!' -> መለሎ ሜእገ ቆፈ፤ 3። 'ቶጵጪየ ዴሬ አሰ ቆኦደይ ጤእቱ ሙኩለፕፔ አአꬍꬊኢስ።' -> ቆንጭጪሶ ሜእገ ቆፈ፤ 4። 'ነዓው! ሎይꬃ ጎየ።' -> ኪኢተ ሜእገ ቆፈ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 2 (የዓረፍተ ነገር ዓይነቶችን መለየት፣ ገጽ 99)፦ 1. ጥያቄያዊ (Oysha)፤ 2. አጋናኝ (Malalo)፤ 3. ገላጭ (Qoncciso)፤ 4. ትዕዛዛዊ (Kiita)።
- **English:** Exercise 2 (Identifying Sentence Types, p. 99): 1. Interrogative (Oysha); 2. Exclamatory (Malalo); 3. Declarative (Qoncciso); 4. Imperative (Kiita).

### Gr8_Ch7 — Shemppo 7: Yooga Nayta Wolqqa Ooson Qoho | ሼምፕፖ 7፦ ዮኦገ ነይተ ዎልቅቀ ኦኦሶን ቆሆ | ምዕራፍ 7፦ የሕፃናት ጉልበት ብዝበዛና ጉዳቱ | Chapter 7: The Harm of Child Labor Exploitation and Protecting Youth Education
- **Pages:** Book pp. 95–106 (PDF pp. 101–112) • **Source:** [Grade 8 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/160NHNy-4orPcAIk-L5t6tOEtZoWemz_-/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Yooga Nayta Wolqqa Toochoy Kaalethiya Qoha* (ዮኦገ ነይተ ዎልቅቀ ቶኦቾይ ከአሌꬂየ ቆሀ) — የሕፃናት ጉልበት ብዝበዛ የሚያስከትለው ጉዳት / The Harm Caused by Child Labor Exploitation and Trafficking (pp. 103–106 (Book pp. 97–100))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Tamanne hosppun laythappe garssa gidida yooga nayti dumma dumma sohotan guutha dokke koyya maqqi zaariya asati odiya malalisiyaa haasayan yooga nayta hirayssa wolqqa koyya keethaawas aathidi imoosona. | ተመንኔ ሆስፕፑን ለይꬃፕፔ ገርስሰ ጊዲደ ዮኦገ ነይቲ ዱምመ ዱምመ ሶሆተን ጉኡꬃ ዶክኬ ኮይየ መቅቂ ዘአሪየ አሰቲ ኦዲየ መለሊሲየአ ሀአሰየን ዮኦገ ነይተ ሂረይስሰ ዎልቅቀ ኮይየ ኬእꬃአወስ አአꬂዲ ኢሞኦሶነ። | ዕድሜያቸው ከአሥራ ስምንት ዓመት በታች የሆኑ ታዳጊ ሕፃናት በተለያዩ ቦታዎች ጥቂት ጥቅም በሚፈልጉ አታላይ ደلالoch (ሕገ-ወጥ አዘዋዋሪዎች) አማካይነት ርካሽ ጉልበት ለሚፈልጉ አሠሪዎች ተላልፈው ይሰጣሉ። | Children under eighteen years of age are deceived by traffickers seeking small payouts and handed over to employers demanding cheap labor. |
| Wursethan, yooga nayta wolqqa toochoy yoogati tamaarana maatay bonchettidi tamaarenna mela udis. Tamaarida asi issi gade dichchaas zokko meqethi. | ዉርሴꬃን፣ ዮኦገ ነይተ ዎልቅቀ ቶኦቾይ ዮኦገቲ ተመአረነ መአተይ ቦንቼትቲዲ ተመአሬንነ ሜለ ኡዲስ። ተመአሪደ አሲ ኢስሲ ገዴ ዲችቻአስ ዞክኮ ሜቄꬂ። | በመጨረሻም የሕፃናት ጉልበት ብዝበዛ ሕፃናት የመማር መብታቸው ተከብሮ እንዳይማሩ ያደርጋል። የተማረ ሰው ለአንድ አገር ዕድገት የጀርባ አጥንት ነው። | Ultimately, child labor exploitation deprives children of their right to education; an educated citizenry is the backbone (zokko meqethi) of a country's development. |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Cuga Tobba (pp. 102–103): Cuga daannanne xaafanchcha dooridi 'Yooga nayta wolqqa tooshe' giya kaara bolla tobbidi kifile tamaaretas shiishite.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ጩገ ቶብበ (ፕፕ። 102–103)፦ ጩገ ደአንነንኔ ጠአፈንችቻ ዶኦሪዲ 'ዮኦገ ነይተ ዎልቅቀ ቶኦሼ' ጊየ ከአረ ቦልለ ቶብቢዲ ኪፊሌ ተመአሬተስ ሺኢሺቴ።
- **Amharic:** የቡድን ውይይት (ገጽ 102–103)፦ የቡድን ሰብሳቢና ጸሐፊ መርጣችሁ 'የሕፃናት ጉልበት ብዝበዛ' በሚል ርዕስ ተወያይታችሁ ለክፍል አቅርቡ።
- **English:** Structured Group Discussion (pp. 102–103): Elect a group chairperson (cuga daanna) and secretary (xaafanchcha) and conduct a panel discussion on preventing child labor exploitation.

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **hashetidi** | ሀሼቲዲ | `CNV` | phalqqe: bizzattidi | ተባብረው (ተቃራኒ፦ bizzattidi / ለብቻ) | cooperating together (antonym: bizzattidi 'individually') |
| **doloha** | ዶሎሀ | `N` | phalqqe: wuloha | ምሥራቅ (ተቃራኒ፦ wuloha / ምዕራብ) | east / sunrise direction (antonym: wuloha 'west') |
| **hirayssi** | ሂረይስሲ | `ADJ` | phalqqe: al77o | ርካሽ (ተቃራኒ፦ al77o / ውድ) | cheap / inexpensive (antonym: al77o 'precious/expensive') |
| **toochidi** | ቶኦቺዲ | `CNV` | phalqqe: bonchidi | ንቆ / በድሎ (ተቃራኒ፦ bonchidi / አክብሮ) | disrespecting / exploiting (antonym: bonchidi 'respecting') |
| **labbanidi** | ለብበኒዲ | `CNV` | phalqqe: baalotidi | ደክሞ / ተጎሳቁሎ (ተቃራኒ፦ baalotidi / ተዝናንቶ) | exhausted / worn out (antonym: baalotidi 'rested/comfortable') |
| **aakkis** | አአክኪስ | `V` | phalqqe: xuummis | አሰፋ / ከፈተ (ተቃራኒ፦ xuummis / አጠበበ) | widened / opened (antonym: xuummis 'narrowed/closed') |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Misse Qaalinne Phalqqe Qaala (Synonyms & Antonyms, p. 111) & Review of 4 Sentence Types (Qoncciso, Kiita, Oysho, Malalo, pp. 111–112): Mallas: Makkalla = Geella (misse), Suure (phalqqe); Xaama = Lee7o (misse), Orddo (phalqqe); Zodo = Suure (misse), Geema (phalqqe).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሚስሴ ቀአሊንኔ ጳልቅቄ ቀአለ (ስይኖኝምስ & አንቶኝምስ፣ ፕ። 111) & ሬቪእው ኦፍ 4 ሴንቴንጬ ትይፔስ (ቆንጭጪሶ፣ ኪኢተ፣ ኦይሾ፣ መለሎ፣ ፕፕ። 111–112)፦ መልለስ፦ መክከልለ = ጌእልለ (ሚስሴ)፣ ሱኡሬ (ጳልቅቄ)፤ ጠአመ = ሌእዖ (ሚስሴ)፣ ኦርድዶ (ጳልቅቄ)፤ ዞዶ = ሱኡሬ (ሚስሴ)፣ ጌእመ (ጳልቅቄ)።
- **Amharic:** ተመሳሳይና ተቃራኒ ቃላት (ገጽ 111) እና የዓረፍተ ነገር ዓይነቶች ክለሳ (ገጽ 111–112)፦ ምሳሌ፦ Makkalla = Geella (ተመሳሳይ)፣ Suure (ተቃራኒ)፤ Xaama = Lee7o (ተመሳሳይ)፣ Orddo (ተቃራኒ)።
- **English:** Synonyms (Misse Qaala) & Antonyms (Phalqqe Qaala, p. 111) and Sentence Type Consolidation (pp. 111–112).

#### E. Proverbs, Idioms, Riddles & Oral Literature (`Leemiso / Tosse / Aaqeta / Sedhemenkke / Qan77ishe`)
| Category | Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Aaqeta / Metaphor (p. 105) | **Tamaarida asi issi gade dichchaas zokko meqethi.** | ተመአሪደ አሲ ኢስሲ ገዴ ዲችቻአስ ዞክኮ ሜቄꬂ። | የተማረ ሰው ለአንድ አገር ዕድገት የጀርባ አጥንት ነው። | An educated person is the backbone (zokko meqethi) of a nation's development. |

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 3 (Dooro Oysha, p. 108): 8. 'Tamaarida asi issi gade dichaas zokko meqethi.' Zokko meqethi guussi: -> Zaaro: Bertte woykko baaso.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 3 (ዶኦሮ ኦይሻ፣ ፕ። 108)፦ 8። 'ተመአሪደ አሲ ኢስሲ ገዴ ዲቻአስ ዞክኮ ሜቄꬂ።' ዞክኮ ሜቄꬂ ጉኡስሲ፦ -> ዘአሮ፦ ቤርትቴ ዎይክኮ በአሶ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 3 (ምርጫ፣ ገጽ 108)፦ 8. 'የጀርባ አጥንት (zokko meqethi)' ማለት ምን ማለት ነው? -> መልስ፦ ወሳኝ ወይም መሠረት (Bertte woykko baaso)።
- **English:** Exercise 3 (Multiple Choice, p. 108): 8. What does 'zokko meqethi' (backbone) mean in 'An educated person is the backbone of a country's development'? -> Answer: Essential foundation / pillar.
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 4 (Phalqqe Qaala Dabbotho, p. 109): 1. Hashetidi -> C. Bizzattidi; 3. Doloha -> J. Wuloha; 5. Hirayssi -> A. Al77o; 6. Toochidi -> F. Bonchidi; 10. Aakkis -> B. Xuummis.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 4 (ጳልቅቄ ቀአለ ደብቦꬆ፣ ፕ። 109)፦ 1። ሀሼቲዲ -> ጭ። ቢዝዘትቲዲ፤ 3። ዶሎሀ -> ጅ። ዉሎሀ፤ 5። ሂረይስሲ -> አ። አልዕዖ፤ 6። ቶኦቺዲ -> ፍ። ቦንቺዲ፤ 10። አአክኪስ -> ብ። ጡኡምሚስ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 4 (ተቃራኒ ቃላትን ማዛመድ፣ ገጽ 109)፦ 1. ተባብሮ -> ተነጣጥሎ፤ 3. ምሥራቅ -> ምዕራብ፤ 5. ርካሽ -> ውድ፤ 6. ንቆ -> አክብሮ፤ 10. አሰፋ -> አጠበበ።
- **English:** Exercise 4 (Antonym Matching, p. 109): 1. Hashetidi -> Bizzattidi; 3. Doloha -> Wuloha; 5. Hirayssi -> Al77o; 6. Toochidi -> Bonchidi; 10. Aakkis -> Xuummis.

### Gr8_Ch8 — Shemppo 8: Sarotethi | ሼምፕፖ 8፦ ሰሮቴꬂ | ምዕራፍ 8፦ ሰላምና የሰላም ግንባታ | Chapter 8: Peace, Conflict Prevention, and Social Harmony
- **Pages:** Book pp. 107–120 (PDF pp. 113–126) • **Source:** [Grade 8 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/160NHNy-4orPcAIk-L5t6tOEtZoWemz_-/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Sarotethi (Sarotetha Aybatethinne 10 Sarotetha Dethata)* (ሰሮቴꬂ (ሰሮቴꬃ አይበቴꬂንኔ 10 ሰሮቴꬃ ዴꬃተ)) — የሰላም ምንነትና አሥሩ የሰላም እርከኖች / The Nature of Peace: Philosophical Perspectives (Einstein, MLK, Dalai Lama, Spinoza, Galtung) and 10 Levels of Peace (pp. 115–119 (Book pp. 109–113))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Sisa wodeppe doomidi ha77i gaso asa na7i sarotethas amonne baaxetethan gam7idayssi tillattidaba. | ሲሰ ዎዴፕፔ ዶኦሚዲ ሀዕዒ ገሶ አሰ ነዒ ሰሮቴꬃስ አሞንኔ በአጤቴꬃን ገምዒደይስሲ ቲልለትቲደበ። | ከጥንት ጊዜ ጀምሮ እስከ አሁን ድረስ የሰው ልጅ ለሰላም በምኞትና በጥረት ሲተጋ መቆየቱ የታወቀ ነው። | It is well established that from ancient times to the present, humanity has continually yearned and strived for peace. |
| Albert Anystayn xeeluwan 'sarotethi ola dhaysso xalaala gidenna'; gaasoykka sarotethi daannata, seera, woga, dere asaa kawotethaa ubba kuuyidi oyqqiya gisho. | አልቤርት አኝስተይን ጤእሉወን 'ሰሮቴꬂ ኦለ ꬋይስሶ ጠለአለ ጊዴንነ'፤ ገአሶይክከ ሰሮቴꬂ ደአንነተ፣ ሴእረ፣ ዎገ፣ ዴሬ አሰአ ከዎቴꬃአ ኡብበ ኩኡዪዲ ኦይቅቂየ ጊሾ። | በአልበርት አይንስታይን አመለካከት 'ሰላም የጦርነት አለመኖር ብቻ አይደለም'፤ ምክንያቱም ሰላም ፍትሕን፣ ሕግን፣ ሥርዓትንና የሕዝብ አስተዳደርን ሁሉ አካትቶ ስለሚይዝ ነው። | In Albert Einstein's view, 'peace is not merely the absence of war,' because true peace encompasses justice, law, ethics, and governance. |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Haasayancha Yeda Haasaya -> Hayzanchas Geliya Qoncciso (Paraphrasing Idiomatic Speech, pp. 114–115): Mallas: 'Ushsha mathoppe maatha mathoy daro iita' -> 'Kaloynne de7oy daridi mathoyida asi ushshan mathotida asappe aadhees.'
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሀአሰየንቻ ዬደ ሀአሰየ -> ሀይዘንቻስ ጌሊየ ቆንጭጪሶ (ፐረጵረሲንግ ኢዲኦመቲጭ ስፔእች፣ ፕፕ። 114–115)፦ መልለስ፦ 'ኡሽሻ መꬆፕፔ መአꬃ መꬆይ ደሮ ኢኢተ' -> 'ከሎይንኔ ዴዖይ ደሪዲ መꬆዪደ አሲ ኡሽሻን መꬆቲደ አሰፕፔ አአꬌእስ።'
- **Amharic:** ፈሊጣዊ ንግግሮችን ለአድማጭ በሚገባ ግልጽ ቋንቋ መተርጎም (ገጽ 114–115)፦ ምሳሌ፦ 'ከመጠጥ ስካር የወተት (የጥጋብ) ስካር ይከፋል' -> 'በሀብትና በጥጋብ የታበየ ሰው በመጠጥ ከሰከረ ሰው የባሰ ይጎዳል።'
- **English:** Paraphrasing Figurative Speech for Listeners (pp. 114–115): E.g., 'Intoxication from milk (wealth/arrogance) is worse than intoxication from alcohol' -> 'Arrogance from excess wealth is more dangerous than drunkenness.'

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **sarotethi** | ሰሮቴꬂ | `N` | gadeti wol ola eso / sigetethi | ሰላም (አገራት እርስ በርስ ጦርነት ማቆምና መስማማት) | peace / harmony / absence of conflict |
| **tillattidaba** | ቲልለትቲደበ | `NP` | erettidaba | የታወቀ / ግልጽ የሆነ ነገር | well-known / evident fact |
| **siiqotethi** | ሲኢቆቴꬂ | `N` | dosetethi | ፍቅር / መዋደድ | mutual love / affection |
| **la77a** | ለዕዓ | `ADJ/N` | wallo | ነጻ / ነጻነት | free / liberty |
| **zarbiso** | ዘርቢሶ | `N` | irxxisees | ማብረድ / ማረጋጋት | calming / pacifying / cooling down |
| **daayis** | ደአዪስ | `V` | dalgis / aacis | ተስፋፋ / በዛ | spread widely / expanded |
| **bu7ettis** | ቡዔትቲስ | `V` | walakettis | ተቀላቀለ / ተዋሐደ | blended / united together |
| **sirphatethi** | ሲርጳቴꬂ | `N` | kooshe dhayso | ጸጥታ / እርጋታ | tranquility / quietness |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** 1. Cacha Malaata (11 Punctuation Marks, pp. 122–124): (1) Selle cacha (.), (2) Daabula cacha (:), (3) Oysha malaata (?), (4) Shaaho malaata (,), (5) Heedzu cacha (...), (6) Malaalo malaata (!), (7) Qilico malaata (¡), (8) Zuge malaata (/), (9) Bagga shaaho (;), (10) Gartha malaata (“ ”), (11) Gasetho cacha (-).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 1። ጨቻ መለአተ (11 ፑንጭቱአቲኦን መርክስ፣ ፕፕ። 122–124)፦ (1) ሴልሌ ጨቻ (።)፣ (2) ደአቡለ ጨቻ (፦)፣ (3) ኦይሻ መለአተ (?)፣ (4) ሻአሆ መለአተ (፣)፣ (5) ሄእꬑ ጨቻ (።።።)፣ (6) መለአሎ መለአተ (!)፣ (7) ቂሊጮ መለአተ (¡)፣ (8) ዙጌ መለአተ (/)፣ (9) በግገ ሻአሆ (፤)፣ (10) ገርꬃ መለአተ (“ ”)፣ (11) ገሴꬆ ጨቻ (-)።
- **Amharic:** 1. ሥርዓተ-ነጥቦች (Cacha Malaata፣ ገጽ 122–124)፦ አራት ነጥብ (Selle cacha .)፣ ሁለት ነጥብ (Daabula cacha :)፣ የጥያቄ ምልክት (Oysha malaata ?)፣ ነጠላ ሰረዝ (Shaaho malaata ,)፣ ሦስት ነጥብ (Heedzu cacha ...)፣ ትዕምርተ አንክሮ (Malaalo malaata !)፣ የምጸት ምልክት (Qilico malaata ¡)፣ እዝባር (Zuge malaata /)፣ ድርብ ሰረዝ (Bagga shaaho ;) እና ትዕምርተ ጥቅስ (Gartha malaata “ ”)።
- **English:** 1. Eleven Punctuation Marks in Gofatho (pp. 122–124): Period (Selle cacha), Colon (Daabula cacha), Question mark (Oysha malaata), Comma (Shaaho malaata), Ellipsis (Heedzu cacha), Exclamation mark (Malaalo malaata), Irony/Sarcasm mark (Qilico malaata), Slash (Zuge malaata), Semicolon (Bagga shaaho), Quotation marks (Gartha malaata), and Hyphen.
- **Gofatho Latin:** 2. Wodhdhidi Eqqiya vs. Pana Dandda7idi Eqqiya Meega Qofa (Dependent vs. Independent Clauses, pp. 125–126): Mallas: 'Ta aaway soo yiya wode (wodhdhidi eqqiya), taani soon uttada xana7ays (pana dandda7idi eqqiya).'
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 2። ዎꬍꬊዲ እቅቂየ ቭስ። ፐነ ደንድደዒዲ እቅቂየ ሜእገ ቆፈ (ዴፔንዴንት ቭስ። ኢንዴፔንዴንት ጭለኡሴስ፣ ፕፕ። 125–126)፦ መልለስ፦ 'ተ አአወይ ሶኦ ዪየ ዎዴ (ዎꬍꬊዲ እቅቂየ)፣ ተአኒ ሶኦን ኡትተደ ጠነዓይስ (ፐነ ደንድደዒዲ እቅቂየ)።'
- **Amharic:** 2. ጥገኛ ዓረፍተ ነገርና ነጻ (ራሱን ችሎ የሚቆም) ዓረፍተ ነገር (ገጽ 125–126)፦ ምሳሌ፦ 'አባቴ ወደ ቤት በሚመጣበት ጊዜ (ጥገኛ ሐረግ/ዓ.ነገር)፣ እኔ ቤት ተቀምጬ አጠናለሁ (ነጻ ዓ.ነገር)።'
- **English:** 2. Subordinate/Dependent Clauses vs. Independent Clauses (pp. 125–126): Subordinate clauses (marked by 'wode', '-shin', '-ashe') cannot stand alone and depend on the main finite clause.

#### E. Proverbs, Idioms, Riddles & Oral Literature (`Leemiso / Tosse / Aaqeta / Sedhemenkke / Qan77ishe`)
| Category | Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Tosse (Proverb, p. 115) | **Ushsha mathoppe maatha mathoy daro iita.** | ኡሽሻ መꬆፕፔ መአꬃ መꬆይ ደሮ ኢኢተ። | ከመጠጥ ስካር የወተት (የጥጋብ) ስካር እጅግ ይከፋል። | Intoxication from milk (wealth/arrogance) is far worse than intoxication from alcohol. |
| Tosse (Proverb, p. 115) | **Ayfe qooqey soo geli aqin wozana qooqey ogen aqis.** | አይፌ ቆኦቄይ ሶኦ ጌሊ አቂን ዎዘነ ቆኦቄይ ኦጌን አቂስ። | ዓይነ ስውር ቤት ገብቶ ሲያድር ልበ ስውር ግን መንገድ ላይ አደረ። | While the blind in eye found his way home for the night, the blind in heart spent the night stranded on the road. |
| Tosse (Proverb, p. 115) | **Wodoro miyaara bin bixaala miyaara yasu.** | ዎዶሮ ሚየአረ ቢን ቢጠአለ ሚየአረ የሱ። | ገመድ የምትበላዋ ስትሄድ ልጓም የምትበላዋ መጣች። | When the one that chews the rope left, the one that chews the iron bridle arrived (out of the frying pan into the fire). |

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 1 (Cacha Malaata Kuntho, p. 124): 1. Woo [!] na7ay waanii. 4. Nu soo aayiya giya bada [:] shunkuruuto, kuttonne oyssi shammasu. 6. Nena oshinchchi oyqidee [?]
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 1 (ጨቻ መለአተ ኩንꬆ፣ ፕ። 124)፦ 1። ዎኦ [!] ነዓይ ወአኒኢ። 4። ኑ ሶኦ አአዪየ ጊየ በደ [፦] ሹንኩሩኡቶ፣ ኩትቶንኔ ኦይስሲ ሻምመሱ። 6። ኔነ ኦሺንችቺ ኦይቂዴእ [?]
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 1 (ሥርዓተ-ነጥብ መሙላት፣ ገጽ 124)፦ 1. ትዕምርተ አንክሮ [!]፤ 4. ሁለት ነጥብ [:]፤ 6. የጥያቄ ምልክት [?]።
- **English:** Exercise 1 (Punctuation Insertion, p. 124): 1. Exclamation mark [!]; 4. Colon [:]; 6. Question mark [?].

### Gr8_Ch9 — Shemppo 9: Ayyere Haaro | ሼምፕፖ 9፦ አይዬሬ ሀአሮ | ምዕራፍ 9፦ የአየር ንብረትና የአየር ንብረት ለውጥ | Chapter 9: Climate Zones, Weather Patterns, and Climate Change
- **Pages:** Book pp. 121–132 (PDF pp. 127–138) • **Source:** [Grade 8 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/160NHNy-4orPcAIk-L5t6tOEtZoWemz_-/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Ayyere Haaro Laame (Medheta Gaasoranne Asi Medhdhiya Ayyere Haaro Laame)* (አይዬሬ ሀአሮ ለአሜ (ሜꬌተ ገአሶረንኔ አሲ ሜꬍꬊየ አይዬሬ ሀአሮ ለአሜ)) — የአየር ንብረት ለውጥ (ተፈጥሮአዊና ሰው ሠራሽ መንስኤዎች) / Climate Change: Natural Drivers, Industrial Greenhouse Gases, and Global Warming (pp. 132–134 (Book pp. 126–128))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Ayyere haaro laamey nam77u kifilen shaakettees: Medheta gaasoranne Asi medhdhiya metota geetettoosona. | አይዬሬ ሀአሮ ለአሜይ ነምዕዑ ኪፊሌን ሻአኬትቴእስ፦ ሜꬌተ ገአሶረንኔ አሲ ሜꬍꬊየ ሜቶተ ጌእቴትቶኦሶነ። | የአየር ንብረት ለውጥ በሁለት ይከፈላል፦ በተፈጥሮአዊ ምክንያቶችና ሰው በሚፈጥራቸው ችግሮች የሚከሰቱ ይባላሉ። | Climate change is divided into two categories: natural causes and human-induced causes. |
| Hayssa ayyere haaro laame gathiday cililo keetha gaazeta (greenhouse gases) gidanaw dandda7ees; daro worati qeexettidi gaazeta carkkon buqethi gathees. | ሀይስሰ አይዬሬ ሀአሮ ለአሜ ገꬂደይ ጪሊሎ ኬእꬃ ገአዜተ (ግሬእኞኡሴ ገሴስ) ጊደነው ደንድደዔእስ፤ ደሮ ዎረቲ ቄእጤትቲዲ ገአዜተ ጨርክኮን ቡቄꬂ ገꬄእስ። | ይህን የአየር ንብረት ለውጥ ያመጡት የሙቀት አማቂ ጋዞች (cililo keetha gaazeta) ሲሆኑ ደኖች ሲጨፈጨፉ በከባቢ አየር ውስጥ ጋዞች እንዲከማቹ ያደርጋል። | This climate change is driven by greenhouse gases (cililo keetha gaazeta); widespread deforestation and industrial emissions release heat-trapping gases into the atmosphere. |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Palama Woga (Formal Debate Rules, pp. 129–130): Palama kaalethiya daannanne afakaato (timekeeper/moderator) dooridi, imettida wodiya wogan go7ettidi, markka shiishidi palamanawuren pirdda amin77idi eko.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ፐለመ ዎገ (ፎርመል ዴበቴ ሩሌስ፣ ፕፕ። 129–130)፦ ፐለመ ከአሌꬂየ ደአንነንኔ አፈከአቶ (ቲሜኬእፔር/ሞዴረቶር) ዶኦሪዲ፣ ኢሜትቲደ ዎዲየ ዎገን ጎዔትቲዲ፣ መርክከ ሺኢሺዲ ፐለመነዉሬን ፒርድደ አሚንዕዒዲ እኮ።
- **Amharic:** የክርክር ሥነ-ሥርዓት (ገጽ 129–130)፦ የክርክር ዳኛና ሰዓት ተቆጣጣሪ በመምረጥ፣ የተሰጠውን ሰዓት በአግባቡ በመጠቀም፣ በማስረጃ በመከራከርና በመጨረሻም ውሳኔውን በጸጋ መቀበል።
- **English:** Formal Debate Protocol (pp. 129–130): Selecting debate judges, staying on topic and within time limits, supporting arguments with evidence, and accepting the final verdict.

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **ayyere haaro** | አይዬሬ ሀአሮ | `NP` | carkko laame | የአየር ንብረት | climate / weather pattern |
| **cililo keetha gaazeta** | ጪሊሎ ኬእꬃ ገአዜተ | `NP` | ho77o oyqqiya gaazeta | ሙቀት አማቂ ጋዞች (ግሪንሀውስ ጋዞች) | greenhouse gases |
| **ceco-gezze** | ጬጮ-ጌዝዜ | `N` | dari irxxiya gezze | ውርጭ (ከፍተኛ ቅዝቃዜ ያለው ደጋ) | alpine / frosty highland zone (Wurch) |
| **pilgganchcha** | ፒልግገንችቻ | `N` | erancha | ተመራማሪ / ሳይንቲስት | researcher / scientist |
| **qeexettees** | ቄእጤትቴእስ | `V.PASS` | qanxxettees | ይጨፈጨፋል / ይቆረጣል (ደን) | is cleared / deforested |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meega Qofa Medho (Sentence Structure: Kawushsha/Yeda, Dakko, Gite, pp. 137–138): Mallas: (1) 'Boorenni minnidi xana7iya gisho paaciyan lo77o muruuta demmis.' (Gite/Complex clause with 'gisho'); (2) 'Taani soo yishin nu soo aaya zagon dorsse qachawus.' (Complex clause with simultaneous converb '-ishin').
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእገ ቆፈ ሜꬎ (ሴንቴንጬ ስትሩጭቱሬ፦ ከዉሽሻ/ዬደ፣ ደክኮ፣ ጊቴ፣ ፕፕ። 137–138)፦ መልለስ፦ (1) 'ቦኦሬንኒ ሚንኒዲ ጠነዒየ ጊሾ ፐአጪየን ሎዕዖ ሙሩኡተ ዴምሚስ።' (ጊቴ/ጮምፕሌጥ ጭለኡሴ ዊꬅ 'ጊሾ')፤ (2) 'ተአኒ ሶኦ ዪሺን ኑ ሶኦ አአየ ዘጎን ዶርስሴ ቀቻዉስ።' (ጮምፕሌጥ ጭለኡሴ ዊꬅ ሲሙልተኔኦኡስ ጮንቬርብ '-ኢሺን')።
- **Amharic:** የዓረፍተ ነገር መዋቅር (ነጠላ፣ ድርብና ውስብስብ ዓረፍተ ነገር፣ ገጽ 137–138)፦ ምሳሌ፦ 'ቦሬኒ ጠንክሮ ስለሚያጠና በፈተና ጥሩ ውጤት አገኘ።' (ውስብስብ ዓ.ነገር)።
- **English:** Sentence Structure (Simple, Compound, and Complex Sentences, pp. 137–138): Combining clauses using causal ('gisho') and temporal/simultaneous ('-ishin') subordination.

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 4 (Phalqqe Qaalata, p. 135): Give antonyms for underlined words: 1. Tamaareti [maaran] uttidosona -> maara dhayon; 2. Dn. Saabori xeessara [linqisa] adde -> orddo / xifama; 3. Mishiriya [adussa] gixito danccettidi -> qantha.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 4 (ጳልቅቄ ቀአለተ፣ ፕ። 135)፦ ጊቬ አንቶኝምስ ፎር ኡንዴርሊኔድ ዎርድስ፦ 1። ተመአሬቲ [መአረን] ኡትቲዶሶነ -> መአረ ꬋዮን፤ 2። ድን። ሰአቦሪ ጤእስሰረ [ሊንቂሰ] አድዴ -> ኦርድዶ / ጢፈመ፤ 3። ሚሺሪየ [አዱስሰ] ጊጢቶ ደንጭጬትቲዲ -> ቀንꬃ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 4 (ተቃራኒ ቃላት፣ ገጽ 135)፦ 1. maaran (በሥርዓት) -> maara dhayon፤ 2. linqisa (ቀጭን) -> orddo፤ 3. adussa (ረጅም) -> qantha (አጭር)።
- **English:** Exercise 4 (Contextual Antonyms, p. 135): 1. maaran (orderly) -> maara dhayon (disorderly); 2. linqisa (slender) -> orddo (stout); 3. adussa (long) -> qantha (short).

### Gr8_Ch10 — Shemppo 10: Diraama | ሼምፕፖ 10፦ ዲረአመ | ምዕራፍ 10፦ ድራማ (ተውኔት) | Chapter 10: Theatrical Drama, Idioms (Aaqe Oda), and Discourse Connectors
- **Pages:** Book pp. 133–144 (PDF pp. 139–150) • **Source:** [Grade 8 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/160NHNy-4orPcAIk-L5t6tOEtZoWemz_-/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Shalula Woytetasu (Diraama Xuufe: Koyro Beso & Beso Nam77a)* (ሻሉለ ዎይቴተሱ (ዲረአመ ጡኡፌ፦ ኮይሮ ቤሶ & ቤሶ ነምዕዓ)) — ሻሉላ ተሸለመች (ባለ ሁለት ትዕይንት ተውኔት) / Shalula Was Awarded (Two-Scene School Drama on Girls' Education and Diligence) (pp. 141–145 (Book pp. 135–139))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Shalulo: 'Ta micheto! Taas mala lo77otethi gaatha mala; hayssatikkaa asatethi, lo77o era goda gido, de7iya eran deretetha minotethan maade gidiya gisho hintte giya cala mali tana kochenna. Taani ta malan zemppada, ta timirtte qanxxada dure gela aqanaas koyikke.' | ሻሉሎ፦ 'ተ ሚቼቶ! ተአስ መለ ሎዕዖቴꬂ ገአꬃ መለ፤ ሀይስሰቲክከአ አሰቴꬂ፣ ሎዕዖ እረ ጎደ ጊዶ፣ ዴዒየ እረን ዴሬቴꬃ ሚኖቴꬃን መአዴ ጊዲየ ጊሾ ሂንትቴ ጊየ ጨለ መሊ ተነ ኮቼንነ። ተአኒ ተ መለን ዜምፕፐደ፣ ተ ቲሚርትቴ ቀንጥጠደ ዱሬ ጌለ አቀነአስ ኮዪክኬ።' | ሻሉሎ፦ 'እህቶቼ! ለእኔ ውበት ማለት የውስጥ ውበት ነው፤ እርሱም ሰብአዊነት፣ የዕውቀት ባለቤት መሆንና ባለኝ ዕውቀት ማኅበረሰቡን በብርታት መርዳት ስለሆነ እናንተ የምትሉት የውጭ መልክ አያጓጓኝም። በመልኬ ተማምኜ ትምህርቴን አቋርጬ ሀብታም አግብቼ መኖር አልፈልግም።' | Shalulo: 'My sisters! For me, true beauty is inner beauty — character, possessing knowledge, and serving the community strongly with that knowledge. I refuse to drop out of school and rely on superficial looks to marry a rich man.' |
| Dn. Gixime: 'Nu timirtte keethan koyro kifileppe hospuntho kifile gaso de7iya 3000 tamaaretappe 99 giddole muruuta demmada koyro detha ke7ida Shubaashe Shalulis giigida woytuwa immana mala bonchon shoobays.' | ድን። ጊጢሜ፦ 'ኑ ቲሚርትቴ ኬእꬃን ኮይሮ ኪፊሌፕፔ ሆስፑንꬆ ኪፊሌ ገሶ ዴዒየ 3000 ተመአሬተፕፔ 99 ጊድዶሌ ሙሩኡተ ዴምመደ ኮይሮ ዴꬃ ኬዒደ ሹበአሼ ሻሉሊስ ጊኢጊደ ዎይቱወ ኢምመነ መለ ቦንቾን ሾኦበይስ።' | አቶ ግጪሜ (ርዕሰ መምህር)፦ 'በትምህርት ቤታችን ከ1ኛ እስከ 8ኛ ክፍል ከሚገኙ 3000 ተማሪዎች መካከል 99 አማካይ ውጤት በማምጣት አንደኛ የወጣችውን ተማሪ ሹባሼ ሻሉሎን ለመሸለም በክብር እጋብዛለሁ።' | Principal Gixime: 'With great joy I invite the Woreda Chief Administrator to present the award to student Shubaashe Shalulo, who ranked first out of 3,000 students in Grades 1–8 with an average score of 99%.' |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Diraama 1 (10.2 Haasaya Hiilla, pp. 140–141): Dn. Shuguxe, Mle. Balmoona & Gurzza — Mle. Balmoona: 'Cimatethay daro lo77o asa sigethiyaabayka keeha ooso gido shin cimatethappe guye ushshi uyashe daro karen qamisoppa.' Gurzza: 'Abbo itoy gida mela hayana karen daro qamisoppa!' Dn. Shuguxe: 'Entena aybe hirggisey taani ta keena eriya asi.' (Wurssethan Dn. Shuguxey ushshan mathottidi soo bishin oge bolla kaamey dom77idi hayqqees).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ዲረአመ 1 (10።2 ሀአሰየ ሂኢልለ፣ ፕፕ። 140–141)፦ ድን። ሹጉጤ፣ ምሌ። በልሞኦነ & ጉርዝዘ — ምሌ። በልሞኦነ፦ 'ጪመቴꬃይ ደሮ ሎዕዖ አሰ ሲጌꬂየአበይከ ኬእሀ ኦኦሶ ጊዶ ሺን ጪመቴꬃፕፔ ጉዬ ኡሽሺ ኡየሼ ደሮ ከሬን ቀሚሶፕፐ።' ጉርዝዘ፦ 'አብቦ ኢቶይ ጊደ ሜለ ሀየነ ከሬን ደሮ ቀሚሶፕፐ!' ድን። ሹጉጤ፦ 'እንቴነ አይቤ ሂርግጊሴይ ተአኒ ተ ኬእነ እሪየ አሲ።' (ዉርስሴꬃን ድን። ሹጉጤይ ኡሽሻን መꬆትቲዲ ሶኦ ቢሺን ኦጌ ቦልለ ከአሜይ ዶምዕዒዲ ሀይቅቄእስ)።
- **Amharic:** ድራማ 1 (ገጽ 140–141)፦ የአቶ ሹጉጤ፣ የወ/ሮ ባልሞና እና የልጃቸው ጉርዛ ምልልስ፦ ወ/ሮ ባልሞናና ጉርዛ አባታቸው ከሽምግልና በኋላ መጠጥ ጠጥቶ በምሽት እንዳይቆይ ቢመክሩትም አቶ ሹጉጤ 'ልኬን አውቃለሁ' ብሎ አምሽቶ በስካር ሲመለስ በመኪና ተገጭቶ ይሞታል።
- **English:** Drama Dialogue 1 (pp. 140–141): Dn. Shuguxe, his wife Balmoona, and son Gurzza — Balmoona and Gurzza plead with him not to drink late after elder mediation, but he insists he knows his limit, gets drunk at night, and is fatally struck by a car.
- **Gofatho Latin:** Diraama 2 (10.6 Xaafo Hiilla, p. 146): 'Ayfe caddana mithi ayfes beetenna' — Aawa Zalanthi, Aayi Danttenna & Na7a Gurzza dialogue on peace and reconciliation.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ዲረአመ 2 (10።6 ጠአፎ ሂኢልለ፣ ፕ። 146)፦ 'አይፌ ጨድደነ ሚꬂ አይፌስ ቤእቴንነ' — አአወ ዘለንꬂ፣ አአዪ ደንትቴንነ & ነዓ ጉርዝዘ ዲአሎጉእ ኦን ፔአጬ አንድ ሬጮንጪሊአቲኦን።
- **Amharic:** ድራማ 2 (ገጽ 146)፦ 'ዓይንን የሚወጋ እንጨት ለዓይን አይታይም' (Ayfe caddana mithi ayfes beetenna) — የአጭር ድራማ ምልልስ።
- **English:** Drama Dialogue 2 (p. 146): 'The twig that pokes the eye is not seen by the eye' — dramatic script writing exercise.

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **miiciso diraama** | ሚኢጪሶ ዲረአመ | `NP` | ufayssisoninne miiccison wuriya diraama | ኮሜዲ / አስቂኝ ድራማ (በደስታና በሳቅ የሚጠናቀቅ) | comedy drama (ends in joy and laughter) |
| **kaayiso diraama** | ከአዪሶ ዲረአመ | `NP` | kaayison wuriya diraama | ትራጄዲ / አሳዛኝ ድራማ (በሐዘን የሚጠናቀቅ) | tragedy drama (ends in sorrow/catastrophe) |
| **kaayo-miiciso diraama** | ከአዮ-ሚኢጪሶ ዲረአመ | `NP` | nam77u diraama kanddo shiishi oykkees | ትራጂ-ኮሜዲ (ሐዘንና ደስታን አጣምሮ የያዘ ድራማ) | tragicomedy drama (combines tragic and comic elements) |
| **kana inxxarsi** | ከነ ኢንጥጠርሲ | `IDIOM` | keehippe daro lee7o | እጅግ በጣም ስስ / ቀጭን (ፈሊጣዊ ንግግር) | paper-thin / extremely thin (lit. a dog's tongue — e.g., thin injera) |
| **kana siidhe** | ከነ ሲኢꬌ | `IDIOM` | daro irxxa | በጣም ቀዝቃዛ (ፈሊጣዊ ንግግር) | ice-cold (lit. a dog's nose) |
| **mana bangga** | መነ በንግገ | `IDIOM` | dharccettis, maara dhayis | የተበታተነ / ሥርዓት ያጣ | scattered in disorder (lit. spilled potter's barley) |
| **budon gukkida haathe** | ቡዶን ጉክኪደ ሀአꬄ | `IDIOM` | zaari simmonnabaa | ላይመለስ የፈሰሰ / ያለፈ ነገር | irretrievable / water spilled from a gourd |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Dumma Dumma Gasethota (5 Classes of Discourse Connectors, p. 148): (a) Dooretho (Alternative: woy, hankoode, atto giiko, ixxiko), (b) Gaaso (Causal/Resultative: hayssa gisho, ayis giikko, gidida gisho, hayssappe denddidayssan), (c) Phalqqe (Contrastive: shin, hanoshin, gidoppe attin, gidikkokka), (d) Guzho (Additive: gujjidi, hayssa bolla, haray qassi), (e) Kuuyethi (Sequential/Concluding: koyro, nam7anthon, kaallidi, wurssethan).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ዱምመ ዱምመ ገሴꬆተ (5 ጭለስሴስ ኦፍ ዲስጮኡርሴ ጮንኔጭቶርስ፣ ፕ። 148)፦ (አ) ዶኦሬꬆ (አልቴርነቲቬ፦ ዎይ፣ ሀንኮኦዴ፣ አትቶ ጊኢኮ፣ ኢጥጢኮ)፣ (ብ) ገአሶ (ጨኡሰል/ሬሱልተቲቬ፦ ሀይስሰ ጊሾ፣ አዪስ ጊኢክኮ፣ ጊዲደ ጊሾ፣ ሀይስሰፕፔ ዴንድዲደይስሰን)፣ (ጭ) ጳልቅቄ (ጮንትረስቲቬ፦ ሺን፣ ሀኖሺን፣ ጊዶፕፔ አትቲን፣ ጊዲክኮክከ)፣ (ድ) ጉዦ (አድዲቲቬ፦ ጉጅጂዲ፣ ሀይስሰ ቦልለ፣ ሀረይ ቀስሲ)፣ (እ) ኩኡዬꬂ (ሴቁእንቲአል/ጮንጭሉዲንግ፦ ኮይሮ፣ ነምዓንꬆን፣ ከአልሊዲ፣ ዉርስሴꬃን)።
- **Amharic:** አምስቱ የአያያዥ ቃላት ክፍሎች (Gasethota፣ ገጽ 148)፦ (ሀ) አማራጭ አመልካች (Dooretho)፣ (ለ) ምክንያትና ውጤት አመልካች (Gaaso)፣ (ሐ) ተቃርኖ አመልካች (Phalqqe)፣ (መ) ተጨማሪ ሐሳብ አመልካች (Guzho)፣ (ሠ) ቅደም ተከተልና ማጠቃለያ አመልካች (Kuuyethi)።
- **English:** Five Classes of Discourse Connectors (Gasethota, p. 148): Alternative (Dooretho), Causal/Resultative (Gaaso), Contrastive/Concessive (Phalqqe), Additive (Guzho), and Sequential/Concluding (Kuuyethi).

#### E. Proverbs, Idioms, Riddles & Oral Literature (`Leemiso / Tosse / Aaqeta / Sedhemenkke / Qan77ishe`)
| Category | Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Tosse (Proverb, p. 146) | **Ayfe caddana mithi ayfes beetenna.** | አይፌ ጨድደነ ሚꬂ አይፌስ ቤእቴንነ። | ዓይንን የሚወጋ እንጨት ለዓይን አይታይም። | The twig that will poke the eye is not visible to the eye. |
| Aaqeta (Idiom Example, p. 147) | **Kataman uukettiya soolloy kana inxxarssi aazees.** | ከተመን ኡኡኬትቲየ ሶኦልሎይ ከነ ኢንጥጠርስሲ አአዜእስ። | በከተማ የሚጋገረው እንጀራ እንደ ውሻ ምላስ የሳሳ ነው። | The flatbread baked in the city is as thin as a dog's tongue. |
| Aaqeta (Idiom Example, p. 147) | **Harggancha geday kana siidhe gidis.** | ሀርግገንቻ ጌደይ ከነ ሲኢꬌ ጊዲስ። | የታማሚው እግር እንደ ውሻ አፍንጫ ቀዘቀዘ። | The patient's foot became as cold as a dog's nose. |

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 1 (Hayzo Wode, p. 140): 1. [Miiciso] diraama qommoy wursethay miicison wurees. 2. [Kaayiso] diraama qommoy wursethay kaayison wurees. 3. [Kaayo-Miiciso] diraama qommoy wursethay kaayisoninne miicison wurees.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 1 (ሀይዞ ዎዴ፣ ፕ። 140)፦ 1። [ሚኢጪሶ] ዲረአመ ቆምሞይ ዉርሴꬃይ ሚኢጪሶን ዉሬእስ። 2። [ከአዪሶ] ዲረአመ ቆምሞይ ዉርሴꬃይ ከአዪሶን ዉሬእስ። 3። [ከአዮ-ሚኢጪሶ] ዲረአመ ቆምሞይ ዉርሴꬃይ ከአዪሶኒንኔ ሚኢጪሶን ዉሬእስ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 1 (ባዶ ቦታ መሙላት፣ ገጽ 140)፦ 1. [Miiciso (ኮሜዲ)] በሳቅ ይጠናቀቃል፤ 2. [Kaayiso (ትራጄዲ)] በሐዘን ይጠናቀቃል፤ 3. [Kaayo-Miiciso (ትራጂ-ኮሜዲ)] በሐዘንና በሳቅ ይጠናቀቃል።
- **English:** Exercise 1 (Drama Genres Cloze, p. 140): 1. Comedy (Miiciso) ends in laughter; 2. Tragedy (Kaayiso) ends in grief; 3. Tragicomedy (Kaayo-Miiciso) combines both.
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 2 (Qawosa Gasetho, p. 149): 2. Phalqqe qofata gasethiya gasethoti oonanteeko qonccisite -> Zaaro: shin, hanoshin, hanoppe attin, gidoppe attin, gidikkokka.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 2 (ቀዎሰ ገሴꬆ፣ ፕ። 149)፦ 2። ጳልቅቄ ቆፈተ ገሴꬂየ ገሴꬆቲ ኦኦነንቴእኮ ቆንጭጪሲቴ -> ዘአሮ፦ ሺን፣ ሀኖሺን፣ ሀኖፕፔ አትቲን፣ ጊዶፕፔ አትቲን፣ ጊዲክኮክከ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 2 (ሰዋስው፣ ገጽ 149)፦ 2. ተቃራኒ ሐሳቦችን የሚያያይዙ አያያዦች እነማን ናቸው? -> መልስ፦ shin, hanoshin, gidoppe attin, gidikkokka።
- **English:** Exercise 2 (Contrastive Conjunctions, p. 149): 2. List the conjunctions that connect contrasting ideas -> Answer: shin, hanoshin, hanoppe attin, gidoppe attin, gidikkokka.

### Gr9_Ch1 — Shemppo 1: Doona | ሼምፕፖ 1፦ ዶኦነ | ምዕራፍ 1፦ ቋንቋ (የቋንቋ ምንነት፣ ባህሪያትና የዓረፍተ ነገር ዓይነቶች) | Chapter 1: Language (Nature, Growth, Endangerment, and Sentence Types)
- **Pages:** Book pp. 1–7 (PDF pp. 5–11) • **Source:** [Grade 9 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1uvGaBT4-O_Jn7oQbuNl1W88lFBFiHULU/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Doona* (ዶኦነ) — ቋንቋ (የቋንቋ ምንነት፣ ዕድገትና መጥፋት ምክንያቶች) / Language: Social Function, Linguistic Structure, Growth, and Language Endangerment (pp. 6–7 (Book pp. 2–3))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Dooni issippetetha asa saynsse gididi issoy haraara pa kayuwa, ufayssa, gallasi gallasi pantana gakkiya metuwa wolira maadettiya miishe. | ዶኦኒ ኢስሲፕፔቴꬃ አሰ ሰይንስሴ ጊዲዲ ኢስሶይ ሀረአረ ፐ ከዩወ፣ ኡፈይስሰ፣ ገልለሲ ገልለሲ ፐንተነ ገክኪየ ሜቱወ ዎሊረ መአዴትቲየ ሚኢሼ። | ቋንቋ ማኅበራዊ ሳይንስ ሆኖ ሰዎች እርስ በርሳቸው ሐዘናቸውን፣ ደስታቸውንና የዕለት ተዕለት ችግሮቻቸውን የሚጋሩበትና የሚረዳዱበት መሣሪያ ነው። | Language is a social science and medium through which people share their grief, joy, and daily challenges and support one another. |
| Dooni diccey ooratha maxaafeti attamettishin, gaazexatinne hara xuufeti za nne ge birshshethi imettishin, timirtte eqotatan timirttey imettishinne miidiyan go7etethi doomettishina. | ዶኦኒ ዲጭጬይ ኦኦረꬃ መጠአፌቲ አትተሜትቲሺን፣ ገአዜጠቲንኔ ሀረ ጡኡፌቲ ዘ ንኔ ጌ ቢርሽሼꬂ ኢሜትቲሺን፣ ቲሚርትቴ እቆተተን ቲሚርትቴይ ኢሜትቲሺንኔ ሚኢዲየን ጎዔቴꬂ ዶኦሜትቲሺነ። | ቋንቋ የሚያድገው አዳዲስ መጻሕፍት ሲታተሙ፣ ጋዜጦችና ሌሎች ጽሑፎች ሲተረጎሙ፣ በትምህርት ተቋማት ትምህርት ሲሰጥበትና በሚዲያ ጥቅም ላይ ሲውል ነው። | A language grows when new books are published, newspapers and texts are translated, schools teach in it, and media broadcast in it. |
| Dooni hayqqey shoorotetha gaason hara doona go7etethan haasayiya asay aggishininne daro wode doona bolla wuda wothiya asi dhayishin doonay hayqos aadhdhi imettees. | ዶኦኒ ሀይቅቄይ ሾኦሮቴꬃ ገአሶን ሀረ ዶኦነ ጎዔቴꬃን ሀአሰዪየ አሰይ አግጊሺኒንኔ ደሮ ዎዴ ዶኦነ ቦልለ ዉደ ዎꬂየ አሲ ꬋዪሺን ዶኦነይ ሀይቆስ አአꬍꬊ ኢሜትቴእስ። | ቋንቋ የሚሞተው በጉርብትና ተጽዕኖ ተናጋሪዎቹ ሌላ ቋንቋ እየተጠቀሙ የራሳቸውን ሲተዉና ለቋንቋው ትኩረት የሚሰጥ ሰው ሲጠፋ ነው። | A language dies when speakers abandon it in favor of neighboring languages or when natural disasters and lack of institutional attention cause its speakers to vanish. |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze Nam77a (1.2 Haasaya, p. 7): 1. Dooni asa na7aba gishin ay guussee? 2. Dooni hayqqiya gaasoti aybee? 3. Dooni saynsse guussi ay guusseekko paattite. 4. Dooni awppe tamaaretti meezetii? 5. Doona kanddoti oonantteekko paatti odite.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ ነምዕዓ (1።2 ሀአሰየ፣ ፕ። 7)፦ 1። ዶኦኒ አሰ ነዓበ ጊሺን አይ ጉኡስሴእ? 2። ዶኦኒ ሀይቅቂየ ገአሶቲ አይቤእ? 3። ዶኦኒ ሰይንስሴ ጉኡስሲ አይ ጉኡስሴእክኮ ፐአትቲቴ። 4። ዶኦኒ አውፕፔ ተመአሬትቲ ሜእዜቲኢ? 5። ዶኦነ ከንድዶቲ ኦኦነንትቴእክኮ ፐአትቲ ኦዲቴ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 2 (1.2 ንግግር፣ ገጽ 7)፦ 1. ቋንቋ የሰው ልጅ ብቻ ሀብት ነው ሲባል ምን ማለት ነው? 2. ቋንቋ የሚሞትባቸው ምክንያቶች ምንድን ናቸው? 3. ቋንቋ ሳይንስ ነው ማለት ምን ማለት ነው? 4. ቋንቋ ከየት ይማራል? 5. የቋንቋ ባህሪያትን ዘርዝሩ።
- **English:** Exercise 2 (1.2 Oral Discussion, p. 7): Discuss why language is uniquely human, why languages die, why language is systematic/scientific, how language is acquired from society, and the core properties of language.

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **dano** | ደኖ | `N` | qopponna gakkiya meto | ሳይታሰብ የሚደርስ አደጋ / ችግር | unexpected calamity / disaster (qopponna gakkiya meto) |
| **haggazo** | ሀግገዞ | `N` | maado / ooso | አገልግሎት / ድጋፍ | service / support |
| **za birshshethi** | ዘ ቢርሽሼꬂ | `NP` | qaabima / zodo birshshethi | ቀጥተኛ (የመዝገበ-ቃላት) ፍቺ | literal / denotative meaning |
| **ge birshshethi** | ጌ ቢርሽሼꬂ | `NP` | gelosa / geema birshshethi | ውስጠ-ወይራ / አገባባዊ ፍቺ | contextual / figurative meaning |
| **lanxxetethi** | ለንጥጤቴꬂ | `N` | hiilla / qaratethi | ቅልጥፍና / ችሎታ | fluency / proficiency |
| **qaabima birshshethi** | ቀአቢመ ቢርሽሼꬂ | `NP` | suure birshshethi | የመዝገበ-ቃላት (ቀጥተኛ) ፍቺ | dictionary / lexical meaning |
| **gelosa birshshethi** | ጌሎሰ ቢርሽሼꬂ | `NP` | meega qofa garssan birshshethi | አገባባዊ ፍቺ (በዓረፍተ ነገር ውስጥ የሚኖረው ፍቺ) | contextual meaning in a sentence |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** 1. Qaalatas Gelosa Birshshetha (Contextual Polysemy of 'mis', pp. 8–9): 'mis' = (a) Na7ay quma mis ('ate food' - literal); (b) Gam77ida gisho oosoy tana mis ('work exhausted me'); (c) Soolluwa tami mis ('fire burned the flatbread'); (d) Ta miishiya kaysoy mis ('thief stole my money'); (e) Kawoy gadiya mis ('king ruled/annexed the land').
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 1። ቀአለተስ ጌሎሰ ቢርሽሼꬃ (ጮንቴጥቱአል ፖልይሴምይ ኦፍ 'ሚስ'፣ ፕፕ። 8–9)፦ 'ሚስ' = (አ) ነዓይ ቁመ ሚስ ('አቴ ፎኦድ' - ሊቴረል)፤ (ብ) ገምዕዒደ ጊሾ ኦኦሶይ ተነ ሚስ ('ዎርክ እጥሀኡስቴድ ሜ')፤ (ጭ) ሶኦልሉወ ተሚ ሚስ ('ፊሬ ቡርኔድ ꬄ ፍለትብሬአድ')፤ (ድ) ተ ሚኢሺየ ከይሶይ ሚስ ('ꬂእፍ ስቶሌ ምይ ሞኔይ')፤ (እ) ከዎይ ገዲየ ሚስ ('ኪንግ ሩሌድ/አንኔጤድ ꬄ ለንድ')።
- **Amharic:** 1. የቃላት አገባባዊ ፍቺ (Gelosa Birshshetha፣ ገጽ 8–9)፦ 'mis' (በላ) የሚለው ቃል በአገባቡ፦ (ሀ) ምግብ በላ፤ (ለ) ሥራው አደከመኝ፤ (ሐ) እንጀራውን እሳት አቃጠለው፤ (መ) ገንዘቤን ሌባ ሰረቀው፤ (ሠ) ንጉሡ አገሩን ገዛ።
