- **English:** 1. Contextual Polysemy (Gelosa Birshshetha, pp. 8–9): Demonstrates how the verb 'mis' ('ate') shifts meaning across 5 contexts: eating food, exhausting someone, fire burning bread, a thief stealing money, or a king ruling territory.
- **Gofatho Latin:** 2. Meega Qofa Qommota Go77an (4 Sentence Types by Function) & Medhuwan (4 Sentence Types by Structure) (pp. 9–11): By function: (1) Paate meega qofa ('Awi ushachara dolidi haddirssi wullees.'), (2) Oysho meega qofa ('Ne aappuntho kifile tamaaree?'), (3) Kiita meega qofa ('Tamaare soo 2:30 gelite.'), (4) Malaalo meega qofa ('Hanna na7iya waana lo77ay!'). By structure: (1) Kawshsha (Simple), (2) Dakko (Compound), (3) Gite (Complex), (4) Dakko gite (Compound-Complex).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 2። ሜእገ ቆፈ ቆምሞተ ጎዕዓን (4 ሴንቴንጬ ትይፔስ ብይ ፉንጭቲኦን) & ሜꬉወን (4 ሴንቴንጬ ትይፔስ ብይ ስትሩጭቱሬ) (ፕፕ። 9–11)፦ ብይ ፉንጭቲኦን፦ (1) ፐአቴ ሜእገ ቆፈ ('አዊ ኡሻቻረ ዶሊዲ ሀድዲርስሲ ዉልሌእስ።')፣ (2) ኦይሾ ሜእገ ቆፈ ('ኔ አአፕፑንꬆ ኪፊሌ ተመአሬእ?')፣ (3) ኪኢተ ሜእገ ቆፈ ('ተመአሬ ሶኦ 2፦30 ጌሊቴ።')፣ (4) መለአሎ ሜእገ ቆፈ ('ሀንነ ነዒየ ወአነ ሎዕዓይ!')። ብይ ስትሩጭቱሬ፦ (1) ከውሽሻ (ሲምፕሌ)፣ (2) ደክኮ (ጮምፖኡንድ)፣ (3) ጊቴ (ጮምፕሌጥ)፣ (4) ደክኮ ጊቴ (ጮምፖኡንድ-ጮምፕሌጥ)።
- **Amharic:** 2. የዓረፍተ ነገር ዓይነቶች በአገልግሎታቸው (Paate, Oysho, Kiita, Malaalo) እና በአወቃቀራቸው (Kawshsha/ነጠላ፣ Dakko/ድርብ፣ Gite/ውስብስብ፣ Dakko gite/ድርብ ውስብስብ) (ገጽ 9–11)።
- **English:** 2. Sentence Classification by Function (Declarative/Paate, Interrogative/Oysho, Imperative/Kiita, Exclamatory/Malaalo) and by Clause Structure (Simple/Kawshsha, Compound/Dakko, Complex/Gite, Compound-Complex/Dakko Gite).

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze Isso (1.1 Hayzo, p. 5): 1. Asa na7i paw si7etiyaaba kaha giddon zaarethidi [doona] ogetan qonccisees. 5. Asa na7i pa qofa nam77u ogen qonccisees: [haasayon] nne [xaafon].
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ ኢስሶ (1።1 ሀይዞ፣ ፕ። 5)፦ 1። አሰ ነዒ ፐው ሲዔቲየአበ ከሀ ጊድዶን ዘአሬꬂዲ [ዶኦነ] ኦጌተን ቆንጭጪሴእስ። 5። አሰ ነዒ ፐ ቆፈ ነምዕዑ ኦጌን ቆንጭጪሴእስ፦ [ሀአሰዮን] ንኔ [ጠአፎን]።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 1 (1.1 ማዳመጥ፣ ገጽ 5)፦ የሰው ልጅ ሐሳቡን በሁለት መንገድ ይገልጻል፦ [በንግግር (haasayon)] እና [በጽሑፍ (xaafon)]።
- **English:** Exercise 1 (Listening Cloze, p. 5): Humanity expresses thought in two primary modalities: spoken language (haasayon) and written language (xaafon).
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze Ichchasha (1.5 Qabirshshe, pp. 8–9): Construct two sentences showing literal vs. contextual meanings for: 1. Tama, 2. Oykkis, 3. Dhuuqqis, 4. Shugo, 5. Michchis, 6. Godare, 7. Qeris, 8. Haathe, 9. Shuchchi, 10. Yeggasu.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ ኢችቻሻ (1።5 ቀቢርሽሼ፣ ፕፕ። 8–9)፦ ጮንስትሩጭት ትዎ ሴንቴንጬስ ሾዊንግ ሊቴረል ቭስ። ጮንቴጥቱአል ሜአኒንግስ ፎር፦ 1። ተመ፣ 2። ኦይክኪስ፣ 3። ꬉኡቅቂስ፣ 4። ሹጎ፣ 5። ሚችቺስ፣ 6። ጎደሬ፣ 7። ቄሪስ፣ 8። ሀአꬄ፣ 9። ሹችቺ፣ 10። ዬግገሱ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 5 (አገባባዊ ፍቺ፣ ገጽ 8–9)፦ ለ10 ቃላት (Tama, Oykkis, Dhuuqqis, Shugo, Michchis, Godare, Qeris, Haathe, Shuchchi, Yeggasu) ቀጥተኛና አገባባዊ ፍቺ የሚያሳዩ ሁለት ዓረፍተ ነገሮችን መመሥረት።
- **English:** Exercise 5 (Contextual Polysemy Sentences, pp. 8–9): Write pairs of sentences illustrating literal and figurative senses for the 10 target words.

### Gr9_Ch2 — Shemppo 2: Giira | ሼምፕፖ 2፦ ጊኢረ | ምዕራፍ 2፦ ግብር (የግብር አከፋፈልና ጠቀሜታ) | Chapter 2: Taxation, Public Infrastructure, and Verb Tense Paradigms
- **Pages:** Book pp. 8–13 (PDF pp. 12–17) • **Source:** [Grade 9 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1uvGaBT4-O_Jn7oQbuNl1W88lFBFiHULU/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Giira Cigo* (ጊኢረ ጪጎ) — የግብር አከፋፈልና ለአገር ልማት ያለው ፋይዳ / Tax Collection: Origins in Ancient Egypt, Sources of Revenue, and Public Infrastructure (p. 13 (Book p. 9))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Giira cigoy alame dethaba gididi koyro Gibxxen doomettis. | ጊኢረ ጪጎይ አለሜ ዴꬃበ ጊዲዲ ኮይሮ ጊብጥጤን ዶኦሜትቲስ። | የግብር አከፋፈል ዓለም አቀፋዊ ልምድ ሲሆን ለመጀመሪያ ጊዜ በግብፅ ተጀመረ። | Tax collection is a global institution that first began in ancient Egypt. |
| Giiroy kawo oosanchchatappe, goshshanchatappe, zal77anchatappenne issi issi eqotatappe shiiqiya qaye bira demisha gididi baaso dichcha eqotata keexxanaw maadiya miishe. | ጊኢሮይ ከዎ ኦኦሰንችቻተፕፔ፣ ጎሽሻንቻተፕፔ፣ ዘልዕዓንቻተፕፔንኔ ኢስሲ ኢስሲ እቆተተፕፔ ሺኢቂየ ቀዬ ቢረ ዴሚሻ ጊዲዲ በአሶ ዲችቻ እቆተተ ኬእጥጠነው መአዲየ ሚኢሼ። | ግብር ከመንግሥት ሠራተኞች፣ ከአርሶ አደሮች፣ ከነጋዴዎችና ከተለያዩ ተቋማት የሚሰበሰብ የገንዘብ ገቢ ሆኖ መሠረተ ልማቶችን ለመገንባት የሚረዳ መሣሪያ ነው። | Tax is monetary revenue collected from civil servants, farmers, merchants, and enterprises to build public infrastructure and accelerate services. |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze Nam77a (2.2 Haasaya, p. 14): 1. Giira cigos qoppobay aybee? 2. Giira demishay beettiya pulttoti oonanttee? 6. Giira wogara cigo gishin qanthan ay guussee?
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ ነምዕዓ (2።2 ሀአሰየ፣ ፕ። 14)፦ 1። ጊኢረ ጪጎስ ቆፕፖበይ አይቤእ? 2። ጊኢረ ዴሚሻይ ቤእትቲየ ፑልትቶቲ ኦኦነንትቴእ? 6። ጊኢረ ዎገረ ጪጎ ጊሺን ቀንꬃን አይ ጉኡስሴእ?
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 2 (2.2 ንግግር፣ ገጽ 14)፦ 1. የግብር አከፋፈል ዓላማ ምንድን ነው? 2. የግብር ገቢ ምንጮች እነማን ናቸው? 6. ግብርን በአግባቡ መክፈል ማለት ምን ማለት ነው?
- **English:** Exercise 2 (2.2 Oral Discussion, p. 14): Discuss the purpose of taxation, sources of tax revenue, and the meaning of paying taxes honestly, on time, and voluntarily.

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **qaye bira** | ቀዬ ቢረ | `NP` | qoodettiya bira | ጥሬ ገንዘብ (የሚቆጠር ገንዘብ) | liquid cash / currency (qoodettiya woykko geesha bira) |
| **abbe qosonna** | አብቤ ቆሶንነ | `VP` | minjjonna | ሳይለግሙ / ሳይቆጥቡ | without holding back / wholeheartedly |
| **demisha** | ዴሚሻ | `N` | geliya bira | ገቢ | income / revenue |
| **cawa gate** | ጨወ ገቴ | `NP` | ooso ciga / damooze | ደመወዝ (የላብ ዋጋ) | salary / wages |
| **worandzancha** | ዎረንꬓንቻ | `N` | kayso / higge menthiya ase | ዘራፊ / ሕገ-ወጥ ወንጀለኛ | robber / outlaw |
| **mor77ettonna** | ሞርዕዔትቶንነ | `ADV` | nam77u qopponna | ሳያመነቱ / ሳይጠራጠሩ | without hesitation or doubt |
| **baaso dichcha** | በአሶ ዲችቻ | `NP` | dichcha eqota | መሠረተ ልማት | infrastructure (roads, electricity, clean water, schools, clinics) |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Haasaya Wodenne Peeshota (Zara 1: Verb Tense Table, pp. 16–17): Three speech times: (1) Aadhode (Past), (2) Ha7ode (Present), (3) Sinthode (Future). Zara 1 Paradigm: Xaafo -> Xaafis (Aadhode) | Xaafees (Ha7ode) | Xaafana (Sinthode); Goyy -> Goyis | Goyees | Goyana; Ooso -> Oothis | Oothees | Oothana; Muussa -> Mis | Mees | Maana; Nabbabo -> Nabbabis | Nabbabees | Nabbabana.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሀአሰየ ዎዴንኔ ፔእሾተ (ዘረ 1፦ ቬርብ ቴንሴ ተብሌ፣ ፕፕ። 16–17)፦ ꬅሬእ ስፔእች ቲሜስ፦ (1) አአꬎዴ (ፐስት)፣ (2) ሀዖዴ (ፕሬሴንት)፣ (3) ሲንꬆዴ (ፉቱሬ)። ዘረ 1 ፐረዲግም፦ ጠአፎ -> ጠአፊስ (አአꬎዴ) | ጠአፌእስ (ሀዖዴ) | ጠአፈነ (ሲንꬆዴ)፤ ጎይይ -> ጎዪስ | ጎዬእስ | ጎየነ፤ ኦኦሶ -> ኦኦꬂስ | ኦኦꬄእስ | ኦኦꬃነ፤ ሙኡስሰ -> ሚስ | ሜእስ | መአነ፤ ነብበቦ -> ነብበቢስ | ነብበቤእስ | ነብበበነ።
- **Amharic:** የንግግር ጊዜያትና ግሶች (Zara 1፣ ገጽ 16–17)፦ ሦስቱ የንግግር ጊዜያት፦ ኃላፊ ጊዜ (Aadhode)፣ የአሁን ጊዜ (Ha7ode) እና የወደፊት ጊዜ (Sinthode)። ምሳሌ፦ Xaafis (ጻፈ) | Xaafees (ይጽፋል) | Xaafana (ይጽፋል/ወደፊት)።
- **English:** Verb Tenses and Inflectional Table (Zara 1, pp. 16–17): Past (Aadhode: -is), Present (Ha7ode: -ees), and Future (Sinthode: -ana) across verbal nouns (Xaafo, Goyy, Ooso, Muussa, Nabbabo).

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze Heedza (2.3 Nabbabo Dooro, pp. 14–15): 6. 'Giira wogara cigo' giya qofa loythi qonccisey: A. Maarara, B. Wodera, C. Eenotara, D. Ubbaa -> Zaaro: D (Ubbaa). 8. Giiras pultto gidonnayssi awssee? A. Zal7anchchata, B. Goshanchchata, C. Woranzanchchata, D. Kawo oosanchchata -> Zaaro: C (Woranzanchchata).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ ሄእꬓ (2።3 ነብበቦ ዶኦሮ፣ ፕፕ። 14–15)፦ 6። 'ጊኢረ ዎገረ ጪጎ' ጊየ ቆፈ ሎይꬂ ቆንጭጪሴይ፦ አ። መአረረ፣ ብ። ዎዴረ፣ ጭ። እእኖተረ፣ ድ። ኡብበአ -> ዘአሮ፦ ድ (ኡብበአ)። 8። ጊኢረስ ፑልትቶ ጊዶንነይስሲ አውስሴእ? አ። ዘልዓንችቻተ፣ ብ። ጎሻንችቻተ፣ ጭ። ዎረንዘንችቻተ፣ ድ። ከዎ ኦኦሰንችቻተ -> ዘአሮ፦ ጭ (ዎረንዘንችቻተ)።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 3 (ምርጫ፣ ገጽ 14–15)፦ 6. ግብርን በአግባቡ መክፈል ማለት በሕግ፣ በወቅቱና በፈቃደኝነት መክፈል ነው (መልስ፦ D ሁሉም)። 8. የግብር ምንጭ ያልሆነው የትኛው ነው? -> መልስ፦ C (ዘራፊዎች/Woranzanchchata)።
- **English:** Exercise 3 (Reading Multiple Choice, pp. 14–15): 6. Paying tax properly means paying according to rules, on time, and willingly (D. All). 8. Which is NOT a legitimate source of tax? -> Answer: C (Outlaws/Woranzanchchata).
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze Usuppuna (2.6 Qawosa II, p. 17): Identify the tense of each sentence: 1. Zambbota caamma shammawus -> Ha7ode; 2. Daanna Buujuloy boori waaxis -> Aadhode; 3. Taani omarssi ginxxo xaafana -> Sinthode; 4. Kambbaati aaho oosuwa oothees -> Ha7ode; 5. Aadhoy pa na7aara kaa7is -> Aadhode.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ ኡሱፕፑነ (2።6 ቀዎሰ ኢኢ፣ ፕ። 17)፦ ኢዴንቲፍይ ꬄ ቴንሴ ኦፍ እአች ሴንቴንጬ፦ 1። ዘምብቦተ ጨአምመ ሻምመዉስ -> ሀዖዴ፤ 2። ደአንነ ቡኡጁሎይ ቦኦሪ ወአጢስ -> አአꬎዴ፤ 3። ተአኒ ኦመርስሲ ጊንጥጦ ጠአፈነ -> ሲንꬆዴ፤ 4። ከምብበአቲ አአሆ ኦኦሱወ ኦኦꬄእስ -> ሀዖዴ፤ 5። አአꬎይ ፐ ነዓአረ ከአዒስ -> አአꬎዴ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 6 (ሰዋስው፣ ገጽ 17)፦ የዓረፍተ ነገሮቹን ጊዜ መለየት፦ 1. የአሁን (Ha7ode)፤ 2. ኃላፊ (Aadhode)፤ 3. የወደፊት (Sinthode)፤ 4. የአሁን (Ha7ode)፤ 5. ኃላፊ (Aadhode)።
- **English:** Exercise 6 (Identifying Verb Tenses, p. 17): 1. shammawus (Ha7ode/Present); 2. waaxis (Aadhode/Past); 3. xaafana (Sinthode/Future); 4. oothees (Ha7ode/Present); 5. kaa7is (Aadhode/Past).

### Gr9_Ch3 — Shemppo 3: Miniraale Booho | ሼምፕፖ 3፦ ሚኒረአሌ ቦኦሆ | ምዕራፍ 3፦ የማዕድን ቁፋሮና ልማት | Chapter 3: Mineral Mining, Paragraph Composition (Daassa Xaafo), and Punctuation
- **Pages:** Book pp. 14–22 (PDF pp. 18–26) • **Source:** [Grade 9 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1uvGaBT4-O_Jn7oQbuNl1W88lFBFiHULU/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Miniraale Booho* (ሚኒረአሌ ቦኦሆ) — የማዕድን ቁፋሮና የተፈጥሮ ሀብት ጥበቃ / Mineral Mining: Solid vs. Liquid Minerals, Industrial Processing, and Conservation (p. 20 (Book p. 16))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Miniraaley biita garssaninne haatha zedhen beettiya medheta shalo. | ሚኒረአሌይ ቢኢተ ገርስሰኒንኔ ሀአꬃ ዜꬌን ቤእትቲየ ሜꬌተ ሻሎ። | ማዕድን በመሬት ውስጥና በውኃ ወለል ስር (ከርሰ ምድር) የሚገኝ የተፈጥሮ ሀብት ነው። | Minerals are natural resources found underground and beneath waterbeds. |
| Miniraale qommoti nam77u gita cugatan shaakettoosona: minniya miniraaleti (worqa, bira, xarqqamala, biraata, shuchcha kasale, naase, maxine) nne gukkiya miniraaleti (haathe, lambba, kaame zayte). | ሚኒረአሌ ቆምሞቲ ነምዕዑ ጊተ ጩገተን ሻአኬትቶኦሶነ፦ ሚንኒየ ሚኒረአሌቲ (ዎርቀ፣ ቢረ፣ ጠርቅቀመለ፣ ቢረአተ፣ ሹችቻ ከሰሌ፣ ነአሴ፣ መጢኔ) ንኔ ጉክኪየ ሚኒረአሌቲ (ሀአꬄ፣ ለምብበ፣ ከአሜ ዘይቴ)። | የማዕድን ዓይነቶች በሁለት ዋና ቡድኖች ይከፈላሉ፦ ጠጣር ማዕድናት (ወርቅ፣ ብር፣ ነሐስ፣ ብረት፣ የድንጋይ ከሰል፣ ጨው) እና ፈሳሽ ማዕድናት (ውኃ፣ ጋዝ/ነዳጅ፣ ድፍድፍ ዘይት)። | Minerals are classified into two major groups: solid minerals (gold, silver, copper, iron, coal, bronze, salt) and liquid minerals (water, kerosene/gas, crude oil). |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze Nam77a (3.2 Haasaya, p. 19): 1. Hintte heeran de7iya miniraaleta ane hintte laggetas paattite. 2. Miniraaleti aybis go77iyaakko qonccisite.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ ነምዕዓ (3።2 ሀአሰየ፣ ፕ። 19)፦ 1። ሂንትቴ ሄእረን ዴዒየ ሚኒረአሌተ አኔ ሂንትቴ ለግጌተስ ፐአትቲቴ። 2። ሚኒረአሌቲ አይቢስ ጎዕዒየአክኮ ቆንጭጪሲቴ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 2 (3.2 ንግግር፣ ገጽ 19)፦ 1. በአካባቢያችሁ የሚገኙ ማዕድናትን ለጓደኞቻችሁ ዘርዝሩ። 2. ማዕድናት ለምን እንደሚጠቅሙ አብራሩ።
- **English:** Exercise 2 (3.2 Oral Discussion, p. 19): Describe local mineral resources in your area and explain their industrial and household uses.

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **zedhe** | ዜꬌ | `N` | hiithe / garssa | ከርሰ ምድር / የውኃ ወለል ስር | subsoil / seabed / underground depth |
| **xarqqamala** | ጠርቅቀመለ | `N` | miniraale qommo | መዳብ | copper |
| **shuchcha kasale** | ሹችቻ ከሰሌ | `NP` | biittappe ke7iya kasale | የድንጋይ ከሰል | coal / mineral coal |
| **paabirikka** | ፐአቢሪክከ | `N` | miisheta muruutisiya eqota | ፋብሪካ (ዕቃዎችን የሚያመርት ተቋም) | factory / manufacturing plant |
| **inddusttere** | ኢንድዱስትቴሬ | `N` | maashinen muruutiso | ኢንዱስትሪ (በማሽን ማምረት) | industry |
| **hapha** | ሀጳ | `N` | giddon oykkidaba | እቅፍ / በውስጡ የያዘው ክምችት | reserve / deposit held inside the earth |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** 1. Daassa Xaafo (Paragraph Writing Structure, pp. 21–22): Issi daassas heedzu kifileti de7oosona: (a) Ayfe meega qofa (Topic sentence), (b) Qonccisiya / カlssiya meega qofata (Supporting sentences), (c) Kuushsha meega qofa (Concluding sentence). Model paragraph on 'Haatha Go77a' (The Value of Water, p. 22).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 1። ደአስሰ ጠአፎ (ፐረግረጵ ውሪቲንግ ስትሩጭቱሬ፣ ፕፕ። 21–22)፦ ኢስሲ ደአስሰስ ሄእꬑ ኪፊሌቲ ዴዖኦሶነ፦ (አ) አይፌ ሜእገ ቆፈ (ቶፒጭ ሴንቴንጬ)፣ (ብ) ቆንጭጪሲየ / カልስሲየ ሜእገ ቆፈተ (ሱፕፖርቲንግ ሴንቴንጬስ)፣ (ጭ) ኩኡሽሻ ሜእገ ቆፈ (ጮንጭሉዲንግ ሴንቴንጬ)። ሞዴል ፐረግረጵ ኦን 'ሀአꬃ ጎዕዓ' (ꬄ ቨሉእ ኦፍ ወቴር፣ ፕ። 22)።
- **Amharic:** 1. የአንቀጽ አጻጻፍ (Daassa Xaafo፣ ገጽ 21–22)፦ አንድ አንቀጽ ሦስት ክፍሎች አሉት፦ (ሀ) ኃይለ-ቃል/መሪ ዓ.ነገር፣ (ለ) ዘርዛሪ/ደጋፊ ዓ.ነገሮች፣ (ሐ) መደምደሚያ ዓ.ነገር። ምሳሌ፦ 'Haatha go77a' (የውኃ ጥቅም)።
- **English:** 1. Paragraph Composition (Daassa Xaafo, pp. 21–22): Topic sentence, supporting sentences, and concluding sentence, illustrated with a model paragraph on 'Haatha Go77a' (The Importance of Water).
- **Gofatho Latin:** 2. Cachcha Malaatata (7 Punctuation Marks, pp. 24–25): (1) Selle cachchi (.), (2) Nam77u cachche (:), (3) Oysha malaata (?), (4) Malaalo malaata (!), (5) Gartha malaata (“ ”), (6) Shaaho malaata (,), (7) Heedzu cachchi (...). Malla: 'Zentti shaafay Oyda Dalshshape keyidi...'
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 2። ጨችቻ መለአተተ (7 ፑንጭቱአቲኦን መርክስ፣ ፕፕ። 24–25)፦ (1) ሴልሌ ጨችቺ (።)፣ (2) ነምዕዑ ጨችቼ (፦)፣ (3) ኦይሻ መለአተ (?)፣ (4) መለአሎ መለአተ (!)፣ (5) ገርꬃ መለአተ (“ ”)፣ (6) ሻአሆ መለአተ (፣)፣ (7) ሄእꬑ ጨችቺ (።።።)። መልለ፦ 'ዜንትቲ ሻአፈይ ኦይደ ደልሽሻፔ ኬዪዲ።።።'
- **Amharic:** 2. ሰባቱ ሥርዓተ-ነጥቦች (ገጽ 24–25)፦ አራት ነጥብ (.)፣ ሁለት ነጥብ (:)፣ የጥያቄ ምልክት (?)፣ ትዕምርተ አንክሮ (!)፣ ትዕምርተ ጥቅስ (“ ”)፣ ነጠላ ሰረዝ (,) እና ሦስት ነጥብ (...)።
- **English:** 2. Seven Punctuation Marks (pp. 24–25): Period, Colon, Question mark, Exclamation mark, Quotation marks, Comma, and Ellipsis.

#### E. Proverbs, Idioms, Riddles & Oral Literature (`Leemiso / Tosse / Aaqeta / Sedhemenkke / Qan77ishe`)
| Category | Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Malaalo Oda / Blessing Idiom (p. 25) | **Ne aayiya nena yelo!** | ኔ አአዪየ ኔነ ዬሎ! | እናትህ ትውለድህ! (የአድናቆትና የምርቃት አገላለጽ) | May your mother give birth to you again! (Traditional Gofatho exclamation of admiration and praise). |

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze Heedza (3.3 VI Dabbotho, p. 21): Match terms: 1. Medheta imota -> G. Miniraale; 2. Boohoo -> F. Biitta giddoppe keso; 5. Haathenne lambba -> B. Gukkiya miniraaleta; 6. Paabirikka -> C. Miishata murutisiya eqota; 7. Worqqa, biraanne h.h.m -> D. Mino miniraaleta.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ ሄእꬓ (3።3 ቪ ደብቦꬆ፣ ፕ። 21)፦ መትች ቴርምስ፦ 1። ሜꬌተ ኢሞተ -> ግ። ሚኒረአሌ፤ 2። ቦኦሆኦ -> ፍ። ቢኢትተ ጊድዶፕፔ ኬሶ፤ 5። ሀአꬄንኔ ለምብበ -> ብ። ጉክኪየ ሚኒረአሌተ፤ 6። ፐአቢሪክከ -> ጭ። ሚኢሻተ ሙሩቲሲየ እቆተ፤ 7። ዎርቅቀ፣ ቢረአንኔ ህ።ህ።ም -> ድ። ሚኖ ሚኒረአሌተ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 3 (ማዛመድ፣ ገጽ 21)፦ 1. የተፈጥሮ ስጦታ -> ማዕድን፤ 2. ቁፋሮ -> ከመሬት ውስጥ ማውጣት፤ 5. ውኃና ነዳጅ -> ፈሳሽ ማዕድናት፤ 6. ፋብሪካ -> ዕቃዎችን የሚያመርት ተቋም፤ 7. ወርቅና ብር -> ጠጣር ማዕድናት።
- **English:** Exercise 3 (Matching, p. 21): 1. Natural gift -> Mineral; 2. Mining -> Extracting from underground; 5. Water & kerosene -> Liquid minerals; 6. Factory -> Manufacturing facility; 7. Gold & silver -> Solid minerals.

### Gr9_Ch4 — Shemppo 4: Medhi-Darsso | ሼምፕፖ 4፦ ሜꬊ-ደርስሶ | ምዕራፍ 4፦ ልቦለድ ድርሰት ('ዳሮታ') | Chapter 4: Creative Fiction ('Darota') and Independent vs. Dependent Clauses
- **Pages:** Book pp. 23–31 (PDF pp. 27–35) • **Source:** [Grade 9 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1uvGaBT4-O_Jn7oQbuNl1W88lFBFiHULU/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Darota (Adarsso: G. Yirdaw)* (ደሮተ (አደርስሶ፦ ግ። ዪርደው)) — ዳሮታ (አጭር ልቦለድ በጂ. ይርዳው) / Darota (Short Story by Gifo Yirdaw about Daanna Bonqqo, Mishire Keehanna, and Their Son Darota) (pp. 29–31 (Book pp. 25–27))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Daanna Bonqqora Mishire Keehannira issi tammu laythatappe sinthe Hottolo Qoore geetettiya bessan Darota giya na7a yelidi de7iya goshshanchcha. | ደአንነ ቦንቅቆረ ሚሺሬ ኬእሀንኒረ ኢስሲ ተምሙ ለይꬃተፕፔ ሲንꬄ ሆትቶሎ ቆኦሬ ጌእቴትቲየ ቤስሰን ደሮተ ጊየ ነዓ ዬሊዲ ዴዒየ ጎሽሻንችቻ። | አቶ ቦንቆ እና ወ/ሮ ኬሃና ከአሥር ዓመታት በፊት ሆቶሎ ቆሬ በሚባል ቦታ ዳሮታ የሚባል ልጅ ወልደው የሚኖሩ አርሶ አደሮች ናቸው። | Daanna Bonqqo and Mishire Keehanna are farmers living in Hottolo Qoore who ten years ago had their only child, Darota, in their old age. |
| Daroti yelettoodeppe ichchashu laytha gaso ifili dhayidi hargge machcho gidis; Darota geetetti sunthettidaykka harggey darida gishossa. | ደሮቲ ዬሌትቶኦዴፕፔ ኢችቻሹ ለይꬃ ገሶ ኢፊሊ ꬋዪዲ ሀርግጌ መችቾ ጊዲስ፤ ደሮተ ጌእቴትቲ ሱንꬄትቲደይክከ ሀርግጌይ ደሪደ ጊሾስሰ። | ዳሮታ ከተወለደበት ጊዜ አንስቶ እስከ አምስት ዓመቱ ድረስ ጤና አጥቶ የበሽታ ጥገኛ ሆነ፤ ዳሮታ ተብሎ የተሰየመውም በሽታ ስለበዛበት ነው። | From birth until age five, Darota suffered from recurring fevers, asthma/wheezing, and measles; he was named 'Darota' ('abundance') because illnesses multiplied upon him. |
| He wode away aadhdhidi, deren kuyi me77ishin Darota tohotinne kusheti wuxarkketeyssappe, bollaykka misha xuuggeyssape leppishe leppishe bis; daanna Bonqqoranne mishiree Keehannira medhdha Xoossa galatishe aqidosona. | ሄ ዎዴ አወይ አአꬍꬊዲ፣ ዴሬን ኩዪ ሜዕዒሺን ደሮተ ቶሆቲንኔ ኩሼቲ ዉጠርክኬቴይስሰፕፔ፣ ቦልለይክከ ሚሻ ጡኡግጌይስሰፔ ሌፕፒሼ ሌፕፒሼ ቢስ፤ ደአንነ ቦንቅቆረንኔ ሚሺሬእ ኬእሀንኒረ ሜꬍꬋ ጦኦስሰ ገለቲሼ አቂዶሶነ። | በዚያን ጊዜ ፀሐይ አዘቅዝቃ ጥላ ሲረዝም የዳሮታ እግርና እጅ መንፈራገጥና የሰውነቱ ትኩሳት እየቀነሰ ሄደ፤ አቶ ቦንቆና ወ/ሮ ኬሃናም ፈጣሪ አምላክን እያመሰገኑ አደሩ። | As the afternoon shadows lengthened, Darota's convulsions and burning fever subsided, and his parents Bonqqo and Keehanna spent the night thanking God the Creator as the boy recovered and grew. |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Dialogue inside 'Darota' (pp. 30–31): Mishiree Keehanna: 'Hay godaw… Ta new ay iissadinnaa? Hayana ne maarota yeddarkki. Ne pathiko… Xoosso ne keethas oothidi de7ana mela new magana na7a giddo.' Daanna Bonqqo: 'Nuus wolqqay daaburishin, ayfey ixxishin, nuna heemana nu ushachaa ne Xoossa keethas immana geyssi taw gelibenna. Ha na7aay diccikko nuus mixi tikkidi nu ayfeppe buuxo denthanay iya era.'
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ዲአሎጉእ ኢንሲዴ 'ደሮተ' (ፕፕ። 30–31)፦ ሚሺሬእ ኬእሀንነ፦ 'ሀይ ጎደው… ተ ኔው አይ ኢኢስሰዲንነአ? ሀየነ ኔ መአሮተ ዬድደርክኪ። ኔ ፐꬂኮ… ጦኦስሶ ኔ ኬእꬃስ ኦኦꬂዲ ዴዓነ ሜለ ኔው መገነ ነዓ ጊድዶ።' ደአንነ ቦንቅቆ፦ 'ኑኡስ ዎልቅቀይ ደአቡሪሺን፣ አይፌይ ኢጥጢሺን፣ ኑነ ሄእመነ ኑ ኡሻቻአ ኔ ጦኦስሰ ኬእꬃስ ኢምመነ ጌይስሲ ተው ጌሊቤንነ። ሀ ነዓአይ ዲጭጪክኮ ኑኡስ ሚጢ ቲክኪዲ ኑ አይፌፕፔ ቡኡጦ ዴንꬃነይ ኢየ እረ።'
- **Amharic:** በ'ዳሮታ' ልቦለድ ውስጥ ያለው የወ/ሮ ኬሃና እና የአቶ ቦንቆ ምልልስ (ገጽ 30–31)፦ ወ/ሮ ኬሃና ልጇ ከዳነ ለእግዚአብሔር ቤት አገልጋይ እንዲሆን ስትሳል፣ አቶ ቦንቆ ደግሞ በእርጅናችን እንጨት ለቅሞና ውኃ ቀድቶ የሚጦረንን አንድ ልጃችንን እንዴት ትሰጪያለሽ ብሎ ይከራከራታል።
- **English:** Dialogue in 'Darota' (pp. 30–31): Weeping over her feverish child, Keehanna vows to dedicate him to serve God's house if healed, while Bonqqo protests that in their old age they need their only son to gather wood and draw water for them.

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **besode** | ቤሶዴ | `N` | bessenne wode | መቼት (ታሪኩ የተፈጸመበት ቦታና ጊዜ) | setting (place 'besse' + time 'wode') |
| **odaawa** | ኦደአወ | `N` | adarssanchchi medhdhida ase | ገጸ-ባህሪ (ደራሲው የፈጠረው ሰው) | character in fiction/drama |
| **meqethi** | ሜቄꬂ | `N` | gaasonne muruuta | ሴራ (የታሪኩ ምክንያትና ውጤት ትስስር) | plot (lit. bone/skeleton of the narrative) |
| **dom77e** | ዶምዕዔ | `N` | qofa zokkotho | ግጭት (የሐሳብ ወይም የድርጊት ፍጭት) | conflict |
| **ayfe qofa** | አይፌ ቆፈ | `NP` | adarssuwa bertte kiita | ጭብጥ / ዋና መልእክት | central theme / core message |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Pacce Kiita Meega Qofata (Clauses within Compound & Complex Sentences, pp. 33–35): (1) Pana danda7idi eqqiya pacce kiita meega qofa (Independent clause: 'Dichchi pa kaduwa massis'); (2) Pana danda7idi eqqonna pacce kiita meega qofa (Dependent converb/causal clause: 'Daroti putota tokkidinne haatha ushshidi...').
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ፐጭጬ ኪኢተ ሜእገ ቆፈተ (ጭለኡሴስ ዊꬂን ጮምፖኡንድ & ጮምፕሌጥ ሴንቴንጬስ፣ ፕፕ። 33–35)፦ (1) ፐነ ደንደዒዲ እቅቂየ ፐጭጬ ኪኢተ ሜእገ ቆፈ (ኢንዴፔንዴንት ጭለኡሴ፦ 'ዲችቺ ፐ ከዱወ መስሲስ')፤ (2) ፐነ ደንደዒዲ እቅቆንነ ፐጭጬ ኪኢተ ሜእገ ቆፈ (ዴፔንዴንት ጮንቬርብ/ጨኡሰል ጭለኡሴ፦ 'ደሮቲ ፑቶተ ቶክኪዲንኔ ሀአꬃ ኡሽሺዲ።።።')።
- **Amharic:** ንዑሳን ዓረፍተ ነገሮች (ገጽ 33–35)፦ (1) ራሱን ችሎ የሚቆም (ነጻ) ዓ.ነገር (Pana danda7idi eqqiya)፤ (2) ራሱን ችሎ የማይቆም (ጥገኛ) ዓ.ነገር (Pana danda7idi eqqonna)።
- **English:** Independent vs. Dependent Clauses (pp. 33–35): Independent clauses end in a finite verb (e.g., massis, pee7is, qopees), whereas dependent clauses end in converbs (-idi, -ada) or subordinators (gidida gisho).

#### E. Proverbs, Idioms, Riddles & Oral Literature (`Leemiso / Tosse / Aaqeta / Sedhemenkke / Qan77ishe`)
| Category | Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Aaqeta (Idiom in 'Darota', p. 30) | **Hargge machcho gidis.** | ሀርግጌ መችቾ ጊዲስ። | የበሽታ ጥገኛ (የበሽታ ባለቤት) ሆነ። | Became a constant companion ('spouse') of illness. |
| Aaqeta (Idiom in 'Darota', p. 30) | **Bollay layti uttis.** | ቦልለይ ለይቲ ኡትቲስ። | ሰውነቱ ሟሸሸ / ከሳ። | His body wasted away and became frail from repeated illness. |

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze Heedza (4.3 Nabbabo, pp. 31–32): 1. Darota giya medh-darssuwaas besodey awee? -> Zaaro: Hottolo Qoore (bessa), tammu laythatappe sinthe (wode). 2. Odaawati oonanttee? -> Zaaro: Daanna Bonqqo, Mishire Keehanna, nne Darota.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ ሄእꬓ (4።3 ነብበቦ፣ ፕፕ። 31–32)፦ 1። ደሮተ ጊየ ሜꬍ-ደርስሱወአስ ቤሶዴይ አዌእ? -> ዘአሮ፦ ሆትቶሎ ቆኦሬ (ቤስሰ)፣ ተምሙ ለይꬃተፕፔ ሲንꬄ (ዎዴ)። 2። ኦደአወቲ ኦኦነንትቴእ? -> ዘአሮ፦ ደአንነ ቦንቅቆ፣ ሚሺሬ ኬእሀንነ፣ ንኔ ደሮተ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 3 (ምንባብ፣ ገጽ 31–32)፦ 1. የ'ዳሮታ' ልቦለድ መቼት የትና መቼ ነው? -> መልስ፦ ሆቶሎ ቆሬ፣ ከአሥር ዓመት በፊት። 2. ገጸ-ባህሪያቱ እነማን ናቸው? -> መልስ፦ አቶ ቦንቆ፣ ወ/ሮ ኬሃና እና ዳሮታ።
- **English:** Exercise 3 (Reading Comprehension, pp. 31–32): 1. What is the setting of 'Darota'? -> Hottolo Qoore, ten years ago. 2. Who are the characters? -> Daanna Bonqqo, Mishire Keehanna, and Darota.

### Gr9_Ch5 — Shemppo 5: Bila-Oda | ሼምፕፖ 5፦ ቢለ-ኦደ | ምዕራፍ 5፦ ሥነ-ቃል (ተረት፣ እንቆቅልሽ፣ ምሳሌያዊ ንግግር፣ ሙገሳ/ፉከራ፣ ቃንጪሼ እና የልጆች ጨዋታ) | Chapter 5: Gofatho Oral Literature (Folktales, Riddles, Proverbs, Heroic Praise Chants, Qan77ishe, and Children's Songs)
- **Pages:** Book pp. 32–46 (PDF pp. 36–50) • **Source:** [Grade 9 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1uvGaBT4-O_Jn7oQbuNl1W88lFBFiHULU/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Bila-Oda Shaahota: Gaammora nne Ecerera (Hayse), Sedhemenkke, Tosse, Saba, Qan77ishe & Nayta Yethi* (ቢለ-ኦደ ሻአሆተ፦ ገአምሞረ ንኔ እጬሬረ (ሀይሴ)፣ ሴꬌሜንክኬ፣ ቶስሴ፣ ሰበ፣ ቀንዕዒሼ & ነይተ ዬꬂ) — የጎፋ ሥነ-ቃል ዘውጎች፦ አንበሳውና አይጧ (ተረት)፣ እንቆቅልሽ፣ ምሳሌያዊ ንግግሮች፣ ሙገሳና የልጆች ጨዋታ / Genres of Gofatho Oral Literature: The Lion and the Mouse (Folktale), Riddles, Proverbs, Praise Chants, and Children's Songs (pp. 36–48 (Book pp. 32–44))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Bila-odi daro dalgga allale gidishin nam7u gita shaahotan wothanaw danda7ettees: Yeda bila-oda (Hayse, Sedhemenkke, Taarike odo) nne Ginxxo bila-oda (Tosse, Saba, Qan77ishe, Nayta yethata). | ቢለ-ኦዲ ደሮ ደልግገ አልለሌ ጊዲሺን ነምዑ ጊተ ሻአሆተን ዎꬃነው ደንደዔትቴእስ፦ ዬደ ቢለ-ኦደ (ሀይሴ፣ ሴꬌሜንክኬ፣ ተአሪኬ ኦዶ) ንኔ ጊንጥጦ ቢለ-ኦደ (ቶስሴ፣ ሰበ፣ ቀንዕዒሼ፣ ነይተ ዬꬃተ)። | ሥነ-ቃል እጅግ ሰፊ ዘርፍ ሲሆን በሁለት ዋና ክፍሎች ይመደባል፦ የዝርው ሥነ-ቃል (ተረት፣ እንቆቅልሽ፣ ታሪካዊ ትውፊት) እና የግጥም ሥነ-ቃል (ምሳሌያዊ ንግግር፣ ሙገሳ፣ ቃንጪሼ፣ የልጆች ጨዋታ)። | Oral literature is divided into two major branches: Prose Oral Literature (Yeda bila-oda) and Poetic Oral Literature (Ginxxo bila-oda). |
| Odanchchay: 'Haysey hanees!' -> Hayzanchati: 'Harey qooxees!' ... Gorddo heera gakkishe: 'Haysey hayse gakko!' | ኦደንችቻይ፦ 'ሀይሴይ ሀኔእስ!' -> ሀይዘንቻቲ፦ 'ሀሬይ ቆኦጤእስ!' ።።። ጎርድዶ ሄእረ ገክኪሼ፦ 'ሀይሴይ ሀይሴ ገክኮ!' | ተረት ነጋሪው፦ 'ተረት ተረት (Haysey hanees)!' -> አድማጮች፦ 'አህያ ያንቀላፋል (Harey qooxees)!' ... በመጨረሻም፦ 'ተረቱ ይፈጸም (Haysey hayse gakko)!' | Traditional Gofatho Folktale Opening & Closing Formula: Storyteller: 'Haysey hanees!' ('A tale happens!') -> Audience: 'Harey qooxees!' ('The donkey dozes!') ... Closing: 'Haysey hayse gakko!'. |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Sedhemenkke Shiisho Hanota & Asa Paydo Sedhemenkke (pp. 40–42): Keetha aaway = Adde kokonjje; Keetha aayiya = Indda ikikiilo; Attuma na7a = Na7a zalle; Macca na7a = Na7a piijjo. Zaaranchay gam77ishin: 'Ta na7a dulley shuuqqees!'
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሴꬌሜንክኬ ሺኢሾ ሀኖተ & አሰ ፐይዶ ሴꬌሜንክኬ (ፕፕ። 40–42)፦ ኬእꬃ አአወይ = አድዴ ኮኮንጅጄ፤ ኬእꬃ አአዪየ = ኢንድደ ኢኪኪኢሎ፤ አትቱመ ነዓ = ነዓ ዘልሌ፤ መጭጨ ነዓ = ነዓ ፒኢጅጆ። ዘአረንቻይ ገምዕዒሺን፦ 'ተ ነዓ ዱልሌይ ሹኡቅቄእስ!'
- **Amharic:** የቤተሰብ ቁጥር እንቆቅልሽ (ገጽ 40–42)፦ የቤት አባት = Adde kokonjje፤ የቤት እናት = Indda ikikiilo፤ ወንድ ልጅ = Na7a zalle፤ ሴት ልጅ = Na7a piijjo። መላሹ ሲዘገይ፦ 'Ta na7a dulley shuuqqees!' ይባላል።
- **English:** Household-Counting Riddle Protocol (pp. 40–42): Uses symbolic code names for father (Adde kokonjje), mother (Indda ikikiilo), son (Na7a zalle), and daughter (Na7a piijjo) so the respondent identifies which neighbor's family matches that exact count.

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **bila-oda** | ቢለ-ኦደ | `NP` | doonara yeletappe yeletakko aadhdhiya oda | ሥነ-ቃል / ቃላዊ ትውፊት | oral literature / folklore |
| **hayse** | ሀይሴ | `N` | bila taarike | ተረት | folktale / fable |
| **sedhemenkke** | ሴꬌሜንክኬ | `N` | xuura oysha | እንቆቅልሽ | traditional riddle |
| **tosse** | ቶስሴ | `N` | leemiso | ምሳሌያዊ ንግግር / ተረትና ምሳሌ | proverb / wise saying (leemiso) |
| **saba** | ሰበ | `N` | nashsho yethe / gereerssa | ሙገሳ / ቀረርቶ / ፉከራ | heroic praise chant / elegiac or celebratory panegyric |
| **qan77ishe** | ቀንዕዒሼ | `N` | ginxxo kaassa | ቃንጪሼ (የግጥም ምልልስ ጨዋታ) | rhyming poetic dueling game |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Bila-Oda Medhuwa & Kanddota (Oral Poetics, Alliteration & Parallelism, pp. 43–48): (1) Hayse 3-part structure: Gelo (Opening formula), Gomppa (Body), Kuuyethi (Closing formula); (2) Qan77ishe phonological matching (soo shochchi / alliteration & end-rhyme).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ቢለ-ኦደ ሜꬉወ & ከንድዶተ (ኦረል ፖእቲጭስ፣ አልሊቴረቲኦን & ፐረልሌሊስም፣ ፕፕ። 43–48)፦ (1) ሀይሴ 3-ፐርት ስትሩጭቱሬ፦ ጌሎ (ኦፔኒንግ ፎርሙለ)፣ ጎምፕፐ (ቦድይ)፣ ኩኡዬꬂ (ጭሎሲንግ ፎርሙለ)፤ (2) ቀንዕዒሼ ፆኖሎጊጨል መትቺንግ (ሶኦ ሾችቺ / አልሊቴረቲኦን & እንድ-ርህይሜ)።
- **Amharic:** የሥነ-ቃል መዋቅርና ባህሪያት (ገጽ 43–48)፦ (1) የተረት ሦስት ክፍሎች፦ መግቢያ (Gelo)፣ ሐተታ (Gomppa) እና መደምደሚያ (Kuuyethi)፤ (2) የቃንጪሼ የድምፅ ምጣኔና ቤት መምታት (Soo shochchi)።
- **English:** Structural Grammar of Oral Literature (pp. 43–48): Tripartite narrative structure of folktales (Gelo, Gomppa, Kuuyethi) and phonological parallelism/rhyme (soo shochchi) in Qan77ishe and Saba.

#### E. Proverbs, Idioms, Riddles & Oral Literature (`Leemiso / Tosse / Aaqeta / Sedhemenkke / Qan77ishe`)
| Category | Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Tosse (Proverb, p. 44) | **Aawa paray wothenna, na7a paray kessenna.** | አአወ ፐረይ ዎꬄንነ፣ ነዓ ፐረይ ኬስሴንነ። | የአባት ፈረስ አያወርድም፣ የልጅ ፈረስ አያወጣም። | The father's horse does not carry one down, nor the son's horse up (each person must rely on their own effort). |
| Tosse (Proverb, p. 44) | **Baana iray baanara bukkees.** | በአነ ኢረይ በአነረ ቡክኬእስ። | የሚያልፍ ዝናብ ሲሄድ ይዘንባል። | Passing rain pours as it moves on. |
| Tosse (Proverb, p. 44) | **Utetha daroy zerethi coonchees.** | ኡቴꬃ ደሮይ ዜሬꬂ ጮኦንቼእስ። | የመቀመጥ ብዛት የዘር እህል ያስጨርሳል። | Sitting idle too long consumes the seed grain. |
| Tosse (Proverb, p. 44) | **Kalisana kathay keren erettees.** | ከሊሰነ ከꬃይ ኬሬን እሬትቴእስ። | የሚያጠግብ እህል በወፍጮ ላይ ይታወቃል። | Grain that will satisfy is known right on the grinding stone. |
| Tosse (Proverb, p. 44) | **Oge adussi shemppo adussees.** | ኦጌ አዱስሲ ሼምፕፖ አዱስሴእስ። | ረጅም መንገድ ዕድሜ (ነፍስ) ያረዝማል። | Taking the long, safe road prolongs life. |
| Tosse (Proverb, p. 44) | **Biitti si7o gin derey si7ees.** | ቢኢትቲ ሲዖ ጊን ዴሬይ ሲዔእስ። | ምድር ጸጥ ስትል አገር ይሰማል። | When the land is quiet, the whole community hears. |
| Tosse (Proverb, p. 44) | **Keethan keehin karen santhi aathees.** | ኬእꬃን ኬእሂን ከሬን ሰንꬂ አአꬄእስ። | በቤት ውስጥ ቸር ሆኖ በውጭ ጎመን ያሳልፋል። | Generous inside the house, sharing greens outside. |
| Tosse (Proverb, p. 44) | **Melana haathi kana mees.** | ሜለነ ሀአꬂ ከነ ሜእስ። | ሊደርቅ ያለ ውኃ ውሻ ይበላል (ይወስዳል)። | A river about to dry up sweeps away a dog. |
| Tosse (Proverb, p. 44) | **Dure duufoy uutha giddo.** | ዱሬ ዱኡፎይ ኡኡꬃ ጊድዶ። | የሀብታም መቃብር በእንሰት ተክል መካከል ነው። | A rich person's grave is amidst the flourishing enset garden. |
| Tosse (Proverb, p. 44) | **Maathiw ixxanaa miiziya maatiw ixxawsu.** | መአꬂው ኢጥጠነአ ሚኢዚየ መአቲው ኢጥጠውሱ። | ወተት ልትከለክል ያለች ላም ሣሩንም ትጠላለች። | A cow that refuses to give milk also refuses the grass. |
| Tosse (Proverb, p. 44) | **Hayqqin agganaas daaburin yeekkees.** | ሀይቅቂን አግገነአስ ደአቡሪን ዬእክኬእስ። | ሲሞት ለመተው ሲደክም (ሲታመም) ያለቅሳል። | Weeping when someone is merely tired/ill, only to abandon them when they die. |
| Sedhemenkke (Riddles, p. 39) | **Sedhemenkke (p. 39): (1) Gadhi kalo, gezzey kalo, doolloy kosha -> Kazhe (ant/waist); (2) Bukkishin eexees; awishin to7ees -> Wol7anthi (lightning); (3) Ba7iday xugga; tucciday gola -> Badalla (maize cob); (4) Tilil gida dembban issi ayfe atare -> Awa ayfe (the sun).** | ሴꬌሜንክኬ (ፕ። 39)፦ (1) ገꬊ ከሎ፣ ጌዝዜይ ከሎ፣ ዶኦልሎይ ኮሻ -> ከዤ (አንት/ወኢስት)፤ (2) ቡክኪሺን እእጤእስ፤ አዊሺን ቶዔእስ -> ዎልዓንꬂ (ሊግህትኒንግ)፤ (3) በዒደይ ጡግገ፤ ቱጭጪደይ ጎለ -> በደልለ (መኢዜ ጮብ)፤ (4) ቲሊል ጊደ ዴምብበን ኢስሲ አይፌ አተሬ -> አወ አይፌ (ꬄ ሱን)። | እንቆቅልሾች (ገጽ 39)፦ (1) ቆላው ጥጋብ፣ ደጋው ጥጋብ፣ ወይና ደጋው ረሃብ -> ጉንዳን (Kazhe)፤ (2) ሲዘንብ ይነዳል፣ ፀሐይ ሲወጣ ይጠፋል -> መብረቅ (Wol7anthi)፤ (3) በቆሎ (Badalla)፤ (4) በጠራ ሜዳ ላይ አንዲት አተር -> ፀሐይ (Awa ayfe)። | Sedhemenkke Riddles (p. 39): (1) Full at bottom and top, thin in the middle -> Ant (Kazhe); (2) Burns when raining, goes out when sunny -> Lightning (Wol7anthi); (3) Maize cob (Badalla); (4) A single pea on a clear plain -> The sun (Awa ayfe). |
| Saba (Praise Chants, pp. 45–46) | **Saba (Heroic Praise Chants, pp. 45–46): (1) Yeehon shiiqiya saba ('Caliga cokki goyiya sangga godaw...'); (2) Kawo Gaammonaa Amaado Zaala Sabishe ('Haathe oychin maathi immiya, Mari oychin maathi woytiya...'); (3) Arbba naa Tuuthe Bulqqo Sabbishe ('Atare bangga Tophphiyaas gidisiya...').** | ሰበ (ሄሮኢጭ ፕረኢሴ ቻንትስ፣ ፕፕ። 45–46)፦ (1) ዬእሆን ሺኢቂየ ሰበ ('ጨሊገ ጮክኪ ጎዪየ ሰንግገ ጎደው።።።')፤ (2) ከዎ ገአምሞነአ አመአዶ ዘአለ ሰቢሼ ('ሀአꬄ ኦይቺን መአꬂ ኢምሚየ፣ መሪ ኦይቺን መአꬂ ዎይቲየ።።።')፤ (3) አርብበ ነአ ቱኡꬄ ቡልቅቆ ሰብቢሼ ('አተሬ በንግገ ቶጵጪየአስ ጊዲሲየ።።።')። | ሙገሳ/ፉከራ (Saba፣ ገጽ 45–46)፦ (1) የለቅሶ ሙገሳ፤ (2) የንጉሥ ጋሞና አማዶ ዛላ ሙገሳ ('ውኃ ሲጠይቁት ወተት የሚሰጥ...')፤ (3) የአርባ ልጅ ቱቴ ቡልቆ ሙገሳ ('አተርና ገብስ ለኢትዮጵያ የሚያበቃ...')። | Saba Heroic Praise Chants (pp. 45–46): (1) Funerary elegy chant; (2) Praise of King Gaammona Amaado Zaala ('Who gives milk when asked for water'); (3) Praise of Tuuthe Bulqqo son of Arbba. |

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze Isso (Dabbotho, p. 43): 1. Hayse odaawata -> G. asenne do7a; 3. Hayse dooyo -> I. haysey hanees; 5. Sedhemenkken attuma na7a -> B. na7a zalle; 6. Yallo ma7a -> D. amaato.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ ኢስሶ (ደብቦꬆ፣ ፕ። 43)፦ 1። ሀይሴ ኦደአወተ -> ግ። አሴንኔ ዶዓ፤ 3። ሀይሴ ዶኦዮ -> ኢ። ሀይሴይ ሀኔእስ፤ 5። ሴꬌሜንክኬን አትቱመ ነዓ -> ብ። ነዓ ዘልሌ፤ 6። የልሎ መዓ -> ድ። አመአቶ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 1 (ማዛመድ፣ ገጽ 43)፦ 1. የተረት ገጸ-ባህሪያት -> ሰውና እንስሳት፤ 3. የተረት መክፈቻ -> haysey hanees፤ 5. በእንቆቅልሽ ወንድ ልጅ -> na7a zalle፤ 6. Yallo ma7a -> እርግማን/ውግዘት።
- **English:** Exercise 1 (Oral Literature Matching, p. 43): 1. Folktale characters -> Humans & animals; 3. Folktale opening -> haysey hanees; 5. Son in counting riddle -> na7a zalle; 6. Yallo ma7a -> ritual admonishment.
- **Gofatho Latin:** Shemppo 5 Kuushsha Oyshata (Multiple Choice, pp. 49–50): 1. Kaalliya bila oda qommotappe ginxxo kanddoy de7eyssi awssee? -> A. saba. 2. Sedhemenkken zaaranchchay zaaros wodiya aathishin [ta na7a dulley shuuqqees] gees.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሼምፕፖ 5 ኩኡሽሻ ኦይሻተ (ሙልቲፕሌ ቾኢጬ፣ ፕፕ። 49–50)፦ 1። ከአልሊየ ቢለ ኦደ ቆምሞተፕፔ ጊንጥጦ ከንድዶይ ዴዔይስሲ አውስሴእ? -> አ። ሰበ። 2። ሴꬌሜንክኬን ዘአረንችቻይ ዘአሮስ ዎዲየ አአꬂሺን [ተ ነዓ ዱልሌይ ሹኡቅቄእስ] ጌእስ።
- **Amharic:** የምዕራፍ 5 ማጠቃለያ ጥያቄዎች (ገጽ 49–50)፦ 1. የግጥም ባህሪ ያለው የሥነ-ቃል ዓይነት -> A. ሙገሳ (Saba)። 2. በእንቆቅልሽ መላሹ ሲዘገይ የሚባለው -> 'Ta na7a dulley shuuqqees'።
- **English:** Chapter 5 Review Questions (pp. 49–50): 1. Which oral literature genre has poetic form? -> A. Saba. 2. What does the riddle challenger say when the respondent delays? -> 'Ta na7a dulley shuuqqees'.

### Gr9_Ch6 — Shemppo 6: Ginxxo | ሼምፕፖ 6፦ ጊንጥጦ | ምዕራፍ 6፦ ግጥም (የግጥም ምንነት፣ አላባዊያንና 'አትኮርጅ' ግጥም) | Chapter 6: Poetry (Poetic Meter, Stanza Structure, and the Poem 'Duuqqopa')
- **Pages:** Book pp. 47–53 (PDF pp. 51–57) • **Source:** [Grade 9 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1uvGaBT4-O_Jn7oQbuNl1W88lFBFiHULU/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Ginxxo Aybatetha, Ginxxo Yarata & 'Duuqqopa' (Ginxxo: G. Yirdaw nne G. Isiyaas)* (ጊንጥጦ አይበቴꬃ፣ ጊንጥጦ የረተ & 'ዱኡቅቆፐ' (ጊንጥጦ፦ ግ። ዪርደው ንኔ ግ። ኢሲየአስ)) — የግጥም ምንነት፣ አላባዊያንና 'አትኮርጅ (Duuqqopa)' ግጥም / Poetics and the 8-Stanza Didactic Poem 'Duuqqopa' ('Do Not Cheat/Plagiarize' by G. Yirdaw & G. Isiyaas) (pp. 51–54 (Book pp. 47–50))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Ginxxoy qorettida qaalan dalgga qofi qanthara mal77o hanotan shiiqiya bila xuufe. | ጊንጥጦይ ቆሬትቲደ ቀአለን ደልግገ ቆፊ ቀንꬃረ መልዕዖ ሀኖተን ሺኢቂየ ቢለ ጡኡፌ። | ግጥም በተመረጡ ቃላት ሰፊ ሐሳብ በአጭሩና በጣፋጭ ሁኔታ የሚቀርብበት የሥነ-ጽሑፍ ዘውግ ነው። | Poetry (Ginxxo) is a literary art form in which expansive ideas are presented concisely and melodiously through carefully chosen words. |
| Duuqqopa (Kure 1): 'Xana77appe attin asaba duuqqopa, / Asaba wuttada wontto yeellatoppa, / Shaakkonna sal77ada coobaas xoonettoppa, / Nenan ammanetta haratan katoppa.' | ዱኡቅቆፐ (ኩሬ 1)፦ 'ጠነዕዓፕፔ አትቲን አሰበ ዱኡቅቆፐ፣ / አሰበ ዉትተደ ዎንትቶ ዬእልለቶፕፐ፣ / ሻአክኮንነ ሰልዕዓደ ጮኦበአስ ጦኦኔትቶፕፐ፣ / ኔነን አምመኔትተ ሀረተን ከቶፕፐ።' | 'አትኮርጅ' (አንጓ 1)፦ 'ከማጥናት በቀር የሰው አትኮርጅ፣ / የሰው ገልብጠህ ነገ አትፈር፣ / ሳትለይ አግበስብሰህ ለከንቱ ነገር አትሸነፍ፣ / በራስህ ተማመን በሌሎች አትደገፍ።' | 'Do Not Cheat' (Stanza 1): 'Study diligently rather than copying others' work; do not copy others and bring shame upon yourself tomorrow; believe in yourself and do not lean on others.' |
| Duuqqopa (Kure 8): 'Minni xana7iday araata demmanaa, / Ayso soo gakkikko ubbata allanaa, / Wobbaato billidi wogidi kanthana, / Oothikko odikko wuri amin77anaa, / Haggaazaan alidi anjjishe simmanaa.' | ዱኡቅቆፐ (ኩሬ 8)፦ 'ሚንኒ ጠነዒደይ አረአተ ዴምመነአ፣ / አይሶ ሶኦ ገክኪክኮ ኡብበተ አልለነአ፣ / ዎብበአቶ ቢልሊዲ ዎጊዲ ከንꬃነ፣ / ኦኦꬂክኮ ኦዲክኮ ዉሪ አሚንዕዓነአ፣ / ሀግገአዘአን አሊዲ አንጅጂሼ ሲምመነአ።' | 'አትኮርጅ' (አንጓ 8)፦ 'ጠንክሮ ያጠና ክብር (ዙፋን) ያገኛል፣ / በአመራር ቦታ ሲደርስ ሁሉንም ያስደስታል፣ / የተጣመመውን አቃንቶ በሕግ ያሻግራል፣ / በአገልግሎቱ ከብሮ እየተመረቀ ይመለሳል።' | 'Do Not Cheat' (Stanza 8): 'One who studies diligently attains honor; when reaching leadership, they serve all fairly, resolve disputes justly, and earn the community's blessing.' |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze Nam77a (6.2 Haasaya, p. 55): 'Duuqqopa' giya ginxxuwa eedo naagidi kifile tamaaretas nabbabite; duuqoy (exam cheating) tamaare bolla, aakime bolla, nne daanna bolla gathiya qohota tobbite.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ ነምዕዓ (6።2 ሀአሰየ፣ ፕ። 55)፦ 'ዱኡቅቆፐ' ጊየ ጊንጥጡወ እእዶ ነአጊዲ ኪፊሌ ተመአሬተስ ነብበቢቴ፤ ዱኡቆይ (እጠም ቼአቲንግ) ተመአሬ ቦልለ፣ አአኪሜ ቦልለ፣ ንኔ ደአንነ ቦልለ ገꬂየ ቆሆተ ቶብቢቴ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 2 (6.2 ንግግር፣ ገጽ 55)፦ 'Duuqqopa' (አትኮርጅ) የሚለውን ግጥም ዜማውን ጠብቃችሁ አንብቡ፤ ኩረጃ በተማሪ፣ በሐኪምና በዳኛ ላይ የሚያስከትለውን ጉዳት ተወያዩ።
- **English:** Exercise 2 (6.2 Oral Recitation & Discussion, p. 55): Recite the poem 'Duuqqopa' with proper poetic rhythm and discuss the harm of academic cheating across professions (students, doctors, judges).

| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |

| **keso (ginxxon)** | ኬሶ (ጊንጥጦን) | `N` | issi shemppon xeesettiya kooshe | ቀለም (በግጥም ውስጥ በአንድ ትንፋሽ የሚጠራ ድምፅ/ክፍለ-ቃል) | poetic syllable / metrical unit |
| **tura (ginxxon)** | ቱረ (ጊንጥጦን) | `N` | issi ziras bagga | ሐረግ (የስንኝ ግማሽ ክፍል) | hemistich / half-line of verse |
| **zira** | ዚረ | `N` | ginxxos issi yafara | ስንኝ (የግጥም አንድ መስመር) | single line of verse |
| **kure** | ኩሬ | `N` | zirata shiiqo | አንጓ (አንድ ዋና ሐሳብ የያዘ የስንኞች ስብስብ) | stanza |
| **mixe** | ሚጤ | `N` | kesota paydo ginatethi | ምጣኔ (የቀለማት ቁጥር እኩልነት — ምሳሌ 6/6) | metrical syllable count (e.g., 6/6 syllables per hemistich) |
| **eedo** | እእዶ | `N` | yethaada haniya mal77o kooshe | ዜማ / ምት (ግጥም ሲነበብ የሚሰማው ጣፋጭ ድምፅ) | poetic rhythm / melody |
| **polo / dinxxo** | ፖሎ / ዲንጥጦ | `N` | wurssetha misatiya kooshe | ቤት መምታት / መድረሻ ድምፅ | end-rhyme / stanzaic rhyme scheme |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Ginxxo Yarata & 6/6 Metrical Scansion (pp. 52–53): Malla: 'Timirttiyan minnidi / salora paallanaa' -> Scanned as /Ti-mirt-tiyan min-ni-di/ (6 keso) + /sa-lo-ra paal-la-na/ (6 keso) = 6/6 metrical line (Zira) composed of two 6-syllable hemistichs (Tura).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ጊንጥጦ የረተ & 6/6 ሜትሪጨል ስጨንሲኦን (ፕፕ። 52–53)፦ መልለ፦ 'ቲሚርትቲየን ሚንኒዲ / ሰሎረ ፐአልለነአ' -> ስጨንኔድ አስ /ቲ-ሚርት-ቲየን ሚን-ኒ-ዲ/ (6 ኬሶ) + /ሰ-ሎ-ረ ፐአል-ለ-ነ/ (6 ኬሶ) = 6/6 ሜትሪጨል ሊኔ (ዚረ) ጮምፖሴድ ኦፍ ትዎ 6-ስይልለብሌ ሄሚስቲችስ (ቱረ)።
- **Amharic:** የግጥም ምጣኔና አወቃቀር (ገጽ 52–53)፦ ምሳሌ፦ '/Ti-mirt-tiyan min-ni-di/ (6 ቀለም) + /sa-lo-ra paal-la-na/ (6 ቀለም)' = በ6/6 ቀለም የተመጠነ ስንኝ (Zira)።
- **English:** Poetic Meter and Scansion (pp. 52–53): Demonstrates 6/6 syllabic meter (Mixe) dividing a 12-syllable line (Zira) into two 6-syllable hemistichs (Tura).

#### E. Proverbs, Idioms, Riddles & Oral Literature (`Leemiso / Tosse / Aaqeta / Sedhemenkke / Qan77ishe`)
| Category | Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Tosse (Proverb on Poetic Conciseness, p. 51) | **Odi darin haren caanettenna.** | ኦዲ ደሪን ሀሬን ጨአኔትቴንነ። | ወሬ ቢበዛ በአህያ አይጫንም። | Too much talk cannot be loaded onto a donkey (concise speech is better than endless chatter). |

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze Heedza (6.3 Nabbabo, pp. 55–56): 1. Ha ginxxuwaas ('Duuqqopa') aappun kurey de7ii? -> Zaaro: 8 kure (8 stanzas). 3. Ginxxoy aappun keson mixettidee? -> Zaaro: 6/6 (12) keson mixettis.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ ሄእꬓ (6።3 ነብበቦ፣ ፕፕ። 55–56)፦ 1። ሀ ጊንጥጡወአስ ('ዱኡቅቆፐ') አአፕፑን ኩሬይ ዴዒኢ? -> ዘአሮ፦ 8 ኩሬ (8 ስተንዘስ)። 3። ጊንጥጦይ አአፕፑን ኬሶን ሚጤትቲዴእ? -> ዘአሮ፦ 6/6 (12) ኬሶን ሚጤትቲስ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 3 (ምንባብ፣ ገጽ 55–56)፦ 1. 'Duuqqopa' ግጥም ስንት አንጓ (kure) አለው? -> መልስ፦ 8 አንጓ። 3. በስንት ቀለም (keso) ተመጠነ? -> መልስ፦ 6/6 ቀለም።
- **English:** Exercise 3 (Poem Analysis, pp. 55–56): 1. How many stanzas (kure) does 'Duuqqopa' have? -> 8 stanzas. 3. What is its syllabic meter (mixe)? -> 6/6 syllables.

### Gr9_Ch7 — Shemppo 7: Kovide-19 Kooroona Vayresse Hargge | ሼምፕፖ 7፦ ኮቪዴ-19 ኮኦሮኦነ ቨይሬስሴ ሀርግጌ | ምዕራፍ 7፦ ኮभिድ-19 የኮሮና ቫይረስ በሽታና የጎፋኛ ሥርዓተ-ጽሕፈት ሕግጋት | Chapter 7: COVID-19 Coronavirus Pandemic, Gofatho Phonotactic Orthography (Ustta/Guttuma), and Bound Suffixes
- **Pages:** Book pp. 54–64 (PDF pp. 58–68) • **Source:** [Grade 9 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1uvGaBT4-O_Jn7oQbuNl1W88lFBFiHULU/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Koroona Vayrese Hargge (KOVIDE-19)* (ኮሮኦነ ቨይሬሴ ሀርግጌ (ኮቪዴ-19)) — የኮሮና ቫይረስ በሽታ (ኮቪድ-19) / Coronavirus Disease (COVID-19): Origin, Symptoms, Impact, and Prevention (pp. 59–61 (Book pp. 55–57))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Koroona vayrese hargge (KOVIDE-19) geetettidi erettiya vayrese harggey kare gade laytha qoodaan 2019 wurssethan Chayna Yohan kataman koyro wode beettida gado hargge. | ኮሮኦነ ቨይሬሴ ሀርግጌ (ኮቪዴ-19) ጌእቴትቲዲ እሬትቲየ ቨይሬሴ ሀርግጌይ ከሬ ገዴ ለይꬃ ቆኦደአን 2019 ዉርስሴꬃን ቻይነ ዮሀን ከተመን ኮይሮ ዎዴ ቤእትቲደ ገዶ ሀርግጌ። | ኮቪድ-19 ተብሎ የሚታወቀው የኮሮና ቫይረስ በሽታ በውጭ አቆጣጠር በ2019 መጨረሻ በቻይና ዉሃን ከተማ ለመጀመሪያ ጊዜ የተገኘ ወረርሽኝ ነው። | The coronavirus disease known as COVID-19 is a pandemic first identified at the end of 2019 in Wuhan, China. |
| Ha harggey oykkida asa bolla gita qoxonne misha, shemppanaw un77etho, mela zode qufenne qoodhe giddo saho malaatati beettoosona. | ሀ ሀርግጌይ ኦይክኪደ አሰ ቦልለ ጊተ ቆጦንኔ ሚሻ፣ ሼምፕፐነው ኡንዕዔꬆ፣ ሜለ ዞዴ ቁፌንኔ ቆኦꬌ ጊድዶ ሰሆ መለአተቲ ቤእትቶኦሶነ። | ይህ በሽታ በያዘው ሰው ላይ ከፍተኛ ትኩሳት፣ ለመተንፈስ መቸገር፣ ደረቅ ሳልና የጉሮሮ ሕመም ምልክቶች ይታያሉ። | Symptoms in infected patients include high fever, difficulty breathing, dry cough, and sore throat, progressing to affect the lungs (goofina) and kidneys (kilaho). |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze Nam77a (7.2 Haasaya Palama, p. 61): Debate between two motions with evidence: (1) 'Koorona vayresse harggey asa na7a aadho qohees' vs. (2) 'Eedise (ECHI-AY-VI) harggey qohan aadhdhees.'
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ ነምዕዓ (7።2 ሀአሰየ ፐለመ፣ ፕ። 61)፦ ዴበቴ ቤትዌእን ትዎ ሞቲኦንስ ዊꬅ እቪዴንጬ፦ (1) 'ኮኦሮነ ቨይሬስሴ ሀርግጌይ አሰ ነዓ አአꬎ ቆሄእስ' ቭስ። (2) 'እእዲሴ (እቺ-አይ-ቪ) ሀርግጌይ ቆሀን አአꬍꬌእስ።'
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 2 (7.2 የክርክር ርዕሶች፣ ገጽ 61)፦ (1) 'የኮሮና ቫይረስ በሽታ የሰውን ልጅ የበለጠ ይጎዳል' እና (2) 'ኤች.አይ.ቪ/ኤድስ በጉዳት ይበልጣል' በሚሉት ሐሳቦች ላይ በማስረጃ ተከራከሩ።
- **English:** Exercise 2 (7.2 Structured Classroom Debate, p. 61): Debate with evidence whether COVID-19 or HIV/AIDS has caused greater harm to humanity.

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **ustta (U)** | ኡስትተ (ኡ) | `N` | ustta pidale | አናባቢ ፊደል (a, e, i, o, u / aa, ee, ii, oo, uu) | vowel phoneme/letter |
| **guttuma (G)** | ጉትቱመ (ግ) | `N` | guttuma pidale | ተነባቢ ፊደል | consonant phoneme/letter |
| **gilootaley (7)** | ጊሎኦተሌይ (7) | `N` | 7 malaata | ግሎታል ድምፅ (7 = /ʔ/) | glottal stop ('7' = /ʔ/) separating dissimilar vowels |
| **goofina** | ጎኦፊነ | `N` | shemppiya asho yara | ሳምባ | lung(s) |
| **kilaho** | ኪለሆ | `N` | asho gaatha yara | ኩላሊት | kidney |
| **doona gultte** | ዶኦነ ጉልትቴ | `NP` | doonanne siidhe kammiya | የአፍና አፍንጫ መሸፈኛ ጭምብል (ማስክ) | face mask |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** 1. Goofatho Doona Xaafo Hiillatanne Pidaleta Qaala Giddon Wogara Xaafo Maara (6 Core Orthographic Rules & Zara 5 CV Table, pp. 63–65): Ustta = U (vowel), Guttuma = G (consonant). Rules: (1) Two different vowels (usttati) never cluster at word onset; (2) Consonants (guttumati) never geminate at word onset (*GGUG is illegal); (3) When two different vowels meet word-medially, glottal stop '7' (gilootaley) is inserted (e.g., sa7a, na7a, ma7o); (4) Doubled consonants medial-internally are pronounced geminated (minni nabbabettoosona); (5) Doubled vowels are pronounced long (aduqqi nabbabettoosona); (6) Digraph consonants (waatha guttumati: ch, dh, dz, ny, ph, sh, th, zh) represent a single phoneme. Zara 5 patterns: U (i, ee), GU (ba, ta, ne), GUGU (mata, kathi, qofa), GUGUGGU (timirtte, atarqqa), UGGUGUGU (asttamaare), GUGUU (nam77a, gam77a).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 1። ጎኦፈꬆ ዶኦነ ጠአፎ ሂኢልለተንኔ ፒደሌተ ቀአለ ጊድዶን ዎገረ ጠአፎ መአረ (6 ጮሬ ኦርꬆግረጪጭ ሩሌስ & ዘረ 5 ጭቭ ተብሌ፣ ፕፕ። 63–65)፦ ኡስትተ = ኡ (ቮዌል)፣ ጉትቱመ = ግ (ጮንሶነንት)። ሩሌስ፦ (1) ትዎ ዲፍፌሬንት ቮዌልስ (ኡስትተቲ) ኔቬር ጭሉስቴር አት ዎርድ ኦንሴት፤ (2) ጮንሶነንትስ (ጉትቱመቲ) ኔቬር ጌሚነቴ አት ዎርድ ኦንሴት (*ግጉግ ኢስ ኢልሌገል)፤ (3) ውሄን ትዎ ዲፍፌሬንት ቮዌልስ ሜእት ዎርድ-ሜዲአልልይ፣ ግሎትተል ስቶፕ '7' (ጊሎኦተሌይ) ኢስ ኢንሴርቴድ (እ።ግ።፣ ሰዓ፣ ነዓ፣ መዖ)፤ (4) ዶኡብሌድ ጮንሶነንትስ ሜዲአል-ኢንቴርነልልይ አሬ ፕሮኖኡንጬድ ጌሚነቴድ (ሚንኒ ነብበቤትቶኦሶነ)፤ (5) ዶኡብሌድ ቮዌልስ አሬ ፕሮኖኡንጬድ ሎንግ (አዱቅቂ ነብበቤትቶኦሶነ)፤ (6) ዲግረጵ ጮንሶነንትስ (ወአꬃ ጉትቱመቲ፦ ች፣ ꬍ፣ ꬕ፣ ኝ፣ ጵ፣ ሽ፣ ꬅ፣ ዥ) ሬፕሬሴንት አ ሲንግሌ ፆኔሜ። ዘረ 5 ፐትቴርንስ፦ ኡ (ኢ፣ እእ)፣ ጉ (በ፣ ተ፣ ኔ)፣ ጉጉ (መተ፣ ከꬂ፣ ቆፈ)፣ ጉጉግጉ (ቲሚርትቴ፣ አተርቅቀ)፣ ኡግጉጉጉ (አስትተመአሬ)፣ ጉጉኡ (ነምዕዓ፣ ገምዕዓ)።
- **Amharic:** 1. የጎፋኛ ቋንቋ የፊደላት አጻጻፍ ሕግጋት (Ustta = አናባቢ፣ Guttuma = ተነባቢ፣ ገጽ 63–65)፦ (1) ሁለት የተለያዩ አናባቢዎች በቃል መነሻ ላይ አይደረቡም፤ (2) ተነባቢዎች በቃል መነሻ ላይ አይጠብቁም (*GGUG አይፈቀድም)፤ (3) ሁለት አናባቢዎች በቃል መካከል ሲገናኙ ግሎታል '7' ይገባል (sa7a, na7a, ma7o)፤ (4) ተነባቢዎች በቃል ውስጥ ሲደረቡ ጠብቀው ይነበባሉ፤ (5) አናባቢዎች ሲደረቡ ተስበው (ረዝመው) ይነበባሉ፤ (6) ጥምር ተነባቢዎች (ch, dh, dz, ph, sh, th, zh) አንድ ድምፅ ይወክላሉ።
- **English:** 1. Six Fundamental Gofatho Orthographic & Phonotactic Rules (Zara 5, pp. 63–65): Formalizes CV syllable templates (Ustta 'U' = Vowel, Guttuma 'G' = Consonant), bans initial consonant clusters/geminates (*GGUG) and initial vowel hiatus (*UUGU), mandates glottal stop '7' between medial vowels (sa7a, na7a, ma7o), and defines consonant gemination and vowel length.
- **Gofatho Latin:** 2. Dhanthetetha & Sigetho/Gasetho Suffixation (pp. 66–68): Attaching bound case/postpositional suffixes (-ppe, -ra, -n, -s, -tethi, -ncha) directly to stems (Malla: Sawlappe, goshshanchcha, medheta = medho).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 2። ꬋንꬄቴꬃ & ሲጌꬆ/ገሴꬆ ሱፍፊጠቲኦን (ፕፕ። 66–68)፦ አትተቺንግ ቦኡንድ ጨሴ/ፖስትፖሲቲኦነል ሱፍፊጤስ (-ፕፔ፣ -ረ፣ -ን፣ -ስ፣ -ቴꬂ፣ -ንቻ) ዲሬጭትልይ ቶ ስቴምስ (መልለ፦ ሰውለፕፔ፣ ጎሽሻንችቻ፣ ሜꬌተ = ሜꬎ)።
- **Amharic:** 2. የቅጥያዎች (Dhanthetetha) እና የመስተዋድድ/አያያዥ አጠቃቀም (ገጽ 66–68)፦ ቅጥያዎችን (-ppe, -ra, -n, -s, -ncha) ከዋናው ቃል ጋር አያይዞ መጻፍ (ምሳሌ፦ Sawlappe, goshshanchcha)።
- **English:** 2. Bound Derivational and Inflectional Suffixation (pp. 66–68): Attaching case and agentive suffixes (-ppe, -ra, -n, -ncha) to nominal and verbal stems.

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze Ichchasha (7.5 III Dabbotho, p. 65): Match words to CV (Ustta/Guttuma) templates: 1. Kuraacho -> C. GUGUGU; 2. Gado -> D. GUGU; 3. Aafo -> B. UGU; 4. Meeccis -> E. GUGUG; 5. Urqqa -> A. UGGU.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ ኢችቻሻ (7።5 ኢኢኢ ደብቦꬆ፣ ፕ። 65)፦ መትች ዎርድስ ቶ ጭቭ (ኡስትተ/ጉትቱመ) ቴምፕለቴስ፦ 1። ኩረአቾ -> ጭ። ጉጉጉ፤ 2። ገዶ -> ድ። ጉጉ፤ 3። አአፎ -> ብ። ኡጉ፤ 4። ሜእጭጪስ -> እ። ጉጉግ፤ 5። ኡርቅቀ -> አ። ኡግጉ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 5 (የአናባቢና ተነባቢ ቅርጽ ማዛመድ፣ ገጽ 65)፦ 1. Kuraacho -> GUGUGU፤ 2. Gado -> GUGU፤ 3. Aafo -> UGU፤ 4. Meeccis -> GUGUG፤ 5. Urqqa -> UGGU።
- **English:** Exercise 5 (Phonotactic Template Matching, p. 65): 1. Kuraacho -> GUGUGU; 2. Gado -> GUGU; 3. Aafo -> UGU; 4. Meeccis -> GUGUG; 5. Urqqa -> UGGU.

### Gr9_Ch8 — Shemppo 8: Diraama | ሼምፕፖ 8፦ ዲረአመ | ምዕራፍ 8፦ ድራማ (የድራማ አላባዊያን፣ ዓይነቶችና የትምህርትና ሀብት ክርክር ተውኔት) | Chapter 8: Drama (Elements, Comedy vs. Tragedy, and the Play of Worqqa, Ciisha, and Pola)
- **Pages:** Book pp. 65–71 (PDF pp. 69–75) • **Source:** [Grade 9 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1uvGaBT4-O_Jn7oQbuNl1W88lFBFiHULU/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Diraama Aybatetha, Yarata, Qommota & Diraama Kaassa (Worqqa, Ciisha & Pola)* (ዲረአመ አይበቴꬃ፣ የረተ፣ ቆምሞተ & ዲረአመ ከአስሰ (ዎርቅቀ፣ ጪኢሻ & ፖለ)) — የድራማ ምንነትና በትምህርትና በሀብት ላይ የተደረገ የተውኔት ምልልስ (ወርቃ፣ ጪሻ እና ፖላ) / Dramatic Theory and the Student Play 'Aadhdhey Timirtte yee Shalo?' ('Which Is Superior: Education or Wealth?') (pp. 69–75 (Book pp. 65–71))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Diraama giya qaalay 'Giriike' qaala gidishin birshshethaykka 'ooson beso' woykko 'qaaxo' guussi. | ዲረአመ ጊየ ቀአለይ 'ጊሪኢኬ' ቀአለ ጊዲሺን ቢርሽሼꬃይክከ 'ኦኦሶን ቤሶ' ዎይክኮ 'ቀአጦ' ጉኡስሲ። | ድራማ የሚለው ቃል የግሪክ ቃል ሲሆን ትርጉሙም 'በተግባር ማሳየት' ወይም 'ድርጊት/እንቅስቃሴ' ማለት ነው። | The word 'drama' comes from Greek and means 'showing in action' (ooson beso) or 'movement/performance' (qaaxo). |
| Worqqa: 'Shaloy beettees, dhayees, haaroynne shaloy danon woy kayson meetettees; timirtte eri neera merinaw de7ees. Hessa gisho shaloy timirtte gindde yedhdhenna.' | ዎርቅቀ፦ 'ሻሎይ ቤእትቴእስ፣ ꬋዬእስ፣ ሀአሮይንኔ ሻሎይ ደኖን ዎይ ከይሶን ሜእቴትቴእስ፤ ቲሚርትቴ እሪ ኔእረ ሜሪነው ዴዔእስ። ሄስሰ ጊሾ ሻሎይ ቲሚርትቴ ጊንድዴ ዬꬍꬌንነ።' | ወርቃ፦ 'ሀብት ይገኛል፣ ይጠፋል፣ ከብትና ንብረት በአደጋ ወይም በሌባ ይወሰዳል፤ የትምህርት ዕውቀት ግን ከአንተ ጋር ለዘላለም ይኖራል። ስለዚህ ሀብት የትምህርትን ተረከዝ አይረግጥም (አይወዳደርም)።' | Worqqa: 'Wealth is found and lost, and livestock and property can be taken by disaster or thieves, whereas knowledge from education stays with you forever; therefore wealth cannot even touch the heel of education.' |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Full Classroom Drama Dialogue between Pola, Worqqa, and Ciisha (pp. 72–75): Pola asks whether education (timirtte) or wealth (shalo) is greater. Worqqa and Ciisha prove that even clean piped water at school to wash hands against COVID-19 and discovering gold/silver come from education, and Worqqa recites the poem 'Timirttey al77obaa / Azallin beettenna...'. Ciisha concludes: 'Kaysoy demmonna, danoy bochenna merinaa shaluwa timirtte eraa shiisha.'
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ፉልል ጭለስስሮኦም ድረመ ዲአሎጉእ ቤትዌእን ፖለ፣ ዎርቅቀ፣ አንድ ጪኢሻ (ፕፕ። 72–75)፦ ፖለ አስክስ ውሄꬄር እዱጨቲኦን (ቲሚርትቴ) ኦር ዌአልꬅ (ሻሎ) ኢስ ግሬአቴር። ዎርቅቀ አንድ ጪኢሻ ፕሮቬ ꬃት እቬን ጭሌአን ፒፔድ ወቴር አት ስቾኦል ቶ ወሽ ሀንድስ አገኢንስት ጮቪድ-19 አንድ ዲስጮቬሪንግ ጎልድ/ሲልቬር ጮሜ ፍሮም እዱጨቲኦን፣ አንድ ዎርቅቀ ሬጪቴስ ꬄ ፖእም 'ቲሚርትቴይ አልዕዖበአ / አዘልሊን ቤእትቴንነ።።።'። ጪኢሻ ጮንጭሉዴስ፦ 'ከይሶይ ዴምሞንነ፣ ደኖይ ቦቼንነ ሜሪነአ ሻሉወ ቲሚርትቴ እረአ ሺኢሻ።'
- **Amharic:** የፖላ፣ የወርቃ እና የጪሻ የተውኔት ምልልስ (ገጽ 72–75)፦ ፖላ ትምህርት ይበልጣል ወይስ ሀብት ብሎ ሲጠይቅ፣ ወርቃና ጪሻ ትምህርት ከሁሉ እንደሚበልጥ በማስረጃና በግጥም ('Timirttey al77obaa...') ያስረዱታል። ጪሻም 'ሌባ የማያገኘውን፣ አደጋ የማይነካውን የዘላለም ሀብት የትምህርት ዕውቀትን ሰብስብ' ብሎ ይመክረዋል።
- **English:** Three-Character Drama Dialogue (Pola, Worqqa, and Ciisha, pp. 72–75): Debating whether education or material wealth is superior, incorporating COVID-19 handwashing awareness and an embedded 9-line poem praising education.

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **iskkiriptte** | ኢስክኪሪፕትቴ | `N` | xaafettida diraama xuufe | ስክሪፕት (የተጻፈ የድራማ ጽሑፍ) | dramatic script |
| **bertte odaawata** | ቤርትቴ ኦደአወተ | `NP` | taarikiyaan polo gakkiya odaawata | ዋና ገጸ-ባህሪያት (ታሪኩን ከመጀመሪያ እስከ መጨረሻ የሚያሻግሩ) | protagonists / major characters |
| **sima odaawata** | ሲመ ኦደአወተ | `NP` | taariken beettidi dhayiya odaawata | ንዑስ (አጃቢ) ገጸ-ባህሪያት | minor / supporting characters |
| **komeede diraama** | ኮሜእዴ ዲረአመ | `NP` | miicisiya diraama | ኮሜዲ ድራማ (አዝናaniና አስቂኝ ተውኔት) | comedy drama |
| **tiraajeede diraama** | ቲረአጄእዴ ዲረአመ | `NP` | kaha un77ethiyanne kayoyiya diraama | ትራጄዲ ድራማ (ከፍተኛ ግጭትና ሐዘን ያለው ተውኔት) | tragedy drama |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Diraama Xuufe Maara (Dramatic Script Conventions & Stage Ethics, pp. 70–72): Speaker names followed by colon (:), direct first/second-person dialogue, stage movement (qaaxo), and audience etiquette (no phone calls or side chatter during a performance).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ዲረአመ ጡኡፌ መአረ (ድረመቲጭ ስጭሪፕት ጮንቬንቲኦንስ & ስተጌ እꬂጭስ፣ ፕፕ። 70–72)፦ ስፔአኬር ነሜስ ፎልሎዌድ ብይ ጮሎን (፦)፣ ዲሬጭት ፊርስት/ሴጮንድ-ፔርሶን ዲአሎጉእ፣ ስተጌ ሞቬሜንት (ቀአጦ)፣ አንድ አኡዲእንጬ እቲቁእትቴ (ኖ ፆኔ ጨልልስ ኦር ሲዴ ቻትቴር ዱሪንግ አ ፔርፎርመንጬ)።
- **Amharic:** የድራማ ጽሑፍ አጻጻፍና የመድረክ ሥነ-ምግባር (ገጽ 70–72)፦ የገጸ-ባህሪያት ስምና ሁለት ነጥብ (:)፣ የገጸ-ባህሪያት ንግግርና እንቅስቃሴ (qaaxo) እንዲሁም የተመልካቾች ሥነ-ምግባር።
- **English:** Dramatic Script Conventions and Audience Etiquette (pp. 70–72): Formatting character turns, stage actions (qaaxo), and theater etiquette.

#### E. Proverbs, Idioms, Riddles & Oral Literature (`Leemiso / Tosse / Aaqeta / Sedhemenkke / Qan77ishe`)
| Category | Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Tosse (Proverb in Drama, p. 73) | **Erashe oychay, xeellashe sholay.** | እረሼ ኦይቻይ፣ ጤእልለሼ ሾለይ። | እያወቅክ ትጠይቃለህ፣ እያየህ ትሸikማለህ። | You ask what you already know, and stumble while looking straight ahead. |
| Aaqeta / Proverbial Idiom (p. 73) | **Shaloy timirtte gindde yedhdhenna.** | ሻሎይ ቲሚርትቴ ጊንድዴ ዬꬍꬌንነ። | ሀብት የትምህርትን ተረከዝ አይረግጥም (ከትምህርት ጋር አይወዳደርም)። | Material wealth cannot even step on the heel of education (cannot compare to education). |
| Tosse (Proverb in Drama, p. 74) | **Wonttara maxida kuttiw argganthi denthenna.** | ዎንትተረ መጢደ ኩትቲው አርግገንꬂ ዴንꬄንነ። | በማለዳ የለቀመችውን ዶሮ ጭልፊት አያነሣትም። | The hen that gathers her grain early in the morning is not snatched by the hawk (early diligence protects one's future). |

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze Isso (II Dabbotho, p. 72): 1. Diraama -> D. Ooson beso; 2. Diraama xaafanchcha -> G. Adarsanchcha; 3. Komeede diraama -> H. Miicisiya diraama; 4. Sima odaawa -> E. Taariken beettidi dhayiya odaawata; 6. Diraami shiiqiya bessa -> A. Detha; 7. Ayfe qofa -> C. Kiita; 8. Bertte odaawata -> B. Taarikiyaan polo gakkiya odaawata.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ ኢስሶ (ኢኢ ደብቦꬆ፣ ፕ። 72)፦ 1። ዲረአመ -> ድ። ኦኦሶን ቤሶ፤ 2። ዲረአመ ጠአፈንችቻ -> ግ። አደርሰንችቻ፤ 3። ኮሜእዴ ዲረአመ -> ህ። ሚኢጪሲየ ዲረአመ፤ 4። ሲመ ኦደአወ -> እ። ተአሪኬን ቤእትቲዲ ꬋዪየ ኦደአወተ፤ 6። ዲረአሚ ሺኢቂየ ቤስሰ -> አ። ዴꬃ፤ 7። አይፌ ቆፈ -> ጭ። ኪኢተ፤ 8። ቤርትቴ ኦደአወተ -> ብ። ተአሪኪየአን ፖሎ ገክኪየ ኦደአወተ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 1 (ማዛመድ፣ ገጽ 72)፦ 1. ድራማ -> በተግባር ማሳየት፤ 2. የድራማ ጸሐፊ -> ደራሲ፤ 3. ኮሜዲ -> አስቂኝ ድራማ፤ 6. ድራማ የሚቀርብበት ቦታ -> መድረክ (Detha)፤ 7. ጭብጥ -> መልእክት (Kiita)።
- **English:** Exercise 1 (Drama Terminology Matching, p. 72): 1. Diraama -> Ooson beso; 2. Playwright -> Adarsanchcha; 3. Komeede -> Miicisiya diraama; 6. Stage -> Detha; 7. Theme -> Kiita.

### Gr9_Ch9 — Shemppo 9: Miidiyanne Gahetetha | ሼምፕፖ 9፦ ሚኢዲየንኔ ገሄቴꬃ | ምዕራፍ 9፦ ሚዲያና ተግባቦት (መገናኛ ብዙኃን) | Chapter 9: Media and Communication, Report Writing, and Comparative/Contrastive Discourse
- **Pages:** Book pp. 72–79 (PDF pp. 76–83) • **Source:** [Grade 9 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1uvGaBT4-O_Jn7oQbuNl1W88lFBFiHULU/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Miidiyanne Gahetetha* (ሚኢዲየንኔ ገሄቴꬃ) — ሚዲያና ተግባቦት (የመገናኛ ብዙኃን ሚና በኅብረተሰብና በመንግሥት መካከል) / Media and Communication: Print, Broadcast, and Digital Media as a Bridge Between Government and Society (pp. 77–78 (Book pp. 73–74))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Miidiya guussay daro asa giddoyiyaanne markkanddo issi detha akeekan demmanaada maaddiyaba; gahetethi qassi daro asay issoy haraara qofanine markkando laameretetha hanota qonccisees. | ሚኢዲየ ጉኡስሰይ ደሮ አሰ ጊድዶዪየአንኔ መርክከንድዶ ኢስሲ ዴꬃ አኬእከን ዴምመነአደ መአድዲየበ፤ ገሄቴꬂ ቀስሲ ደሮ አሰይ ኢስሶይ ሀረአረ ቆፈኒኔ መርክከንዶ ለአሜሬቴꬃ ሀኖተ ቆንጭጪሴእስ። | ሚዲያ ማለት ብዙ ሕዝብን የሚያገናኝና መረጃን በተመሳሳይ ግንዛቤ እንዲያገኙ የሚረዳ ሲሆን ተግባቦት ደግሞ ሰዎች እርስ በርሳቸው ሐሳብና መረጃ የሚለዋወጡበትን ሁኔታ ይገልጻል። | Media is an institution that connects the public and delivers shared information, while communication describes the mutual exchange of ideas and information. |
| Kuyishin, miidiya giya wode kawotethasinne deretethaas giddon de7iya dhoqqa zooze mela xeelettiyaba gaanaw danda7oos. | ኩዪሺን፣ ሚኢዲየ ጊየ ዎዴ ከዎቴꬃሲንኔ ዴሬቴꬃአስ ጊድዶን ዴዒየ ꬎቅቀ ዞኦዜ ሜለ ጤእሌትቲየበ ገአነው ደንደዖኦስ። | ሲጠቃለል ሚዲያ በመንግሥትና በሕዝብ መካከል ያለ ታላቅ ድልድይ (dhoqqa zooze) ተደርጎ ሊወሰድ ይችላል። | In summary, media serves as a vital bridge (dhoqqa zooze) connecting the government and the public. |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze Nam77a (9.2 Haasayo, p. 78): Prepare and deliver oral radio/TV announcements on: 1. Issi woga ma7o sikkiyaa keetha eriso (Traditional clothing tailoring shop ad); 2. Timirtte keetha dooyetethaanne doometha eriso (School opening announcement); 3. Maxaafe bayziya keethanne imettiya haggaazo (Bookstore service announcement).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ ነምዕዓ (9።2 ሀአሰዮ፣ ፕ። 78)፦ ፕሬፐሬ አንድ ዴሊቬር ኦረል ረዲኦ/ትቭ አንኖኡንጬሜንትስ ኦን፦ 1። ኢስሲ ዎገ መዖ ሲክኪየአ ኬእꬃ እሪሶ (ትረዲቲኦነል ጭሎꬂንግ ተኢሎሪንግ ሾፕ አድ)፤ 2። ቲሚርትቴ ኬእꬃ ዶኦዬቴꬃአንኔ ዶኦሜꬃ እሪሶ (ስቾኦል ኦፔኒንግ አንኖኡንጬሜንት)፤ 3። መጠአፌ በይዚየ ኬእꬃንኔ ኢሜትቲየ ሀግገአዞ (ቦኦክስቶሬ ሴርቪጬ አንኖኡንጬሜንት)።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 2 (9.2 ንግግር፣ ገጽ 78)፦ የሚዲያ ማስታወቂያ አዘጋጅቶ በቃል ማቅረብ፦ 1. የባህል ልብስ ስፌት ቤት ማስታወቂያ፤ 2. የትምህርት ቤት መከፈቻ ማስታወቂያ፤ 3. የመጻሕፍት መደብር አገልግሎት ማስታወቂያ።
- **English:** Exercise 2 (9.2 Oral Media Broadcasts, p. 78): Compose and present oral media advertisements/announcements for a traditional tailoring shop, school opening, or bookstore.

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **miidiya** | ሚኢዲየ | `N` | markkanddo aathiya eqota | መገናኛ ብዙኃን (ሚዲያ) | mass media |
| **kabexa** | ከቤጠ | `N` | ooratha oda | ዜና / ትኩስ መረጃ | news / broadcast report |
| **zooze** | ዞኦዜ | `N` | pinthiya oge | ድልድይ | bridge |
| **zeebulssi** | ዜእቡልስሲ | `N` | carkko kokkorssi | የአየር ሞገድ | airwave / radio wave |
| **xolqqethi** | ጦልቅቄꬂ | `N` | zaari beso, odo | ግብረ-መልስ | feedback / response |
| **gaagla** | ገአግለ | `N` | gallasa, ageena, laythi | ቀን (ቀን፣ ወርና ዓመተ ምሕረት) | date (day, month, year) |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** 1. Balakko (Dependent) vs. La77a (Independent) Pace Meega Qofa (p. 80): Malla: 'Biri taw de7iyaako (Balakko / Conditional Dependent), eessi uyana (La77a / Independent).' 'Na7ay wonttappe utethan de7ishin (Balakko), iri puttu gis (La77a).'
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 1። በለክኮ (ዴፔንዴንት) ቭስ። ለዕዓ (ኢንዴፔንዴንት) ፐጬ ሜእገ ቆፈ (ፕ። 80)፦ መልለ፦ 'ቢሪ ተው ዴዒየአኮ (በለክኮ / ጮንዲቲኦነል ዴፔንዴንት)፣ እእስሲ ኡየነ (ለዕዓ / ኢንዴፔንዴንት)።' 'ነዓይ ዎንትተፕፔ ኡቴꬃን ዴዒሺን (በለክኮ)፣ ኢሪ ፑትቱ ጊስ (ለዕዓ)።'
- **Amharic:** 1. ጥገኛ (Balakko) እና ነጻ (La77a) ንዑሳን ዓረፍተ ነገሮች (ገጽ 80)፦ ምሳሌ፦ 'ገንዘብ ቢኖረኝ ኖሮ (ጥገኛ/Balakko)፣ ማር እጠጣ ነበር (ነጻ/La77a)።'
- **English:** 1. Dependent (Balakko) vs. Independent (La77a) Clauses (p. 80): Conditional (-ikko/-iyaako) and simultaneous (-ishin) clauses are bound (balakko) and require a main clause (la77a).
- **Gofatho Latin:** 2. Geeddaro (Comparison of Similarities), Qeeletho (Contrast of Differences) & Aasetho (Combined Comparison & Contrast) (pp. 81–82): Malla on 'Muuze nne Uuthi' (Banana and Enset) showing shared plant traits (Geeddaro) vs. distinct growth cycles and uses (Qeeletho).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 2። ጌእድደሮ (ጮምፐሪሶን ኦፍ ሲሚለሪቲእስ)፣ ቄእሌꬆ (ጮንትረስት ኦፍ ዲፍፌሬንጬስ) & አአሴꬆ (ጮምቢኔድ ጮምፐሪሶን & ጮንትረስት) (ፕፕ። 81–82)፦ መልለ ኦን 'ሙኡዜ ንኔ ኡኡꬂ' (በነነ አንድ እንሴት) ሾዊንግ ሻሬድ ፕለንት ትረኢትስ (ጌእድደሮ) ቭስ። ዲስቲንጭት ግሮውꬅ ጭይጭሌስ አንድ ኡሴስ (ቄእሌꬆ)።
- **Amharic:** 2. ማወዳደር (Geeddaro - ተመሳሳይነትን ማሳየት)፣ ማነጻጸር (Qeeletho - ልዩነትን ማሳየት) እና ማነጻጸር-ማወዳደር (Aasetho) (ገጽ 81–82)፦ ምሳሌ፦ ሙዝና እንሰትን (Muuze nne Uuthi) ማወዳደርና ማነጻጸር።
- **English:** 2. Comparative (Geeddaro), Contrastive (Qeeletho), and Combined Comparative-Contrastive (Aasetho) Writing (pp. 81–82): Illustrated with Banana vs. Enset.

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze Ichchasha (9.5 Qawosa, pp. 80–81): Complete dependent clauses with independent clauses: 1. Tama eethanaw [mithi maxasu]. 2. Wudey simmishin [sa7ay omarssis]. And underline conjunctions in: 2. 'Ta yeleta bessay Sawla shin Tunggan diccas' -> 'shin'.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ ኢችቻሻ (9።5 ቀዎሰ፣ ፕፕ። 80–81)፦ ጮምፕሌቴ ዴፔንዴንት ጭለኡሴስ ዊꬅ ኢንዴፔንዴንት ጭለኡሴስ፦ 1። ተመ እእꬃነው [ሚꬂ መጠሱ]። 2። ዉዴይ ሲምሚሺን [ሰዓይ ኦመርስሲስ]። አንድ ኡንዴርሊኔ ጮንጁንጭቲኦንስ ኢን፦ 2። 'ተ ዬሌተ ቤስሰይ ሰውለ ሺን ቱንግገን ዲጭጨስ' -> 'ሺን'።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 5 (ሰዋስው፣ ገጽ 80–81)፦ ጥገኛ ሐረጎችን በነጻ ዓረፍተ ነገር ማሟላትና አያያዥ ቃላትን ('shin', 'mela') መለየት።
- **English:** Exercise 5 (Clause Completion & Conjunction Identification, pp. 80–81): Complete dependent clauses and identify subordinators/conjunctions ('shin', 'mela', '-ishin').

### Gr10_Ch1 — Shemppo 1: Doonanne Deretethaa | ሼምፕፖ 1፦ ዶኦነንኔ ዴሬቴꬃአ | ምዕራፍ 1፦ ቋንቋና ኅብረተሰብ | Chapter 1: Language and Society, Sign Language Inclusion, and Discourse Connectors
- **Pages:** Book pp. 1–6 (PDF pp. 6–11) • **Source:** [Grade 10 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1mKIcfMoMgnZ9Pa657NWmE-OGND2wlveM/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Doona (Doonanne Deretethaa)* (ዶኦነ (ዶኦነንኔ ዴሬቴꬃአ)) — ቋንቋና ማኅበረሰብ (የቋንቋ እኩልነት፣ የምልክት ቋንቋና የሥነ-ጽሑፍ ዕድገት) / Language and Society: Scholarly Definitions (Baye Yimam), Sign Language (Malla Doona), and Literary Development (pp. 8–9 (Book pp. 3–4))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Dooni asa na7i ubbay paas de7iyaa heera ubban de7iyaa issoy issuwaara qofa, metuwaa, qiiruwaa, amuwaanne h.h.melata qonccisanaw go7ettiya gita miishe. | ዶኦኒ አሰ ነዒ ኡብበይ ፐአስ ዴዒየአ ሄእረ ኡብበን ዴዒየአ ኢስሶይ ኢስሱወአረ ቆፈ፣ ሜቱወአ፣ ቂኢሩወአ፣ አሙወአንኔ ህ።ህ።ሜለተ ቆንጭጪሰነው ጎዔትቲየ ጊተ ሚኢሼ። | ቋንቋ የሰው ልጅ ሁሉ በሚኖርበት አካባቢ እርስ በርሱ ሐሳቡን፣ ችግሩን፣ ቁጭቱንና ምኞቱን ለመግለጽ የሚጠቀምበት ታላቅ መሣሪያ ነው። | Language is the great instrument through which human beings everywhere express their thoughts, problems, regrets, and aspirations to one another. |
| Ha metuwaa birshshanaas haasaya kooshiya si7onna naytas malla doona (sign language) timirttey koshshees. | ሀ ሜቱወአ ቢርሽሻነአስ ሀአሰየ ኮኦሺየ ሲዖንነ ነይተስ መልለ ዶኦነ (ሲግን ለንጉአጌ) ቲሚርትቴይ ኮሽሼእስ። | ይህን ችግር ለመፍታት የመስማት እክል ላለባቸው ልጆች የምልክት ቋንቋ (malla doona) ትምህርት ያስፈልጋል። | To resolve communication barriers for deaf and hard-of-hearing students, sign language (malla doona) education is essential. |
| Amaarathoy Tophphiyan dhoqqu gidi beettiday ' Fikire Iske Mekabir' geetettiya Haaddis Alamaayehu xaafida adussa medh-darsso xaafoy keyidaape guyena. | አመአረꬆይ ቶጵጪየን ꬎቅቁ ጊዲ ቤእትቲደይ ' ፊኪሬ ኢስኬ ሜከቢር' ጌእቴትቲየ ሀአድዲስ አለመአዬሁ ጠአፊደ አዱስሰ ሜꬍ-ደርስሶ ጠአፎይ ኬዪደአፔ ጉዬነ። | አማርኛ በኢትዮጵያ በሥነ-ጽሑፍ ደረጃ ጎልቶ የታየው ሀዲስ ዓለማየሁ የጻፉት 'ፍቅር እስከ መቃብር' የተሰኘው ረጅም ልቦለድ ከወጣ በኋላ ነው። | Amharic literature rose to prominence in Ethiopia following the publication of Haddis Alemayehu's classic novel 'Fikir Eske Mekabir' ('Love unto Crypt'). |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 2 (1.B Haasaya, p. 7): Group discussion on 9 questions: 1. Dooni ubbay gineeye gina gidennee? 4. Goofatho doonan tamaaroy iiteeye lo7oo? 6. Goofatho doonaa dichaas oosettida oosota qonccisa. 8. Ha7i wode Goofatho Dooni dicci gakkida dethaa paattite.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 2 (1።ብ ሀአሰየ፣ ፕ። 7)፦ ግሮኡፕ ዲስጩስሲኦን ኦን 9 ቁእስቲኦንስ፦ 1። ዶኦኒ ኡብበይ ጊኔእዬ ጊነ ጊዴንኔእ? 4። ጎኦፈꬆ ዶኦነን ተመአሮይ ኢኢቴእዬ ሎዖኦ? 6። ጎኦፈꬆ ዶኦነአ ዲቻአስ ኦኦሴትቲደ ኦኦሶተ ቆንጭጪሰ። 8። ሀዒ ዎዴ ጎኦፈꬆ ዶኦኒ ዲጭጪ ገክኪደ ዴꬃአ ፐአትቲቴ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 2 (1.B ንግግር፣ ገጽ 7)፦ በቡድን ውይይት፦ 1. ሁሉም ቋንቋ እኩል ነው ወይስ አይደለም? 4. በጎፋኛ ቋንቋ መማር ጥሩ ነው ወይስ መጥፎ? 6. ለጎፋኛ ቋንቋ ዕድገት የተሠሩ ሥራዎችን አብራሩ።
- **English:** Exercise 2 (1.B Oral Discussion, p. 7): Discuss language equality, the benefits of mother-tongue education in Gofatho, milestones in Gofatho language development, and current status.

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **hiloha** | ሂሎሀ | `N` | dichcha / ooraxxethi | ሥልጣኔ / ዕድገት | civilization / modern advancement |
| **borsso** | ቦርስሶ | `N` | yeella / kawushsho | ውርደት / ኀፍረት | humiliation / shame / disparagement |
| **pilggethi** | ፒልግጌꬂ | `N` | xinaate | ምርምር / ጥናት | research / investigation |
| **qiiro** | ቂኢሮ | `N` | kayo / gaatha saho | ቁጭት / ጸጸት | regret / remorse / sorrow |
| **deretethi** | ዴሬቴꬂ | `N` | dere asa issippetetha | ማኅበረሰብ / ኅብረተሰብ | society / community |
| **malla doona** | መልለ ዶኦነ | `NP` | malaatan haasayiya doona | የምልክት ቋንቋ | sign language |
| **shankka** | ሻንክከ | `N` | wora do7a oyqqo | አደን | hunting |
| **bila** | ቢለ | `N` | hiilla | ጥበብ / ሥነ-ጥበብ | art / verbal artistry |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Gasethota (Discourse Connectors in Context, 1.F Qawosa, p. 11): (1) Hessa gisho ('therefore'), (2) Gidoppe attin ('however/but'), (3) Hessa mala ('likewise/similarly'), (4) Gujjidikka ('furthermore/moreover'), (5) Kaallidi ('subsequently/next').
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ገሴꬆተ (ዲስጮኡርሴ ጮንኔጭቶርስ ኢን ጮንቴጥት፣ 1።ፍ ቀዎሰ፣ ፕ። 11)፦ (1) ሄስሰ ጊሾ ('ꬄሬፎሬ')፣ (2) ጊዶፕፔ አትቲን ('ሆዌቬር/ቡት')፣ (3) ሄስሰ መለ ('ሊኬዊሴ/ሲሚለርልይ')፣ (4) ጉጅጂዲክከ ('ፉርꬄርሞሬ/ሞሬኦቬር')፣ (5) ከአልሊዲ ('ሱብሴቁእንትልይ/ኔጥት')።
- **Amharic:** አያያዥ ቃላት (Gasethota፣ ገጽ 11)፦ (1) Hessa gisho (ስለዚህ)፣ (2) Gidoppe attin (ነገር ግን)፣ (3) Hessa mala (እንደዚሁም)፣ (4) Gujjidikka (በተጨማሪም)፣ (5) Kaallidi (ቀጥሎም)።
- **English:** Discourse Connectors in Context (p. 11): Hessa gisho ('therefore'), Gidoppe attin ('however'), Hessa mala ('likewise'), Gujjidikka ('furthermore'), and Kaallidi ('next').

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 7 (1.F Qawosa Gasetho Kuntho, p. 11): 1. Odali zambbo tamaare; [gidoppe attin] paaciyan ziqqa muruuta demmis. 2. Otoroy loythi nabbabis; [hessa gisho] lo7o bessi gakkis. 3. Ciishshantta soo asay githe bida imathata loythi mokkidosona; nu soo asaykka [hessa mala]. 4. Timirtte tamaaroy daro bonchisees; [gujjidikka] kare gade gaso efees. 5. Macho ekkanaw koyro na7entta soo cima yeddees; [kaallidi] doona pogiso immees.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 7 (1።ፍ ቀዎሰ ገሴꬆ ኩንꬆ፣ ፕ። 11)፦ 1። ኦደሊ ዘምብቦ ተመአሬ፤ [ጊዶፕፔ አትቲን] ፐአጪየን ዚቅቀ ሙሩኡተ ዴምሚስ። 2። ኦቶሮይ ሎይꬂ ነብበቢስ፤ [ሄስሰ ጊሾ] ሎዖ ቤስሲ ገክኪስ። 3። ጪኢሽሻንትተ ሶኦ አሰይ ጊꬄ ቢደ ኢመꬃተ ሎይꬂ ሞክኪዶሶነ፤ ኑ ሶኦ አሰይክከ [ሄስሰ መለ]። 4። ቲሚርትቴ ተመአሮይ ደሮ ቦንቺሴእስ፤ [ጉጅጂዲክከ] ከሬ ገዴ ገሶ እፌእስ። 5። መቾ እክከነው ኮይሮ ነዔንትተ ሶኦ ጪመ ዬድዴእስ፤ [ከአልሊዲ] ዶኦነ ፖጊሶ ኢምሜእስ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 7 (አያያዥ ቃላትን መሙላት፣ ገጽ 11)፦ 1. [gidoppe attin (ነገር ግን)]፤ 2. [hessa gisho (ስለዚህ)]፤ 3. [hessa mala (እንደዚሁም)]፤ 4. [gujjidikka (በተጨማሪም)]፤ 5. [kaallidi (ቀጥሎም)]።
- **English:** Exercise 7 (Discourse Connector Fill-in-the-Blank, p. 11): 1. gidoppe attin ('however'); 2. hessa gisho ('therefore'); 3. hessa mala ('likewise'); 4. gujjidikka ('furthermore'); 5. kaallidi ('next').

### Gr10_Ch2 — Shemppo 2: Gade Wogan Eko Gelo | ሼምፕፖ 2፦ ገዴ ዎገን እኮ ጌሎ | ምዕራፍ 2፦ ባህላዊ የጋብቻ ሥርዓት በጎፋ | Chapter 2: Traditional Gofatho Marriage Customs and Sentence Transformations
- **Pages:** Book pp. 7–13 (PDF pp. 12–18) • **Source:** [Grade 10 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1mKIcfMoMgnZ9Pa657NWmE-OGND2wlveM/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Beni Wode Goofan Eko Gelo Woga* (ቤኒ ዎዴ ጎኦፈን እኮ ጌሎ ዎገ) — በጎፋ የጥንት ባህላዊ የጋብቻ ሥርዓትና ዓይነቶች / Traditional Marriage Customs and Types of Marriage in Gofa (Cima/Laazanthi, Dafi Eko, Woranzo) (pp. 15–16 (Book pp. 10–11))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Asa na7i shempora de7idi susay duuxxonna melanne kochay dhayonna mela beni sisa wodeppe oykkidi ekkishenne gelishe yelishe ha7i gaso biitta bolla daridi de7is. | አሰ ነዒ ሼምፖረ ዴዒዲ ሱሰይ ዱኡጥጦንነ ሜለንኔ ኮቻይ ꬋዮንነ ሜለ ቤኒ ሲሰ ዎዴፕፔ ኦይክኪዲ እክኪሼንኔ ጌሊሼ ዬሊሼ ሀዒ ገሶ ቢኢትተ ቦልለ ደሪዲ ዴዒስ። | የሰው ልጅ በሕይወት ኖሮ ዘሩ እንዳይቋረጥና ትውልዱ እንዳይጠፋ ከጥንት ጊዜ ጀምሮ እያገባና እየተዳረ እየወለደ እስከ አሁን በምድር ላይ በዝቶ ይኖራል። | So that the human lineage does not break, humanity has married and raised generations from ancient times to the present. |
| Kase doona pogoy imettees; yaagano qaray gam77ishe ekettees; saamintti gelos attishin soofey de7ees; wurssethan yaaganoy dimbbakees. | ከሴ ዶኦነ ፖጎይ ኢሜትቴእስ፤ የአገኖ ቀረይ ገምዕዒሼ እኬትቴእስ፤ ሰአሚንትቲ ጌሎስ አትቲሺን ሶኦፌይ ዴዔእስ፤ ዉርስሴꬃን የአገኖይ ዲምብበኬእስ። | መጀመሪያ የቃል ማሰሪያ (doona pogo) ይሰጣል፤ የሠርጉ ቀጠሮ ይቆረጣል፤ ለሠርጉ አንድ ሳምንት ሲቀረው የ'ሶፌ' ሥርዓት ይደረጋል፤ በመጨረሻም ሠርጉ በነጋሪት/ከበሮ ይደምቃል። | First the betrothal gift (doona pogo) is given, the wedding date is set, the pre-wedding market parade (soofe) takes place a week before, and finally the wedding feast (yaagano) is celebrated with drums. |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Full 22-Turn Dialogue between Students Orzaamo and Murqqenna (2.B Haasaya, pp. 13–14): Orzaamo: 'Beni wode eko gelo wogaabaa neeni eray?' Murqqenna: 'Ee, guuthara erays... Aawaara aayeera zorettidi onttako na7iya lo7o; kochayka lo7o gidi cima yeddoosona... Hessa cimi wurssees gees. "Cimi shi7a muzees" giya tossiyaa si7a erikkii?... Na7iyaa puutto oykkada dakko, gaabe, laxana daddawus. Na7ay keethe keexxidi cumees... Soo ekki gakkishin na7aa aawaay xamaata ekkidi... "Yelettite ayfite, na7a na7as biyi na7as gakkite" gidi anjjees.'
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ፉልል 22-ቱርን ዲአሎጉእ ቤትዌእን ስቱዴንትስ ኦርዘአሞ አንድ ሙርቅቄንነ (2።ብ ሀአሰየ፣ ፕፕ። 13–14)፦ ኦርዘአሞ፦ 'ቤኒ ዎዴ እኮ ጌሎ ዎገአበአ ኔእኒ እረይ?' ሙርቅቄንነ፦ 'እእ፣ ጉኡꬃረ እረይስ።።። አአወአረ አአዬእረ ዞሬትቲዲ ኦንትተኮ ነዒየ ሎዖ፤ ኮቻይከ ሎዖ ጊዲ ጪመ ዬድዶኦሶነ።።። ሄስሰ ጪሚ ዉርስሴእስ ጌእስ። "ጪሚ ሺዓ ሙዜእስ" ጊየ ቶስሲየአ ሲዓ እሪክኪኢ?።።። ነዒየአ ፑኡትቶ ኦይክከደ ደክኮ፣ ገአቤ፣ ለጠነ ደድደዉስ። ነዓይ ኬእꬄ ኬእጥጢዲ ጩሜእስ።።። ሶኦ እክኪ ገክኪሺን ነዓአ አአወአይ ጠመአተ እክኪዲ።።። "ዬሌትቲቴ አይፊቴ፣ ነዓ ነዓስ ቢዪ ነዓስ ገክኪቴ" ጊዲ አንጅጄእስ።'
- **Amharic:** በተማሪ ኦርዛሞ እና በተማሪ ሙርቄና መካከል የተደረገ ባለ 22 ተራ ምልልስ (ገጽ 13–14)፦ ስለ ጎፋ ባህላዊ ጋብቻ ሽምግልና፣ ስለ ሙሽሪት ጥጥ መፍተልና የሙሽራው ቤት መሥራትና ተክል መትከል (cumees)፣ ስለ ሠርግ (yaagano) እና የሙሽራው አባት በእርጎ (xamaata) እግራቸውን እያነካካ 'ውለዱ ክበዱ፣ ለልጅ ልጅ ድረሱ' ብሎ ስለሚመርቅበት ሥርዓት።
- **English:** Complete 22-Turn Dialogue between Orzaamo and Murqqenna (pp. 13–14): Detailed conversation covering family lineage vetting, sending elders (laazanthi cima), bride's cotton spinning (qolle/gaabe), groom's house-building and planting (cumo), equestrian wedding procession, and the father's curd-blessing ritual ('Yelettite ayfite').

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **laazanthi** | ለአዘንꬂ | `N` | macho oychiya cima | ሽምግልና / ሴት ለመጠየቅ የሚላክ ሽማግሌ | marriage matchmaker / betrothal envoy |
| **doona pogo** | ዶኦነ ፖጎ | `NP` | caaqo imo | የቃል ማሰሪያ (የመተጫጫ ስጦታ/ቃል ኪዳን) | betrothal pledge / engagement gift |
| **soofe** | ሶኦፌ | `N` | gelanaappe sinthe giya gelidi wallotiyaa hanota | ከሠርግ በፊት ሙሽሪት በገበያ የምትታይበት ባህላዊ ሥርዓት | pre-wedding celebratory market outing one week before marriage |
| **yaagano** | የአገኖ | `N` | eko gelo baale | ሠርግ | wedding ceremony and feast |
| **githe** | ጊꬄ | `N` | ekki simmidaappe guye na7entta soo biyaa woga | መልስ (ከሠርግ በኋላ ወደ ሙሽሪት ቤተሰብ የሚደረግ ጉዞ) | post-wedding ceremonial visit to the bride's parents' home |
| **soyzo** | ሶይዞ | `N` | keetha ayso | ትዳር / የቤተሰብ አስተዳደር | married household life / matrimony |
| **cumo / cumees** | ጩሞ / ጩሜእስ | `N/V` | dan7on darkko doozata tokko | ሙሽራው ከጋብቻ በፊት በጓሮው የተለያዩ ተክሎችን መትከል | groom's pre-marital planting of food crops around his new homestead |
| **medda** | ሜድደ | `N` | ooratha ekki gelidayssata | ሙሽራ / ሙሽሪት (አዲስ ተጋቢዎች) | newlywed bride and groom |
| **geela7o** | ጌእለዖ | `N` | azina gelonna na7iya | ልጃገረድ (ለአቅመ ሔዋን የደረሰች ያላገባች ወጣት) | unmarried young woman / maiden |
| **sayo** | ሰዮ | `N` | azini baynna maccasi | ባል የሞተባት ወይም ያላገባች ሴት | widow / single woman |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Transforming Cultural Lexemes Across 4 Sentence Moods (2.F Qawosa, p. 18): Mallas for 'Laazanthi' & 'Qolle': (1) Oysho: 'Almaazo Akaalus laazanthi oychidee?' (2) Kiita: 'Astire ane taw qolle dadda.' (3) Paate: 'Akaaloy laazanthi yeddis.' (4) Malalo: 'Hanna qolliya waana lo77ay!'
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ትረንስፎርሚንግ ጩልቱረል ሌጤሜስ አጭሮስስ 4 ሴንቴንጬ ሞኦድስ (2።ፍ ቀዎሰ፣ ፕ። 18)፦ መልለስ ፎር 'ለአዘንꬂ' & 'ቆልሌ'፦ (1) ኦይሾ፦ 'አልመአዞ አከአሉስ ለአዘንꬂ ኦይቺዴእ?' (2) ኪኢተ፦ 'አስቲሬ አኔ ተው ቆልሌ ደድደ።' (3) ፐአቴ፦ 'አከአሎይ ለአዘንꬂ ዬድዲስ።' (4) መለሎ፦ 'ሀንነ ቆልሊየ ወአነ ሎዕዓይ!'
- **Amharic:** ቃላትን በአራቱ የዓረፍተ ነገር ዓይነቶች (ጥያቄያዊ፣ ትዕዛዛዊ፣ ገላጭና አጋናኝ) መጠቀም (ገጽ 18)፦ ምሳሌ፦ 'አልማዞን ለአካሉ ሽማግሌ ጠየቀለት ወይ?' (ጥያቄ)፤ 'አስቴር እባክሽ ጥጥ ፍተይልኝ' (ትዕዛዝ/ልመና)።
- **English:** Sentence Mood Transformations (2.F Qawosa, p. 18): Constructing interrogative, imperative, declarative, and exclamatory sentences for 10 cultural nouns (Laazanthi, Qolle, Githe, Soofe, Soyzo, Yaagano, Medda, Sayo, Wodalla, Bollo).

#### E. Proverbs, Idioms, Riddles & Oral Literature (`Leemiso / Tosse / Aaqeta / Sedhemenkke / Qan77ishe`)
| Category | Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Tosse (Proverb in Dialogue, p. 13 & p. 51) | **Cimi shi7a muzees.** | ጪሚ ሺዓ ሙዜእስ። | ሽማግሌ ኩስ ያስበላል (የሽማግሌ ምክርና ሽምግልና የማይቻለውን ያስችላል/ያስታርቃል)። | An elder can even persuade one to swallow dung (the respected authority of elders resolves even the hardest disputes). |
| Tosse (Proverb in Dialogue, p. 14) | **Dimbboy parsso lage.** | ዲምብቦይ ፐርስሶ ለጌ። | ከበሮ የቦርዴ (የድግስ መጠጥ) ወዳጅ ነው። | The ceremonial drum is the companion of traditional beer (festive music and hospitality go hand in hand). |

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 4 (2.D Xaafo Dabbotho & Dooro, p. 16): Match: 1. Uuga -> B. Guuthi; 2. Laazanthi -> G. Macho oychiya asi; 3. Doona pogo -> F. Caaqo imo; 4. Soyzo -> C. Ayso; 5. Yakka -> A. Dalgga dago. Fill-in: 2. Na7ay koyro macho oychanaw [laazanthi/cima] yeddees. 4. Azina gelanaw gakkida na7iyaa [geela7o] geetettawsu.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 4 (2።ድ ጠአፎ ደብቦꬆ & ዶኦሮ፣ ፕ። 16)፦ መትች፦ 1። ኡኡገ -> ብ። ጉኡꬂ፤ 2። ለአዘንꬂ -> ግ። መቾ ኦይቺየ አሲ፤ 3። ዶኦነ ፖጎ -> ፍ። ጨአቆ ኢሞ፤ 4። ሶይዞ -> ጭ። አይሶ፤ 5። የክከ -> አ። ደልግገ ደጎ። ፊልል-ኢን፦ 2። ነዓይ ኮይሮ መቾ ኦይቻነው [ለአዘንꬂ/ጪመ] ዬድዴእስ። 4። አዚነ ጌለነው ገክኪደ ነዒየአ [ጌእለዖ] ጌእቴትተውሱ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 4 (ማዛመድና ምርጫ፣ ገጽ 16)፦ 1. Uuga -> ጥቂት (Guuthi)፤ 2. Laazanthi -> ሴት የሚጠይቅ ሽማግሌ፤ 3. Doona pogo -> የቃል ማሰሪያ፤ 4. Soyzo -> ትዳር/አስተዳደር፤ 5. Yakka -> ትልቅ ደቦ (Dalgga dago)።
- **English:** Exercise 4 (Matching & Multiple Choice, p. 16): 1. Uuga -> Guuthi; 2. Laazanthi -> Macho oychiya asi; 3. Doona pogo -> Caaqo imo; 4. Soyzo -> Ayso; 5. Yakka -> Dalgga dago.

### Gr10_Ch3 — Shemppo 3: Maccatanne Dichaa (Women and Development) | ሼምፕፖ 3፦ መጭጨተንኔ ዲቻአ | ምዕራፍ 3፦ ሴቶችና ልማት | Chapter 3: Women and Development, Biography of Mishire Amaarachi Aggido, and Full Sentence Syntax
- **Pages:** Book pp. 14–23 (PDF pp. 19–28) • **Source:** [Grade 10 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1mKIcfMoMgnZ9Pa657NWmE-OGND2wlveM/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Mishire (Mre) Amaarachi Aggido Taarike* (ሚሺሬ (ምሬ) አመአረቺ አግጊዶ ተአሪኬ) — የወይዘሮ አማረች አጊዶ የሕይወትና የትምህርት ታሪክ / Biography of Mishire Amaarachi Aggido: Pioneer Female Scholar from Southern Ethiopia (pp. 20–22 (Book pp. 15–17))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Mishire (Mre) Amaarachi Aggido geetettiyaara Dugeha Tophphiyan luxida maccasatappe issiwu. | ሚሺሬ (ምሬ) አመአረቺ አግጊዶ ጌእቴትቲየአረ ዱጌሀ ቶጵጪየን ሉጢደ መጭጨሰተፕፔ ኢስሲዉ። | ወይዘሮ አማረች አጊዶ በደቡብ ኢትዮጵያ በትምህርት ከፍተኛ ደረጃ ከደረሱ ሴቶች አንዷ ናት። | Mishire Amaarachi Aggido is one of the pioneering educated women of Southern Ethiopia. |
| 1975 M.Laythan, Tungga yunbburssen Deretetha Saynsse timirtten anjjettada ooso oykkasu; hessape guye Mastterete timirtte qaada demmada kare gade keyishin I azinay haan attidi naytta baaloyi dichchishe gam7is. | 1975 ም።ለይꬃን፣ ቱንግገ ዩንብቡርስሴን ዴሬቴꬃ ሰይንስሴ ቲሚርትቴን አንጅጄትተደ ኦኦሶ ኦይክከሱ፤ ሄስሰፔ ጉዬ መስትቴሬቴ ቲሚርትቴ ቀአደ ዴምመደ ከሬ ገዴ ኬዪሺን ኢ አዚነይ ሀአን አትቲዲ ነይትተ በአሎዪ ዲችቺሼ ገምዒስ። | በ1975 ዓ.ም. ከአዲስ አበባ (ቱንጋ) ዩኒቨርሲቲ በማኅበራዊ ሳይንስ ተመርቃ ሥራ ያዘች፤ ከዚያም የማስተርስ ትምህርት ዕድል አግኝታ ወደ ውጭ አገር ስትሄድ ባለቤቷ ልጆቹን በኃላፊነት እየተንከባከበ አሳደገ። | In 1975 E.C., she graduated in Social Sciences from Addis Ababa University, and when she went abroad for her Master's degree, her husband stayed home and devotedly raised their two children. |
| Wurssethan iya: 'Ta naytoo, ta micheto, macca tamaaretoo ay wodekka qantha wode meton xoonettidi hintte timirttiyaape guye gooppite' gasu. | ዉርስሴꬃን ኢየ፦ 'ተ ነይቶኦ፣ ተ ሚቼቶ፣ መጭጨ ተመአሬቶኦ አይ ዎዴክከ ቀንꬃ ዎዴ ሜቶን ጦኦኔትቲዲ ሂንትቴ ቲሚርትቲየአፔ ጉዬ ጎኦፕፒቴ' ገሱ። | በመጨረሻም እሷ፦ 'ልጆቼ፣ እህቶቼ፣ ሴት ተማሪዎች ሆይ! በማንኛውም ጊዜ በጊዜያዊ ችግር ተሸንፋችሁ ከትምህርታችሁ ወደ ኋላ አትበሉ' አለች። | In conclusion she advised: 'My children, my sisters, female students: never be defeated by temporary hardships or fall behind in your education.' |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 2 (3.B Haasaya, p. 20): Group discussion on 5 topics: 1. Maccatas gade dichaa bolla de7iya gishaaddetethay ay aazii? 2. Maccatasinne attumatas de7iya ginatethaa bolla haasayite. 5. Maccati laames tamaaro koshshiiyee zal77e koshshiyaako odettite.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 2 (3።ብ ሀአሰየ፣ ፕ። 20)፦ ግሮኡፕ ዲስጩስሲኦን ኦን 5 ቶፒጭስ፦ 1። መጭጨተስ ገዴ ዲቻአ ቦልለ ዴዒየ ጊሻአድዴቴꬃይ አይ አአዚኢ? 2። መጭጨተሲንኔ አትቱመተስ ዴዒየ ጊነቴꬃአ ቦልለ ሀአሰዪቴ። 5። መጭጨቲ ለአሜስ ተመአሮ ኮሽሺኢዬእ ዘልዕዔ ኮሽሺየአኮ ኦዴትቲቴ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 2 (3.B ንግግር፣ ገጽ 20)፦ 1. ሴቶች በአገር ልማት ላይ ያላቸው ድርሻ ምን ይመስላል? 2. በሴቶችና በወንዶች እኩልነት ላይ ተወያዩ። 5. ለሴቶች ለውጥ ትምህርት ይሻላል ወይስ ንግድ?
- **English:** Exercise 2 (3.B Oral Discussion, p. 20): Discuss women's contribution to national development, gender equality, and the role of education and commerce in empowering women.

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **baalo** | በአሎ | `N` | ishaliso / lo77o oyqethi | እንክብካቤ / ምቾት | care / comfort / nurturing |
| **baaxe** | በአጤ | `N` | mino ooso / bute | ጥረት / ትጋት | effort / perseverance / struggle |
| **zalbudda** | ዘልቡድደ | `ADJ` | zambbo / mino | ታታሪ / ብርቱ | diligent / industrious / energetic |
| **cirkka** | ጪርክከ | `ADJ` | qara | ንቁ / ቀልጣፋ | alert / active / sharp |
| **looho** | ሎኦሆ | `N` | timirtte / era | ሥልጠና / ትምህርት | training / professional education |
| **luxe** | ሉጤ | `N` | dhoqqa detha gakko | መራቀቅ / ከፍተኛ ደረጃ መድረስ | excellence / advancement / mastery |
| **ciire** | ጪኢሬ | `N` | muruuta / ayfe | ፍሬ / ውጤት / አስተዋጽኦ | fruit / core result / contribution |
| **yohaadde** | ዮሀአድዴ | `N` | meega qofa yohaadde | ባለቤት (የዓረፍተ ነገር ባለቤት / Subject) | grammatical subject of a sentence |
| **kaalodde** | ከአሎድዴ | `N` | meega qofa kaalodde | ተሳቢ (የዓረፍተ ነገር ተሳቢ / Object) | grammatical object / complement |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** 1. Goofatho Meega Qofa Medhuwa (SOV Word Order: Yohaadde + Kaalodde + Peesho, p. 24): Goofatho qawosa wogan koyro yohaaddey, kaallidi kaaloddey, wurssethan peeshoy (Y + K + P) yees. Mallas: 'Na7ay (Y) uketha (K) mis (P).' (Contrast with English SVO).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 1። ጎኦፈꬆ ሜእገ ቆፈ ሜꬉወ (ሶቭ ዎርድ ኦርዴር፦ ዮሀአድዴ + ከአሎድዴ + ፔእሾ፣ ፕ። 24)፦ ጎኦፈꬆ ቀዎሰ ዎገን ኮይሮ ዮሀአድዴይ፣ ከአልሊዲ ከአሎድዴይ፣ ዉርስሴꬃን ፔእሾይ (ይ + ክ + ፕ) ዬእስ። መልለስ፦ 'ነዓይ (ይ) ኡኬꬃ (ክ) ሚስ (ፕ)።' (ጮንትረስት ዊꬅ እንግሊሽ ስቮ)።
- **Amharic:** 1. የጎፋኛ ዓረፍተ ነገር የቃላት አሰካክ (ባለቤት + ተሳቢ + ግስ / Y + K + P፣ ገጽ 24)፦ በጎፋኛ ሰዋስው መጀመሪያ ባለቤት (Yohaadde)፣ ቀጥሎ ተሳቢ (Kaalodde)፣ በመጨረሻ ግስ (Peesho) ይመጣል። ምሳሌ፦ 'ልጁ (Y) እንጀራ (K) በላ (P)።'
- **English:** 1. Canonical SOV Word Order in Gofatho (Yohaadde + Kaalodde + Peesho, p. 24): Subject (Yohaadde) + Object (Kaalodde) + Verb (Peesho).
- **Gofatho Latin:** 2. Meega Qofa Qommota Kiitaan (Paate [Eenota & Akkayta], Oysho, Malalo, Kiita) & Medhuwan (Yeda, Dakko, Walaka/Gite, Dakko Gite) (pp. 24–28): Includes affirmative (eenota: 'Macca tamaareti puuto tokkidosona') vs. negative (akkayta: 'Maccata maadey baynna gadey diccenna') declarative sentences, and all 4 structural sentence types.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 2። ሜእገ ቆፈ ቆምሞተ ኪኢተአን (ፐአቴ [እእኖተ & አክከይተ]፣ ኦይሾ፣ መለሎ፣ ኪኢተ) & ሜꬉወን (ዬደ፣ ደክኮ፣ ወለከ/ጊቴ፣ ደክኮ ጊቴ) (ፕፕ። 24–28)፦ ኢንጭሉዴስ አፍፊርመቲቬ (እእኖተ፦ 'መጭጨ ተመአሬቲ ፑኡቶ ቶክኪዶሶነ') ቭስ። ኔገቲቬ (አክከይተ፦ 'መጭጨተ መአዴይ በይንነ ገዴይ ዲጭጬንነ') ዴጭለረቲቬ ሴንቴንጬስ፣ አንድ አልል 4 ስትሩጭቱረል ሴንቴንጬ ትይፔስ።
- **Amharic:** 2. የዓረፍተ ነገር ዓይነቶች በመልእክታቸው (አዎንታዊና አሉታዊ ገላጭ፣ ጥያቄያዊ፣ አጋናኝ፣ ትዕዛዛዊ) እና በመዋቅራቸው (ነጠላ/Yeda፣ ድርብ/Dakko፣ ውስብስብ/Walaka፣ ድርብ ውስብስብ/Dakko Gite) (ገጽ 24–28)።
- **English:** 2. Complete Taxonomy of Gofatho Sentences by Mood (Affirmative/Eenota & Negative/Akkayta Declarative, Interrogative, Exclamatory, Imperative) and by Clause Complexity (Simple/Yeda, Compound/Dakko, Complex/Walaka, Compound-Complex/Dakko Gite) (pp. 24–28).

#### E. Proverbs, Idioms, Riddles & Oral Literature (`Leemiso / Tosse / Aaqeta / Sedhemenkke / Qan77ishe`)
| Category | Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Tosse (Proverb in Meeze 7, p. 28) | **Cimappe [loohiday / yuuyiday] aadhdhees.** | ጪመፕፔ [ሎኦሂደይ / ዩኡዪደይ] አአꬍꬌእስ። | ከሽማግሌ (በዕድሜ ከገፋ) የተማረ/የዞረ ይበልጣል። | One who has studied and traveled surpasses one who has merely aged. |

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 6 (3.F Qawosa, p. 26): Identify the mood of each sentence: 1. 'Macca nayta un77etho koshshenna' -> Paate (Akkayta) meega qofa; 2. 'Maccasha masqqali awdee?' -> Oysho meega qofa; 3. 'Bocca! Maakka naawa!' -> Malalo meega qofa; 4. 'Maccata maata naagite' -> Kiita meega qofa.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 6 (3።ፍ ቀዎሰ፣ ፕ። 26)፦ ኢዴንቲፍይ ꬄ ሞኦድ ኦፍ እአች ሴንቴንጬ፦ 1። 'መጭጨ ነይተ ኡንዕዔꬆ ኮሽሼንነ' -> ፐአቴ (አክከይተ) ሜእገ ቆፈ፤ 2። 'መጭጨሻ መስቅቀሊ አውዴእ?' -> ኦይሾ ሜእገ ቆፈ፤ 3። 'ቦጭጨ! መአክከ ነአወ!' -> መለሎ ሜእገ ቆፈ፤ 4። 'መጭጨተ መአተ ነአጊቴ' -> ኪኢተ ሜእገ ቆፈ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 6 (ሰዋስው፣ ገጽ 26)፦ 1. አሉታዊ ገላጭ ዓ.ነገር (Paate)፤ 2. ጥያቄያዊ ዓ.ነገር (Oysho)፤ 3. አጋናኝ ዓ.ነገር (Malalo)፤ 4. ትዕዛዛዊ ዓ.ነገር (Kiita)።
- **English:** Exercise 6 (Sentence Mood Classification, p. 26): 1. Negative Declarative; 2. Interrogative; 3. Exclamatory; 4. Imperative.

### Gr10_Ch4 — Shemppo 4: Dummatethan De7iya Issipetethaa | ሼምፕፖ 4፦ ዱምመቴꬃን ዴዒየ ኢስሲፔቴꬃአ | ምዕራፍ 4፦ በብዝኃነት ውስጥ ያለ አንድነት | Chapter 4: Unity in Diversity, Federalism, and the Five Parts of Speech (Qaala Kifileta)
- **Pages:** Book pp. 24–33 (PDF pp. 29–38) • **Source:** [Grade 10 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1mKIcfMoMgnZ9Pa657NWmE-OGND2wlveM/view?usp=drivesdk)
