- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Pederaale Kawotethi (Dummatethan De7iya Issipetethaa)* (ፔዴረአሌ ከዎቴꬂ (ዱምመቴꬃን ዴዒየ ኢስሲፔቴꬃአ)) — ፌዴራላዊ መንግሥትና በብዝኃነት ውስጥ ያለ አንድነት / Federalism and Unity in Diversity: Mutual Respect Across Religions, Languages, and Peoples in Ethiopia (pp. 31–33 (Book pp. 26–28))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Tophphiya dere asaa giddon medhettanaw dandda7iya dummatetha qommotape bertteti: kochchata (ethnic), doona, ammano, guta (regional), woga, taarikenne polotika dummatethata gidoosona. | ቶጵጪየ ዴሬ አሰአ ጊድዶን ሜꬌትተነው ደንድደዒየ ዱምመቴꬃ ቆምሞተፔ ቤርትቴቲ፦ ኮችቻተ (እꬅኒጭ)፣ ዶኦነ፣ አምመኖ፣ ጉተ (ሬጊኦነል)፣ ዎገ፣ ተአሪኬንኔ ፖሎቲከ ዱምመቴꬃተ ጊዶኦሶነ። | በኢትዮጵያ ሕዝቦች መካከል ከሚታዩት የብዝኃነት ዓይነቶች ዋና ዋናዎቹ፦ የብሔር፣ የቋንቋ፣ የሃይማኖት፣ የአካባቢ፣ የባህል፣ የታሪክና የፖለቲካ ልዩነቶች ናቸው። | The primary dimensions of diversity among the peoples of Ethiopia include ethnicity, language, religion, region, culture, history, and politics. |
| Nu gaden Isilaama ammaniya asatinne Kiristtaane ammano kaalliya asati daro laythas wol bonchchidinne wol siiqidi issippe de7idosona. | ኑ ገዴን ኢሲለአመ አምመኒየ አሰቲንኔ ኪሪስትተአኔ አምመኖ ከአልሊየ አሰቲ ደሮ ለይꬃስ ዎል ቦንችቺዲንኔ ዎል ሲኢቂዲ ኢስሲፕፔ ዴዒዶሶነ። | በአገራችን የእስልምና እና የክርስትና ሃይማኖት ተከታዮች ለብዙ ዘመናት እርስ በርስ ተከባብረውና ተዋደው አብረው ኖረዋል። | In our country, Muslims and Christians have lived together in mutual respect and love for centuries. |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Full Dialogue between Morggaama and Gardda (4.B Haasaya, pp. 30–31, by Mokkonna M.): Morggaama: 'Hintte heera asaa issipetethay ay melee?' Gardda: 'La ta new ay odada ay aggoo! Deretethaa giddon daro dummatethay de7ishin wol dandda7on dumma dumma gahettiya bessatan: wudiyaan, giyaan, woosa keethaan, yaaganoninne h.h.mela bessatan dumma dumma metoti medhetteyssata dandda7idi qaari bessonna woli kammidi de7oosona... "Darana deriyan heedzay daggi uyees" giyaa tossiya mela wolli siiqoninne dandda7an duussay keehipe lo77ope attin iita gidenna.'
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ፉልል ዲአሎጉእ ቤትዌእን ሞርግገአመ አንድ ገርድደ (4።ብ ሀአሰየ፣ ፕፕ። 30–31፣ ብይ ሞክኮንነ ም።)፦ ሞርግገአመ፦ 'ሂንትቴ ሄእረ አሰአ ኢስሲፔቴꬃይ አይ ሜሌእ?' ገርድደ፦ 'ለ ተ ኔው አይ ኦደደ አይ አግጎኦ! ዴሬቴꬃአ ጊድዶን ደሮ ዱምመቴꬃይ ዴዒሺን ዎል ደንድደዖን ዱምመ ዱምመ ገሄትቲየ ቤስሰተን፦ ዉዲየአን፣ ጊየአን፣ ዎኦሰ ኬእꬃአን፣ የአገኖኒንኔ ህ።ህ።ሜለ ቤስሰተን ዱምመ ዱምመ ሜቶቲ ሜꬌትቴይስሰተ ደንድደዒዲ ቀአሪ ቤስሶንነ ዎሊ ከምሚዲ ዴዖኦሶነ።።። "ደረነ ዴሪየን ሄእꬓይ ደግጊ ኡዬእስ" ጊየአ ቶስሲየ ሜለ ዎልሊ ሲኢቆኒንኔ ደንድደዓን ዱኡስሰይ ኬእሂፔ ሎዕዖፔ አትቲን ኢኢተ ጊዴንነ።'
- **Amharic:** በሞርጋማ እና በጋርዳ መካከል የተደረገ ምልልስ (ገጽ 30–31)፦ ሞርጋማ ስለ አካባቢው ሕዝብ አንድነት ሲጠይቅ፣ ጋርዳ በግጦሽ ቦታ (wudiyaan)፣ በገበያ (giyaan)፣ በጸሎት ቤትና በሠርግ ላይ በመቻቻልና በመከባበር አብረው እንደሚኖሩ 'በሚያድግ አገር ሦስት ሰው ተጋግዞ ይጠጣል' በሚለው ምሳሌያዊ ንግግር ያስረዳዋል።
- **English:** Dialogue between Morggaama and Gardda (pp. 30–31): Discussing how diverse communities maintain unity in grazing lands, markets, places of worship, and weddings through tolerance (woli dandda7o), mutual respect (wolli bonchetetha), and equality (gina xeelo).

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **phaxethi** | ጳጤꬂ | `N` | shaakeretethi | መከፋፈል / መለያየት | division / factionalism / splitting |
| **daggo** | ደግጎ | `N` | wol maadetethi | መረዳዳት / ደቦ | mutual aid / cooperative labor |
| **gontte** | ጎንትቴ | `N` | zawa | ድንበር / ወሰን | boundary / border |
| **issipetethi** | ኢስሲፔቴꬂ | `N` | woli gatho | አንድነት / ኅብረት | unity / solidarity |
| **dummatethi** | ዱምመቴꬂ | `N` | issoy issuwappe dummato | ልዩነት / ብዝኃነት | diversity / difference |
| **lancce** | ለንጭጬ | `N` | tooshe / morkketetha kanddo | ንቀት / መድልዎ | contempt / discrimination |
| **zarbbe** | ዘርብቤ | `N` | sirphatethi / sarotethi | መረጋጋት / ሰላም | calm / stability / peace |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Goofatho Qaala Kifileta (5 Parts of Speech, 4.F Qawosa, pp. 35–38): (1) Sunthi (Nouns: adde, macca, na7a, keethe, boori), (2) Peesho (Verbs: ma, imma, immis, bis, ekkis, oothis, bees, maana), (3) Sunqqo (Adjectives modifying nouns: wancco boori, gooba tamaare), (4) Peeqo (Adverbs modifying verbs: loythi, ubbara, zine, wontta, eeson), (5) Dabbotho: Sigetho (Postpositions: mela/-da 'like', bolla, garssa) & Gasetho (Conjunctions: -nne 'and', shin 'but', woy 'or', gisho 'because').
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ጎኦፈꬆ ቀአለ ኪፊሌተ (5 ፐርትስ ኦፍ ስፔእች፣ 4።ፍ ቀዎሰ፣ ፕፕ። 35–38)፦ (1) ሱንꬂ (ኖኡንስ፦ አድዴ፣ መጭጨ፣ ነዓ፣ ኬእꬄ፣ ቦኦሪ)፣ (2) ፔእሾ (ቬርብስ፦ መ፣ ኢምመ፣ ኢምሚስ፣ ቢስ፣ እክኪስ፣ ኦኦꬂስ፣ ቤእስ፣ መአነ)፣ (3) ሱንቅቆ (አድጄጭቲቬስ ሞዲፍዪንግ ኖኡንስ፦ ወንጭጮ ቦኦሪ፣ ጎኦበ ተመአሬ)፣ (4) ፔእቆ (አድቬርብስ ሞዲፍዪንግ ቬርብስ፦ ሎይꬂ፣ ኡብበረ፣ ዚኔ፣ ዎንትተ፣ እእሶን)፣ (5) ደብቦꬆ፦ ሲጌꬆ (ፖስትፖሲቲኦንስ፦ ሜለ/-ደ 'ሊኬ'፣ ቦልለ፣ ገርስሰ) & ገሴꬆ (ጮንጁንጭቲኦንስ፦ -ንኔ 'አንድ'፣ ሺን 'ቡት'፣ ዎይ 'ኦር'፣ ጊሾ 'ቤጨኡሴ')።
- **Amharic:** አምስቱ የጎፋኛ የቃል ክፍሎች (4.F Qawosa፣ ገጽ 35–38)፦ (1) ስም (Sunthi)፣ (2) ግስ (Peesho)፣ (3) ቅጽል (Sunqqo)፣ (4) ተውሳከ ግስ (Peeqo)፣ (5) መስተዋድድና አያያዥ (Dabbotho፦ Sigethonne Gasetho)።
- **English:** The Five Parts of Speech in Gofatho (4.F Qawosa, pp. 35–38): Nouns (Sunthi), Verbs (Peesho), Adjectives (Sunqqo), Adverbs (Peeqo), and Function Words (Dabbotho: Postpositions 'Sigetho' and Conjunctions 'Gasetho').

#### E. Proverbs, Idioms, Riddles & Oral Literature (`Leemiso / Tosse / Aaqeta / Sedhemenkke / Qan77ishe`)
| Category | Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Tosse (Proverb in Dialogue, p. 30) | **Darana deriyan heedzay daggi uyees.** | ደረነ ዴሪየን ሄእꬓይ ደግጊ ኡዬእስ። | በሚያድግ (በሚበለጽግ) አገር ሦስት ሰው ተጋግዞ ይጠጣል። | In a community destined to prosper, three people share and drink together in harmony. |
| Tosse (Proverb in Meeze 6, p. 38) | **Manqqoy manddi kaallees; durey mi-kaallees.** | መንቅቆይ መንድዲ ከአልሌእስ፤ ዱሬይ ሚ-ከአልሌእስ። | ድሀ ተጉዞ (ለፍቶ) ይከተላል፤ ሀብታም በልቶ ይከተላል። | The poor person follows by trudging along; the rich person follows after eating. |

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 6 (4.F Qawosa, p. 38): Identify parts of speech in sentences: 3. 'Darcci ubba wode shiiqotan pa qofaa eeson shiishees' -> Peesho: shiishees; Peeqo: eeson. 5. 'Bazoy zine Bulqqipe wancco boori shammis' -> Sunqqo: wancco. 7. 'Mogoseynne Geetachoy Goofatho maxaafe xaafoosona' -> Gasetho: -nne.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 6 (4።ፍ ቀዎሰ፣ ፕ። 38)፦ ኢዴንቲፍይ ፐርትስ ኦፍ ስፔእች ኢን ሴንቴንጬስ፦ 3። 'ደርጭጪ ኡብበ ዎዴ ሺኢቆተን ፐ ቆፈአ እእሶን ሺኢሼእስ' -> ፔእሾ፦ ሺኢሼእስ፤ ፔእቆ፦ እእሶን። 5። 'በዞይ ዚኔ ቡልቅቂፔ ወንጭጮ ቦኦሪ ሻምሚስ' -> ሱንቅቆ፦ ወንጭጮ። 7። 'ሞጎሴይንኔ ጌእተቾይ ጎኦፈꬆ መጠአፌ ጠአፎኦሶነ' -> ገሴꬆ፦ -ንኔ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 6 (ሰዋስው፣ ገጽ 38)፦ በዓረፍተ ነገር ውስጥ የቃል ክፍሎችን መለየት፦ 3. ግስ (Peesho) = shiishees፤ 5. ቅጽል (Sunqqo) = wancco፤ 7. አያያዥ (Gasetho) = -nne።
- **English:** Exercise 6 (Part-of-Speech Identification, p. 38): 3. Verb = shiishees, Adverb = eeson; 5. Adjective = wancco; 7. Conjunction = -nne.

### Gr10_Ch5 — Shemppo 5: Bila Oda | ሼምፕፖ 5፦ ቢለ ኦደ | ምዕራፍ 5፦ የጎፋ ሥነ-ቃል (ምሳሌያዊ ንግግር፣ ተረት፣ እንቆቅልሽና ቃንጪሼ) | Chapter 5: Gofatho Oral Literature (Dual-Edged Proverbs, Folktales, Riddles, and Poetic Duels)
- **Pages:** Book pp. 34–42 (PDF pp. 39–47) • **Source:** [Grade 10 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1mKIcfMoMgnZ9Pa657NWmE-OGND2wlveM/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Tosse, Goofa Dere Asaa Hayse ('Azalla Adde' Taarike), Sedhemenkkenne Qan77ishe* (ቶስሴ፣ ጎኦፈ ዴሬ አሰአ ሀይሴ ('አዘልለ አድዴ' ተአሪኬ)፣ ሴꬌሜንክኬንኔ ቀንዕዒሼ) — ምሳሌያዊ ንግግር (Tosse)፣ የጎፋ ተረት (ከስንፍና ወደ ታታሪነት የተለወጠው ሰው)፣ እንቆቅልሽና ቃንጪሼ / Proverbial Philosophy (Tosse), Folktale of the Reformed Lazy Husband (Hayse), Riddles (Sedhemenkke), and Poetic Duels (Qan77ishe) (pp. 40–47 (Book pp. 35–42))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Tossey Goofa wogan nam77u bagga oco mashshada issi allalles woy issi asas de7iya kandduwas minonne dol7a miyiyaa gathi qonccisiya wolqqi de7ees. | ቶስሴይ ጎኦፈ ዎገን ነምዕዑ በግገ ኦጮ መሽሻደ ኢስሲ አልለልሌስ ዎይ ኢስሲ አሰስ ዴዒየ ከንድዱወስ ሚኖንኔ ዶልዓ ሚዪየአ ገꬂ ቆንጭጪሲየ ዎልቅቂ ዴዔእስ። | ምሳሌያዊ ንግግር (Tosse) በጎፋ ባህል እንደ ሁለት አፍ ቢላዋ የአንድን ጉዳይ ወይም ሰው ጠንካራና ደካማ ጎን አጣምሮ የመግለጽ ኃይል አለው። | In Gofatho culture, a proverb (tosse) is like a double-edged knife capable of illuminating both the strong and weak sides of any matter or person. |
| Asara Go7ettidi Haysetidabaa (p. 43): Beni issi heeran issi azalla adde de7eyssi... pa manqqotethiw yilotidi biraata miishe wale, kaltta, beexe ekkidi gadha gelis; qeexi kessida gadiyan badalanne leehe wonddo wooqisidi beni attida machees zaari cima yeddin qinxxi qiphphi gada soo yasu. | አሰረ ጎዔትቲዲ ሀይሴቲደበአ (ፕ። 43)፦ ቤኒ ኢስሲ ሄእረን ኢስሲ አዘልለ አድዴ ዴዔይስሲ።።። ፐ መንቅቆቴꬂው ዪሎቲዲ ቢረአተ ሚኢሼ ወሌ፣ ከልትተ፣ ቤእጤ እክኪዲ ገꬋ ጌሊስ፤ ቄእጢ ኬስሲደ ገዲየን በደለንኔ ሌእሄ ዎንድዶ ዎኦቂሲዲ ቤኒ አትቲደ መቼእስ ዘአሪ ጪመ ዬድዲን ቂንጥጢ ቂጵጪ ገደ ሶኦ የሱ። | የተረት ምሳሌ (ገጽ 43)፦ በድሮ ጊዜ አንድ ሰነፍ ሰው ሚስቱ በድህነቱ ምክንያት ትታው ወደ እናቷ ቤት ስትሄድ፣ ተቆጭቶ ማጭድ፣ መጥረቢያና ዶማ ይዞ ወደ ቆላ ወርዶ መሬት መንጥሮ በቆሎና ማሽላ አብቅሎ ሲበለጽግ፣ ሚስቱ በደስታ ወደ ቤቷ ተመለሰች። | Folktale of the Reformed Husband (p. 43): Abandoned by his wife due to his laziness and poverty, a man takes up his sickle, axe, and hoe, clears lowland fields, grows abundant maize and sorghum, and welcomes his wife back in joy. |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Sedhemenkke & Qan77ishe Interactive Performance Dialogues (pp. 44–47): Includes the 5-guess rule in Sedhemenkke, the boast formula ('Ta na7ay para bolla pastti ne na7ay xinqilo bolla cistti; ta na7ay xamaata mixilttees ne na7ay giishshi mixilttees'), and Qan77ishe dialogues between Doli & Baloy and Cumppe & Cula.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሴꬌሜንክኬ & ቀንዕዒሼ ኢንቴረጭቲቬ ፔርፎርመንጬ ዲአሎጉእስ (ፕፕ። 44–47)፦ ኢንጭሉዴስ ꬄ 5-ጉእስስ ሩሌ ኢን ሴꬌሜንክኬ፣ ꬄ ቦአስት ፎርሙለ ('ተ ነዓይ ፐረ ቦልለ ፐስትቲ ኔ ነዓይ ጢንቂሎ ቦልለ ጪስትቲ፤ ተ ነዓይ ጠመአተ ሚጢልትቴእስ ኔ ነዓይ ጊኢሽሺ ሚጢልትቴእስ')፣ አንድ ቀንዕዒሼ ዲአሎጉእስ ቤትዌእን ዶሊ & በሎይ አንድ ጩምፕፔ & ጩለ።
- **Amharic:** የእንቆቅልሽና የቃንጪሼ የተግባር ምልልሶች (ገጽ 44–47)፦ በእንቆቅልሽ መላሹ 5 ጊዜ ከሞከረ በኋላ አገር ሲሰጥ ጠያቂው የሚፎክርበት ሥርዓትና የዶሊና ባሎይ እንዲሁም የጩምፔና ጩላ የቃንጪሼ ምልልስ።
- **English:** Riddle and Poetic Duel Performance Dialogues (pp. 44–47): Full ceremonial dialogue formulas for Sedhemenkke (including the 5-attempt rule and victory boast) and Qan77ishe couplet exchanges.

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **nam77u bagga oco mashsha** | ነምዕዑ በግገ ኦጮ መሽሻ | `NP` | nam77u miyiyaara qanxxiya mashsha | ሁለት አፍ ያለው ስለታም ቢላዋ/ሰይፍ | double-edged sharp knife/sword |
| **nashshishe** | ነሽሺሼ | `CNV` | sabbishe | እያሞገሰ / እያደነቀ | praising / commending |
| **borishe** | ቦሪሼ | `CNV` | qeelethishe | እየተቸ / እየነቀፈ | critiquing / reproaching |
| **wale, kaltta, beexe** | ወሌ፣ ከልትተ፣ ቤእጤ | `NP` | goshsha biraata miisheta | ማጭድ/ቆንጨራ፣ መጥረቢያና ዶማ (የእርሻ መሣሪያዎች) | bush-knife/sickle, axe, and digging hoe (traditional iron farming tools) |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Antithetical & Parallel Structure in Gofatho Proverbs (pp. 40–42): Proverbs pair semantic opposites (Na7a–cima, macca–attuma, manqqo–dure, zambbo–azalla, booza–cincca, kalo–kosha, elleso–gam77o, mal77o–camo, siiqo–ixo) using balanced medial converbs (-in, -idi).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** አንቲꬄቲጨል & ፐረልሌል ስትሩጭቱሬ ኢን ጎፈꬆ ፕሮቬርብስ (ፕፕ። 40–42)፦ ፕሮቬርብስ ፐኢር ሴመንቲጭ ኦፕፖሲቴስ (ነዓ–ጪመ፣ መጭጨ–አትቱመ፣ መንቅቆ–ዱሬ፣ ዘምብቦ–አዘልለ፣ ቦኦዘ–ጪንጭጨ፣ ከሎ–ኮሻ፣ እልሌሶ–ገምዕዖ፣ መልዕዖ–ጨሞ፣ ሲኢቆ–ኢጦ) ኡሲንግ በለንጬድ ሜዲአል ጮንቬርብስ (-ኢን፣ -ኢዲ)።
- **Amharic:** በጎፋኛ ምሳሌያዊ ንግግሮች ውስጥ ያለው የተቃራኒ ቃላትና የሐረጎች ምጣኔ (ገጽ 40–42)፦ ልጅ-ሽማግሌ፣ ድሀ-ሀብታም፣ ታታሪ-ሰነፍ፣ ጥጋብ-ረሃብ፣ ችኮላ-መዘግየት፣ ፍቅር-ጥላቻ።
- **English:** Parallel and Antithetical Syntax in Proverbs (pp. 40–42): Pairing contrasting lexical poles across balanced clauses.

#### E. Proverbs, Idioms, Riddles & Oral Literature (`Leemiso / Tosse / Aaqeta / Sedhemenkke / Qan77ishe`)
| Category | Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Tosse (Nashshishe / Praising Good, p. 40) | **Lo77oy kayora lo77ees.** | ሎዕዖይ ከዮረ ሎዕዔእስ። | ጥሩ ነገር ከቡቃያው (ከጅምሩ) ያምራል። | Goodness blossoms right from the young shoot. |
| Tosse (Nashshishe / Praising Good, p. 40) | **Lo77oy wadhdhin sakkenna; lomppi qoommin qum7enna.** | ሎዕዖይ ወꬍꬊን ሰክኬንነ፤ ሎምፕፒ ቆኦምሚን ቁምዔንነ። | ጥሩ ነገር ቢወድቅ አያምም፣ አጥብቀው ቢይዙት አይጎዳም። | What is good does not hurt when it falls, nor spoil when held close. |
| Tosse (Borishe / Critiquing Good, p. 40) | **Lo77o dullen shi7i de7ees.** | ሎዕዖ ዱልሌን ሺዒ ዴዔእስ። | ከጥሩ ነገር ስር እንከን (ጉድለት) አይጠፋም። | Beneath a good exterior, a flaw may lurk. |
| Tosse (Borishe / Critiquing Appearance, p. 40) | **Malaara malxxaxxaa; kariyara xonggaaraa.** | መለአረ መልጥጠጥጠአ፤ ከሪየረ ጦንግገአረአ። | በውጩ ማራኪ፣ በውስጡ ግን እሾህ/አስቸጋሪ። | Outwardly attractive, inwardly thorny. |
| Tosse (Redeeming Side of Difficulty, p. 40) | **Iiti iita wode ashshees; Iiti ishassa; Haatha iitinne aayi iiti baawa.** | ኢኢቲ ኢኢተ ዎዴ አሽሼእስ፤ ኢኢቲ ኢሻስሰ፤ ሀአꬃ ኢኢቲንኔ አአዪ ኢኢቲ በአወ። | ክፉ ለክፉ ቀን ይደርሳል፤ የውኃ ክፉና የእናት ክፉ የለም። | Even something harsh saves you in hard times; there is no bad water or bad mother. |
| Tosse (Iita Bagga / Critiquing Evil, p. 40) | **Iiti iissees, curqqi shaallees; Iitabay zin77i qakkees; Ixxiyaa imathi laappun qamma aqees.** | ኢኢቲ ኢኢስሴእስ፣ ጩርቅቂ ሻአልሌእስ፤ ኢኢተበይ ዚንዕዒ ቀክኬእስ፤ ኢጥጢየአ ኢመꬂ ለአፕፑን ቀምመ አቄእስ። | ክፋት ያጋጫል፣ ጭቃ ያቆሽሻል፤ ክፉ ሥራ እንቅልፍ ይነሳል፤ የተጠላ እንግዳ ሰባት ሌሊት ያድራል። | Malice ruins relationships as mud stains; evil deeds ruin sleep; an unwelcome guest stays seven nights. |
| Tosse (Deep Figurative Proverbs, p. 41) | **Iras gelidi kaho gishees; Care wozanan yedhdhin oqane uloy sakkees; Yessi be7i yeekkees, missi be7i bookkees.** | ኢረስ ጌሊዲ ከሆ ጊሼእስ፤ ጨሬ ዎዘነን ዬꬍꬊን ኦቀኔ ኡሎይ ሰክኬእስ፤ ዬስሲ ቤዒ ዬእክኬእስ፣ ሚስሲ ቤዒ ቦኦክኬእስ። | ከዝናብ ሸሽቶ ወደ ዶፍ መግባት፤ ቂም በልብ ሲያዝ ውስጡን ያሳምማል፤ ሰው ሲያለቅስ አይቶ ማልቀስ። | Fleeing rain into a downpour; stepping on a thorn in the heart makes the stomach ache; crying merely because others cry. |
| Tosse (Meeze 2 Matching Proverbs, p. 42) | **Paraa dirguwaan harey hayqqees; Diccana na7i gufanthin diccees; Godaa ammanida dorssiyaa goynaa karen ayssawus; Ellesoy gam7on gathees; Ammano mashshay tiriyaan me7ees; Eqqi caddanaw hokki zuummees.** | ፐረአ ዲርጉወአን ሀሬይ ሀይቅቄእስ፤ ዲጭጨነ ነዒ ጉፈንꬂን ዲጭጬእስ፤ ጎደአ አምመኒደ ዶርስሲየአ ጎይነአ ከሬን አይስሰዉስ፤ እልሌሶይ ገምዖን ገꬄእስ፤ አምመኖ መሽሻይ ቲሪየአን ሜዔእስ፤ እቅቂ ጨድደነው ሆክኪ ዙኡምሜእስ። | በፈረሶች ግርግር አህያ ይሞታል፤ የሚያድግ ልጅ እየተደናቀፈ ያድጋል፤ ጌታዋን የተማመነች በግ ላቷን ውጭ ታሳድራለች፤ ችኮላ ወደ መዘግየት ያደርሳል፤ የእምነት ቢላዋ በጉበት ውስጥ ይሰበራል፤ ቆሞ ለመውጋት ጎንበስ ብሎ መጠጋት። | In the stampede of horses the donkey dies; a growing child grows by stumbling; the sheep that trusts its master leaves its tail outside; haste leads to delay; a knife of betrayal breaks in the liver; to strike standing, one first crouches. |
| Sedhemenkke (Riddles, p. 45) | **Sedhemenkke (p. 45): (1) Na7iyaa hello guppaws aayiyaa tira harggaws -> Tuutenne udula (pestle & mortar); (2) Bollay tama garssay tama giddoy shemppo ashshiyaaba -> Difo daabbo (baked bread); (3) Kareetha miiziya bootha marata kaalethawus -> Ayfe tukke sinenne zhabana (black coffee pot & white cups).** | ሴꬌሜንክኬ (ፕ። 45)፦ (1) ነዒየአ ሄልሎ ጉፕፐውስ አአዪየአ ቲረ ሀርግገውስ -> ቱኡቴንኔ ኡዱለ (ፔስትሌ & ሞርተር)፤ (2) ቦልለይ ተመ ገርስሰይ ተመ ጊድዶይ ሼምፕፖ አሽሺየአበ -> ዲፎ ደአብቦ (በኬድ ብሬአድ)፤ (3) ከሬእꬃ ሚኢዚየ ቦኦꬃ መረተ ከአሌꬃዉስ -> አይፌ ቱክኬ ሲኔንኔ ዣበነ (ብለጭክ ጮፍፌእ ፖት & ውሂቴ ጩፕስ)። | እንቆቅልሾች (ገጽ 45)፦ (1) ልጅቷ ስትዘል እናትየው ደረቷን ያማታል -> ሙቀጫና ዘነዘና፤ (2) ላዩ እሳት ታቹ እሳት መሃሉ ነፍስ የሚያድን -> ድፎ ዳቦ፤ (3) ጥቁር ላም ነጭ ጥጃዎችን ትመራለች -> ጀበናና ሲኒዎች። | Riddles (p. 45): (1) Daughter jumps while mother's chest aches -> Pestle and mortar; (2) Fire above, fire below, life-saving food inside -> Baked difo bread; (3) Black cow leading white calves -> Black clay coffee pot and white cups. |
| Qan77ishe (Poetic Duels, pp. 46–47) | **Qan77ishe (pp. 46–47): (1) Ta booraa Shannoy shaafi qocees -> Shaaqanchoy nena gelanaw huuphe pixees; (2) Qalqqaaluwada qaamma -> Qaalichaa nagiriitaada guumma; (3) Woxxashe woxxashe wolaythi gakka -> Harggashe harggashe meqethi atta.** | ቀንዕዒሼ (ፕፕ። 46–47)፦ (1) ተ ቦኦረአ ሻንኖይ ሻአፊ ቆጬእስ -> ሻአቀንቾይ ኔነ ጌለነው ሁኡጴ ፒጤእስ፤ (2) ቀልቅቀአሉወደ ቀአምመ -> ቀአሊቻአ ነጊሪኢተአደ ጉኡምመ፤ (3) ዎጥጠሼ ዎጥጠሼ ዎለይꬂ ገክከ -> ሀርግገሼ ሀርግገሼ ሜቄꬂ አትተ። | ቃንጪሼ (ገጽ 46–47)፦ (1) ኮርማዬ ሻኖ ወንዝ ይመታል -> እጮኛሽ አንቺን ለማግባት ፀጉሩን ያበጥራል፤ (2) እንደ ቆዳ ቃሚ -> እንደ ቃልቻ ነጋሪት አጉረምርሚ፤ (3) እየሮጥክ ወላይታ ድረስ -> እየታመምክ አጥንት ቅር። | Qan77ishe Couplets (pp. 46–47): Traditional rhyming verbal duels. |

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 5 (Qan77ishe Dabbotho, p. 47): Match Qan77ishe prompts in Column A with traditional rhyming responses in Column B: 4. Qan77ishey qasheekko lagee -> F. Qacini harppa lagee; 6. Shaafan shako -> C. Ne sharqqiyaan danqqo; 7. Qalqqalaada qaamma -> B. Qaalicha nagiriitaada guumma; 10. Woxxashe woxxashe wolaythi gakka -> G. Harggashe harggashe meqethi atta.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 5 (ቀንዕዒሼ ደብቦꬆ፣ ፕ። 47)፦ መትች ቀንዕዒሼ ፕሮምፕትስ ኢን ጮሉምን አ ዊꬅ ትረዲቲኦነል ርህይሚንግ ሬስፖንሴስ ኢን ጮሉምን ብ፦ 4። ቀንዕዒሼይ ቀሼእክኮ ለጌእ -> ፍ። ቀጪኒ ሀርፕፐ ለጌእ፤ 6። ሻአፈን ሻኮ -> ጭ። ኔ ሻርቅቂየአን ደንቅቆ፤ 7። ቀልቅቀለአደ ቀአምመ -> ብ። ቀአሊቻ ነጊሪኢተአደ ጉኡምመ፤ 10። ዎጥጠሼ ዎጥጠሼ ዎለይꬂ ገክከ -> ግ። ሀርግገሼ ሀርግገሼ ሜቄꬂ አትተ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 5 (ቃንጪሼ ማዛመድ፣ ገጽ 47)፦ በ'A' ስር የቀረቡትን የቃንጪሼ መነሻዎች በ'B' ስር ካሉት ምላሾች ጋር ማዛመድ።
- **English:** Exercise 5 (Qan77ishe Matching, p. 47): Match each traditional Qan77ishe opening line with its canonical rhyming response.

### Gr10_Ch6 — Shemppo 6: Bayrata Ooso Gisho | ሼምፕፖ 6፦ በይረተ ኦኦሶ ጊሾ | ምዕራፍ 6፦ የሽማግሌዎች (የታላላቆች) ባህላዊ የዕርቅና የሰላም ሚና | Chapter 6: Role of Elders in Indigenous Conflict Resolution, Letter Writing (Dabddaabbe), and Verb Transitivity
- **Pages:** Book pp. 43–56 (PDF pp. 48–61) • **Source:** [Grade 10 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1mKIcfMoMgnZ9Pa657NWmE-OGND2wlveM/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Dondzatas Gade Woga Saro Medhon De7iya Gishaddetethaa* (ዶንꬓተስ ገዴ ዎገ ሰሮ ሜꬎን ዴዒየ ጊሻድዴቴꬃአ) — ሽማግሌዎች በባህላዊ የሰላምና የዕርቅ ሥርዓት ውስጥ ያላቸው ሚና / The Role of Elders in Indigenous Conflict Resolution Across Ethiopia, SNNPR, and Gofa Zone (pp. 49–51 (Book pp. 44–46))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Hessa mela doorettidi giigisiyaa dondzati Amaara heeran Shimaagille, Oroomo heeran Jaarsa, Sidaama heeran Gudumaale geetettidi xeegettoosona. | ሄስሰ ሜለ ዶኦሬትቲዲ ጊኢጊሲየአ ዶንꬓቲ አመአረ ሄእረን ሺመአጊልሌ፣ ኦሮኦሞ ሄእረን ጀአርሰ፣ ሲደአመ ሄእረን ጉዱመአሌ ጌእቴትቲዲ ጤእጌትቶኦሶነ። | እንዲህ ተመርጠው የሚያስታርቁ ሽማግሌዎች በአማራ አካባቢ ሽማግሌ፣ በኦሮሞ አካባቢ ጃርሳ፣ በሲዳማ አካባቢ ጉዱማሌ ተብለው ይጠራሉ። | Elders chosen to mediate disputes are called Shimaagille in Amhara, Jaarsa in Oromo, and Gudumaale in Sidama. |
| Nu kililiyaan xeelliyaa wode: Burji heeran Woma, Baskketon Baalabbaate, Guraagen Kicha, Konson Mora, Gamoninne Goofan Dubbushanne Gozdda geetettidi sarotethaa medhdhishe gam77idosona. | ኑ ኪሊሊየአን ጤእልሊየአ ዎዴ፦ ቡርጂ ሄእረን ዎመ፣ በስክኬቶን በአለብበአቴ፣ ጉረአጌን ኪቻ፣ ኮንሶን ሞረ፣ ገሞኒንኔ ጎኦፈን ዱብቡሻንኔ ጎዝድደ ጌእቴትቲዲ ሰሮቴꬃአ ሜꬍꬊሼ ገምዕዒዶሶነ። | በክልላችን ሲታይ፦ በቡርጂ ወማ፣ በባስኬቶ ባላባቴ፣ በጉራጌ ኪቻ፣ በኮንሶ ሞራ፣ በጋሞና በጎፋ ደግሞ ዱቡሻ እና ጎዝዳ ተብለው ሰላም ሲፈጥሩ ቆይተዋል። | In our region, indigenous peace councils include Woma in Burji, Baalabbaate in Basketo, Kicha in Gurage, Mora in Konso, and Dubbusha and Gozdda in Gamo and Gofa. |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 2 (6.B Haasaya Palama, p. 49): Formal debates on 4 topics: 1. Mutual respect vs. harming others; 2. Communal dining and sharing; 3. Honoring elders (Bayrata bonchcho); 4. Indigenous conflict resolution (Gade wogan giigiso) vs. modern court litigation.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 2 (6።ብ ሀአሰየ ፐለመ፣ ፕ። 49)፦ ፎርመል ዴበቴስ ኦን 4 ቶፒጭስ፦ 1። ሙቱአል ሬስፔጭት ቭስ። ሀርሚንግ ኦꬄርስ፤ 2። ጮምሙነል ዲኒንግ አንድ ሻሪንግ፤ 3። ሆኖሪንግ እልዴርስ (በይረተ ቦንችቾ)፤ 4። ኢንዲጌኖኡስ ጮንፍሊጭት ሬሶሉቲኦን (ገዴ ዎገን ጊኢጊሶ) ቭስ። ሞዴርን ጮኡርት ሊቲገቲኦን።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 2 (6.B የክርክር ርዕሶች፣ ገጽ 49)፦ 1. እርስ በርስ መከባበር፤ 2. አብሮ መብላትና መጠጣት፤ 3. ታላላቆችን ማክበር፤ 4. ባህላዊ የዕርቅ ሥርዓት ከዘመናዊ ፍርድ ቤት ጋር ሲነጻጸር።
- **English:** Exercise 2 (6.B Classroom Debates, p. 49): Debate the merits of indigenous elder mediation (Dubbusha/Gozdda) compared to formal court litigation.

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **dubbusha** | ዱብቡሻ | `N` | woga shiiqo / pirdda bessa | ዱቡሻ (የጎፋና የጋሞ ባህላዊ የሽማግሌዎች የዕርቅና የሸንጎ አደባባይ) | traditional public assembly ground for elder mediation in Gofa and Gamo |
| **gozdda** | ጎዝድደ | `N` | Goofa woga cimata cuga | ጎዝዳ (በጎፋ የባህልና የዕርቅ ሽማግሌዎች ቡድን/ማዕረግ) | traditional council of senior elders and peacemakers in Gofa |
| **dabddaabbe** | ደብድደአብቤ | `N` | kiita waraqqata | ደብዳቤ (የግል ደብዳቤ / Buzo dabddaabbe እና የሥራ ደብዳቤ / Ooso dabddaabbe) | letter (personal letter and official business/application letter) |
| **pinniyaa peesho** | ፒንኒየአ ፔእሾ | `NP` | kaalodde koyiya peesho | ተሻጋሪ ግስ (ተሳቢ/Kaalodde የሚፈልግ ግስ) | transitive verb (requires a direct object / kaalodde) |
| **pinnonna peesho** | ፒንኖንነ ፔእሾ | `NP` | kaalodde koyonna peesho | ኢ-ተሻጋሪ ግስ (ተሳቢ የማይፈልግ ግስ) | intransitive verb (does not take a direct object) |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** 1. Dabddaabbe Xaafo Maara (Personal & Official Letter Writing, 6.D Xaafo, pp. 52–59): (a) Buzo dabddaabbe (Personal letter: sender address, date, greeting 'Ta siiqo...', body [Gelo, Gomppa, Kuushshi], sign-off, recipient address) with full model letters on pp. 53 & 55; (b) Ooso dabddaabbe & Ooso koshsha dabddaabbe (Official & Job Application Letters, pp. 56–59) with reference number (Paydo), date (Gaagla), subject line (Yohoy), stamp (Maateme), and CC ( Garssa/Gakko).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 1። ደብድደአብቤ ጠአፎ መአረ (ፔርሶነል & ኦፍፊጪአል ሌትቴር ውሪቲንግ፣ 6።ድ ጠአፎ፣ ፕፕ። 52–59)፦ (አ) ቡዞ ደብድደአብቤ (ፔርሶነል ሌትቴር፦ ሴንዴር አድድሬስስ፣ ደቴ፣ ግሬእቲንግ 'ተ ሲኢቆ።።።'፣ ቦድይ [ጌሎ፣ ጎምፕፐ፣ ኩኡሽሺ]፣ ሲግን-ኦፍፍ፣ ሬጪፒእንት አድድሬስስ) ዊꬅ ፉልል ሞዴል ሌትቴርስ ኦን ፕፕ። 53 & 55፤ (ብ) ኦኦሶ ደብድደአብቤ & ኦኦሶ ኮሽሻ ደብድደአብቤ (ኦፍፊጪአል & ጆብ አፕፕሊጨቲኦን ሌትቴርስ፣ ፕፕ። 56–59) ዊꬅ ሬፌሬንጬ ኑምቤር (ፐይዶ)፣ ደቴ (ገአግለ)፣ ሱብጄጭት ሊኔ (ዮሆይ)፣ ስተምፕ (መአቴሜ)፣ አንድ ጭጭ ( ገርስሰ/ገክኮ)።
- **Amharic:** 1. የደብዳቤ አጻጻፍ ሥርዓት (6.D Xaafo፣ ገጽ 52–59)፦ (ሀ) የግል ደብዳቤ (Buzo dabddaabbe) አካላትና የናሙና ደብዳቤዎች፤ (ለ) የመሥሪያ ቤትና የሥራ ማመልከቻ ደብዳቤ (Ooso dabddaabbe) አካላት (ቁጥር፣ ቀን፣ ጉዳዩ፣ ማኅተም፣ ግልባጭ)።
- **English:** 1. Formal and Personal Letter Writing Conventions in Gofatho (6.D Xaafo, pp. 52–59): Complete templates and authentic sample letters (e.g., letter to 'Goofa Zoone Timirtte Beero, Sawla' on p. 58).
- **Gofatho Latin:** 2. Pinniyaa Peesho (Transitive Verbs) vs. Pinnonna Peesho (Intransitive Verbs) (6.F Qawosa, pp. 60–61): Pinniyaa peesho transfers action from Yohaadde (Subject) to Kaalodde (Object): 'Gawariyaa eceriwu wodhasu' ('The cat killed the mouse'); 'Ta na7aawu kathaa ma' ('My child, eat the food'). Pinnonna peesho has no direct object: 'Nuuni Sawlan de7oos' ('We live in Sawla'); 'Awi dolohara keyees' ('The sun rises in the east'); 'Tami eexxees' ('The fire burns'). Ambivalent verbs (e.g., 'so77is' / 'feared').
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 2። ፒንኒየአ ፔእሾ (ትረንሲቲቬ ቬርብስ) ቭስ። ፒንኖንነ ፔእሾ (ኢንትረንሲቲቬ ቬርብስ) (6።ፍ ቀዎሰ፣ ፕፕ። 60–61)፦ ፒንኒየአ ፔእሾ ትረንስፌርስ አጭቲኦን ፍሮም ዮሀአድዴ (ሱብጄጭት) ቶ ከአሎድዴ (ኦብጄጭት)፦ 'ገወሪየአ እጬሪዉ ዎꬋሱ' ('ꬄ ጨት ኪልሌድ ꬄ ሞኡሴ')፤ 'ተ ነዓአዉ ከꬃአ መ' ('ምይ ቺልድ፣ እአት ꬄ ፎኦድ')። ፒንኖንነ ፔእሾ ሀስ ኖ ዲሬጭት ኦብጄጭት፦ 'ኑኡኒ ሰውለን ዴዖኦስ' ('ዌ ሊቬ ኢን ሰውለ')፤ 'አዊ ዶሎሀረ ኬዬእስ' ('ꬄ ሱን ሪሴስ ኢን ꬄ እአስት')፤ 'ተሚ እእጥጤእስ' ('ꬄ ፊሬ ቡርንስ')። አምቢቨሌንት ቬርብስ (እ።ግ።፣ 'ሶዕዒስ' / 'ፌአሬድ')።
- **Amharic:** 2. ተሻጋሪ ግስ (Pinniyaa Peesho) እና ኢ-ተሻጋሪ ግስ (Pinnonna Peesho) (6.F Qawosa፣ ገጽ 60–61)፦ ተሻጋሪ ግስ ድርጊቱ ከባለቤት (Yohaadde) ወደ ተሳቢ (Kaalodde) የሚሻገርበት ነው (ምሳሌ፦ 'ድመቷ አይጧን ገደለች')፤ ኢ-ተሻጋሪ ግስ ደግሞ ተሳቢ አይፈልግም (ምሳሌ፦ 'እኛ ሳውላ እንኖራለን'፣ 'ፀሐይ በምሥራቅ ትወጣለች')።
- **English:** 2. Transitive (Pinniyaa) vs. Intransitive (Pinnonna) Verbs (6.F Qawosa, pp. 60–61): Defines verbal valency and direct-object licensing in Gofatho.

#### E. Proverbs, Idioms, Riddles & Oral Literature (`Leemiso / Tosse / Aaqeta / Sedhemenkke / Qan77ishe`)
| Category | Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Tosse (Proverb, p. 51) | **Dosin ixxin cimi shi7a muzees.** | ዶሲን ኢጥጢን ጪሚ ሺዓ ሙዜእስ። | ወደድክም ጠላህም ሽማግሌ ያስታርቃል (የሽማግሌ ቃል አይታጠፍም)። | Whether one likes it or not, an elder's mediation prevails. |

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 3 (6.C Nabbabo Tuma/Worddo, p. 51): 2. Oroomo dere asata heeran giigisiya asati Gaada (Jaarsa) geetettoosona. 6. Nu Goofa heeran asay pirdde soo baanaape cimara giigeyssa dosoosona -> Zaaro: Tuma. 8. Amaara dere asaa heeran giigisiyaa asati Gudumaale geetettoosona -> Zaaro: Worddo (Amaara heeran Shimaagille, Sidaama heeran Gudumaale).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 3 (6።ጭ ነብበቦ ቱመ/ዎርድዶ፣ ፕ። 51)፦ 2። ኦሮኦሞ ዴሬ አሰተ ሄእረን ጊኢጊሲየ አሰቲ ገአደ (ጀአርሰ) ጌእቴትቶኦሶነ። 6። ኑ ጎኦፈ ሄእረን አሰይ ፒርድዴ ሶኦ በአነአፔ ጪመረ ጊኢጌይስሰ ዶሶኦሶነ -> ዘአሮ፦ ቱመ። 8። አመአረ ዴሬ አሰአ ሄእረን ጊኢጊሲየአ አሰቲ ጉዱመአሌ ጌእቴትቶኦሶነ -> ዘአሮ፦ ዎርድዶ (አመአረ ሄእረን ሺመአጊልሌ፣ ሲደአመ ሄእረን ጉዱመአሌ)።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 3 (እውነት/ሐሰት፣ ገጽ 51)፦ 6. በጎፋ አካባቢ ሕዝቡ ወደ ፍርድ ቤት ከመሄድ ይልቅ በሽማግሌ መታረቅን ይመርጣል -> እውነት (Tuma)። 8. በአማራ አካባቢ አስታራቂዎች ጉዱማሌ ይባላሉ -> ሐሰት (Worddo)።
- **English:** Exercise 3 (True/False, p. 51): 6. In Gofa, people prefer reconciling through elders over going to court -> True (Tuma). 8. Mediators in Amhara are called Gudumaale -> False (Worddo; Shimaagille in Amhara, Gudumaale in Sidama).

### Gr10_Ch7 — Shemppo 7: Adussa Medh-Darsso (Novel) | ሼምፕፖ 7፦ አዱስሰ ሜꬍ-ደርስሶ | ምዕራፍ 7፦ ረጅም ልቦለድ (የልቦለድ አላባዊያን፣ ግጭትና የትረካ አንጻር) | Chapter 7: The Novel (Extended Fiction, Plot, Physical vs. Psychological Conflict, and Narrative Point of View)
- **Pages:** Book pp. 57–67 (PDF pp. 62–72) • **Source:** [Grade 10 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1mKIcfMoMgnZ9Pa657NWmE-OGND2wlveM/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Adussa Medh-Darsso (Novel), De7i-Darsso, Yarata & Haasgina ('Dada', 'Darota' & 'Biqqala' Excerpts)* (አዱስሰ ሜꬍ-ደርስሶ (ኖቬል)፣ ዴዒ-ደርስሶ፣ የረተ & ሀአስጊነ ('ደደ'፣ 'ደሮተ' & 'ቢቅቀለ' እጥጬርፕትስ)) — ረጅም ልቦለድ፣ አላባዊያንና የትረካ አንጻር (ከ'ዳዳ'፣ 'ዳሮታ' እና 'ቢቃላ' ልቦለዶች የተወሰዱ ምሳሌዎች) / Theory of the Novel and Literary Analysis (with Excerpts from 'Dada', 'Darota', and Ayyala Gabayo's 'Biqqala') (pp. 62–71 (Book pp. 57–66))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Issi adussa medh-darssoy pana dandda7ida issi gita maxaafe gidanaw dandda7ees; unddenna sinthinne issi ishatamu sha77u (50,000) gaso oykkiya qaalan xaafettanaw dandda7ees. | ኢስሲ አዱስሰ ሜꬍ-ደርስሶይ ፐነ ደንድደዒደ ኢስሲ ጊተ መጠአፌ ጊደነው ደንድደዔእስ፤ ኡንድዴንነ ሲንꬂንኔ ኢስሲ ኢሻተሙ ሻዕዑ (50፣000) ገሶ ኦይክኪየ ቀአለን ጠአፌትተነው ደንድደዔእስ። | አንድ ረጅም ልቦለድ ራሱን የቻለ አንድ ትልቅ መጽሐፍ ሊሆን ይችላል፤ በብዙ ገጾችና እስከ አምሳ ሺህ (50,000) በሚደርሱ ቃላት ሊጻፍ ይችላል። | A novel is a full-length book that can span many pages and 50,000 or more words, whereas a short story (invented in the 19th century by Edgar Allan Poe) typically spans 500–1,500 words and can be read in one sitting. |
| Nam77u dom77e kochchati: Asho Dom77e (physical conflict) nne Kaha Dom77e (psychological conflict); koyro qassi Asi asara medhdhiya dom77e, Asanne medheta giddon dom77e, nne Kaha giddo dom77e. | ነምዕዑ ዶምዕዔ ኮችቻቲ፦ አሾ ዶምዕዔ (ጵይሲጨል ጮንፍሊጭት) ንኔ ከሀ ዶምዕዔ (ፕስይቾሎጊጨል ጮንፍሊጭት)፤ ኮይሮ ቀስሲ አሲ አሰረ ሜꬍꬊየ ዶምዕዔ፣ አሰንኔ ሜꬌተ ጊድዶን ዶምዕዔ፣ ንኔ ከሀ ጊድዶ ዶምዕዔ። | ሁለቱ የግጭት ዓይነቶች፦ አካላዊ ግጭት (Asho dom77e) እና ሥነ-ልቦናዊ ግጭት (Kaha dom77e)፤ እንዲሁም ሰው ከሰው፣ ሰው ከተፈጥሮ፣ እና ሰው ከራሱ ኅሊና ጋር የሚያደርገው ግጭት። | Conflict types include Physical Conflict (Asho dom77e) and Psychological Conflict (Kaha dom77e), manifesting as person-against-person, person-against-nature, and internal conflict. |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** First-Person & Dramatic Narrative Excerpts from Ayyala Gabayo's Novel 'Biqqala' (pp. 69–70): Showcasing Koyro Moode Haasgina ('Taani yaanas; nuuni yaanida') and Diraama Haasgina (dialogue-driven dramatic narration).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ፊርስት-ፔርሶን & ድረመቲጭ ነርረቲቬ እጥጬርፕትስ ፍሮም አይየለ ገበዮ'ስ ኖቬል 'ቢቅቀለ' (ፕፕ። 69–70)፦ ሾውጨሲንግ ኮይሮ ሞኦዴ ሀአስጊነ ('ተአኒ የአነስ፤ ኑኡኒ የአኒደ') አንድ ዲረአመ ሀአስጊነ (ዲአሎጉእ-ድሪቬን ድረመቲጭ ነርረቲኦን)።
- **Amharic:** ከአያሌው ገበየሁ 'ቢቃላ' ልቦለድ የተወሰዱ የአንደኛ መደብና የተውኔታዊ አንጻር ምልልሶች (ገጽ 69–70)።
- **English:** Literary Excerpts from Ayyala Gabayo's Novel 'Biqqala' (pp. 69–70): Illustrating First-Person Point of View (Koyro Moode Haasgina) and Dramatic Point of View (Diraama Haasgina).

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **adussa medh-darsso** | አዱስሰ ሜꬍ-ደርስሶ | `NP` | gita medh-darsso | ረጅም ልቦለድ (ኖቭል) | novel / extended prose fiction |
| **de7i-darsso** | ዴዒ-ደርስሶ | `N` | beettiyabaa bolla katidi xaafettiya xuufe | ኢ-ልቦለድ (በእውነተኛ ታሪክ፣ ደብዳቤ ወይም የሕይወት ታሪክ ላይ የተመሠረተ ጽሑፍ) | non-fiction (history, biography, textbook, letters) |
| **asho dom77e** | አሾ ዶምዕዔ | `NP` | asatetha bute | አካላዊ ግጭት | physical conflict |
| **kaha dom77e** | ከሀ ዶምዕዔ | `NP` | gaatha qofa dom77e | ሥነ-ልቦናዊ (የውስጥ/የኅሊና) ግጭት | psychological / internal conflict |
| **haasgina / xeelli-gina** | ሀአስጊነ / ጤእልሊ-ጊነ | `N` | adarssanchay taarikiya odiya oge | የትረካ አንጻር (Point of View) | narrative point of view |
| **wurssi-eraadde haasgina** | ዉርስሲ-እረአድዴ ሀአስጊነ | `NP` | ubbaa wurssi eriya haysaadde | ሁሉን አዋቂ የትረካ አንጻር (Omniscient POV) | omniscient third-person point of view |
| **xii-eraadde / sima eraadde haasgina** | ጢኢ-እረአድዴ / ሲመ እረአድዴ ሀአስጊነ | `NP` | bagga eriya haysaadde | ውሱን አዋቂ የትረካ አንጻር (Limited POV) | limited third-person point of view |
| **koyro moode haasgina** | ኮይሮ ሞኦዴ ሀአስጊነ | `NP` | taani/nuuni gishe odiya haasgina | የአንደኛ መደብ የትረካ አንጻር ('እኔ/እኛ' ብሎ የሚተርክ) | first-person point of view ('Taani / Nuuni') |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Literary & Narrative Grammar: Pronominal Person (Koyro Moode 1S 'Taani/tana', 1PL 'Nuuni/nuna' vs. Heedzantho Moode 3S/3PL) & 7 Principles of Character Construction (Odaawa Medho, pp. 68–71).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሊቴረርይ & ነርረቲቬ ግረምመር፦ ፕሮኖሚነል ፔርሶን (ኮይሮ ሞኦዴ 1ስ 'ተአኒ/ተነ'፣ 1ፕል 'ኑኡኒ/ኑነ' ቭስ። ሄእꬓንꬆ ሞኦዴ 3ስ/3ፕል) & 7 ፕሪንጪፕሌስ ኦፍ ቻረጭቴር ጮንስትሩጭቲኦን (ኦደአወ ሜꬎ፣ ፕፕ። 68–71)።
- **Amharic:** የትረካ መደቦች (አንደኛ መደብ ነጠላ 'Taani'፣ ብዙ 'Nuuni' እና ሦስተኛ መደብ) እንዲሁም የገጸ-ባህሪ አሳሳል 7 መርሆዎች (ገጽ 68–71)።
- **English:** Grammatical Person in Narrative Point of View (1st Person Singular 'Taani', 1st Person Plural 'Nuuni', and 3rd Person Omniscient/Limited/Dramatic) and 7 Principles of Characterization (pp. 68–71).

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 1 (Tuma/Worddo, p. 64): 4. Diraami ubba wode ginxxon xaafettees -> Zaaro: Worddo (Yedan xaafettida diraami kumi attis). Meeze 4 (Tuma/Worddo, p. 70): 4. Wurssi-eraadde haasgini issi taariken 'Taani' woy 'Nuuni' gishe odes -> Zaaro: Worddo ('Taani/Nuuni' geyssi koyro moode haasgina).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 1 (ቱመ/ዎርድዶ፣ ፕ። 64)፦ 4። ዲረአሚ ኡብበ ዎዴ ጊንጥጦን ጠአፌትቴእስ -> ዘአሮ፦ ዎርድዶ (ዬደን ጠአፌትቲደ ዲረአሚ ኩሚ አትቲስ)። ሜእዜ 4 (ቱመ/ዎርድዶ፣ ፕ። 70)፦ 4። ዉርስሲ-እረአድዴ ሀአስጊኒ ኢስሲ ተአሪኬን 'ተአኒ' ዎይ 'ኑኡኒ' ጊሼ ኦዴስ -> ዘአሮ፦ ዎርድዶ ('ተአኒ/ኑኡኒ' ጌይስሲ ኮይሮ ሞኦዴ ሀአስጊነ)።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 1 እና 4 (እውነት/ሐሰት፣ ገጽ 64 እና 70)፦ 4. ድራማ ሁልጊዜ በግጥም ይጻፋል -> ሐሰት (በዝርው የተጻፈ ድራማ ብዙ ነው)። 4. ሁሉን አዋቂ ተራኪ 'እኔ/እኛ' እያለ ይተርካል -> ሐሰት (እርሱ አንደኛ መደብ ነው)።
- **English:** Exercises 1 & 4 (True/False, pp. 64, 70): 4. Drama is always written in verse -> False. 4. An omniscient narrator narrates using 'I' or 'We' -> False (that is First-Person POV).

### Gr10_Ch8 — Shemppo 8: Deriya Gasethiyaa Miisheta (Social Media) | ሼምፕፖ 8፦ ዴሪየ ገሴꬂየአ ሚኢሼተ | ምዕራፍ 8፦ ማኅበራዊ ሚዲያ፣ አከራካሪ ድርሰትና የግስ ጊዜያት | Chapter 8: Social Media, Argumentative Essay ('Muussinne Ushshi'), and Verb Tenses (Aadhode, Ha7ode, Sinthode)
- **Pages:** Book pp. 68–75 (PDF pp. 73–80) • **Source:** [Grade 10 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1mKIcfMoMgnZ9Pa657NWmE-OGND2wlveM/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Deriyaa Gasethiya Miisheta (Social Media) & Model Essay 'Muussinne Ushshi'* (ዴሪየአ ገሴꬂየ ሚኢሼተ (ሶጪአል ሜዲአ) & ሞዴል እስሰይ 'ሙኡስሲንኔ ኡሽሺ') — ማኅበራዊ ሚዲያ (Social Media) እና 'ምግብና መጠጥ' (የናሙና አከራካሪ ድርሰት) / Social Media (Definitions by Verdegen 2011, Auvinen 2011, UNESCO 2011) and Model Argumentative Essay 'Muussinne Ushshi' (pp. 74–78 (Book pp. 69–73))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Dumma dumma dere miidiyaa qommoti de7ishin hessatape nam77atanne bertteti: deriyaa barbaxiyaa bessata (social networking sites: Peesibuuke, Telegiraame) nne kesota woy biidota laamerettiyaa bessata (content/video sites: Yuutuube). | ዱምመ ዱምመ ዴሬ ሚኢዲየአ ቆምሞቲ ዴዒሺን ሄስሰተፔ ነምዕዓተንኔ ቤርትቴቲ፦ ዴሪየአ በርበጢየአ ቤስሰተ (ሶጪአል ኔትዎርኪንግ ሲቴስ፦ ፔእሲቡኡኬ፣ ቴሌጊረአሜ) ንኔ ኬሶተ ዎይ ቢኢዶተ ለአሜሬትቲየአ ቤስሰተ (ጮንቴንት/ቪዴኦ ሲቴስ፦ ዩኡቱኡቤ)። | ከተለያዩ የማኅበራዊ ሚዲያ ዓይነቶች መካከል ሁለቱ ዋና ዋናዎቹ፦ ማኅበረሰብን የሚያስተሳስሩ ድረ-ገጾች (ፌስቡክ፣ ቴሌግራም) እና ምስልና ድምፅ (ቪዲዮ) የሚለዋወጡባቸው ድረ-ገጾች (ዩቲዩብ) ናቸው። | Two major categories of social media are social networking sites (deriyaa barbaxiyaa bessata, e.g., Facebook, Telegram) and audio-visual content-sharing sites (e.g., YouTube). |
| Muussinne Ushshi (Palantho Adarsso, pp. 77–78): 'Miday bidaa gakkees' gees Goofatho tossey... 'Uloy kallin pili cammees' giya tossey coo mela tossettibeenna; 'Yaare muussi bini qaallis ulo daakkis' gees. | ሙኡስሲንኔ ኡሽሺ (ፐለንꬆ አደርስሶ፣ ፕፕ። ዕዕ–78)፦ 'ሚደይ ቢደአ ገክኬእስ' ጌእስ ጎኦፈꬆ ቶስሴይ።።። 'ኡሎይ ከልሊን ፒሊ ጨምሜእስ' ጊየ ቶስሴይ ጮኦ ሜለ ቶስሴትቲቤእንነ፤ 'የአሬ ሙኡስሲ ቢኒ ቀአልሊስ ኡሎ ደአክኪስ' ጌእስ። | 'ምግብና መጠጥ' (አከራካሪ ድርሰት፣ ገጽ 77–78)፦ 'የበላ የሄደውን ይደርሳል' ይላል የጎፋኛ ምሳሌ... 'ሆድ ሲጠግብ አንጎል (ቅልጥም) ይመራል' የሚለው ምሳሌ በከንቱ አልተነገረም። | From the model argumentative essay 'Food and Drink' (pp. 77–78): Contrasts the dangers of excessive alcohol and gluttony using classic Gofatho proverbs. |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 2 (8.B Haasaya Palama, p. 74): Four classroom debates: 1. Telebizhine vs. Iraadoone; 2. Moobayle vs. Soo silkke (Mobile phone vs. landline); 3. Postta vs. Imeele (Postal mail vs. Email); 4. Beni wode miidiya vs. Ha77i wode miidiya.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 2 (8።ብ ሀአሰየ ፐለመ፣ ፕ። 74)፦ ፎኡር ጭለስስሮኦም ዴበቴስ፦ 1። ቴሌቢዢኔ ቭስ። ኢረአዶኦኔ፤ 2። ሞኦበይሌ ቭስ። ሶኦ ሲልክኬ (ሞቢሌ ፆኔ ቭስ። ለንድሊኔ)፤ 3። ፖስትተ ቭስ። ኢሜእሌ (ፖስተል መኢል ቭስ። እመኢል)፤ 4። ቤኒ ዎዴ ሚኢዲየ ቭስ። ሀዕዒ ዎዴ ሚኢዲየ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 2 (8.B የክርክር ርዕሶች፣ ገጽ 74)፦ 1. ቴሌቪዥንና ሬዲዮ፤ 2. ሞባይል ስልክና የቤት ስልክ፤ 3. ፖስታና ኢሜይል፤ 4. የድሮና የዘመኑ ሚዲያ።
- **English:** Exercise 2 (8.B Structured Debates, p. 74): Debate Television vs. Radio, Mobile Phone vs. Landline, Postal Mail vs. Email, and Traditional vs. Modern Media.

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **deriya gasethiyaa miisheta** | ዴሪየ ገሴꬂየአ ሚኢሼተ | `NP` | dere miidiya | ማኅበራዊ ሚዲያ (Social Media) | social media platforms |
| **dem-bessa** | ዴም-ቤስሰ | `N` | intterneete bessa | ድረ-ገጽ (Web / Website) | website / web platform |
| **barbaxo / barbbaxetetha** | በርበጦ / በርብበጤቴꬃ | `N` | neetworkke / woli gatho | ትስስር / ኔትዎርክ (Networking) | networking / interconnection |
| **denthi-woso** | ዴንꬂ-ዎሶ | `N` | miidiya bolla woso | መጫን / ማውረድ (Upload / Download) | uploading / posting content online |
| **aadhode** | አአꬎዴ | `N` | aadhdhida wode | ኃላፊ ጊዜ (Past Tense) | past tense |
| **ha7ode** | ሀዖዴ | `N` | ha77i wode | የአሁን ጊዜ (Present Tense) | present tense |
| **sinthode** | ሲንꬆዴ | `N` | sintha wode | የወደፊት ጊዜ (Future Tense) | future tense |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** 1. Adarso Qommota (4 Essay Types, 8.D Xaafo, p. 76): (1) Paatetha Adarso (Narrative essay), (2) Qonciso Adarso (Descriptive essay), (3) Qaari beso Adarso (Expository essay), (4) Palantho Adarso (Argumentative essay).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 1። አደርሶ ቆምሞተ (4 እስሰይ ትይፔስ፣ 8።ድ ጠአፎ፣ ፕ። 76)፦ (1) ፐአቴꬃ አደርሶ (ነርረቲቬ እስሰይ)፣ (2) ቆንጪሶ አደርሶ (ዴስጭሪፕቲቬ እስሰይ)፣ (3) ቀአሪ ቤሶ አደርሶ (እጥፖሲቶርይ እስሰይ)፣ (4) ፐለንꬆ አደርሶ (አርጉሜንተቲቬ እስሰይ)።
- **Amharic:** 1. አራቱ የድርሰት ዓይነቶች (8.D Xaafo፣ ገጽ 76)፦ (1) ተራኪ ድርሰት (Paatetha)፣ (2) ሥዕላዊ/ገላጭ ድርሰት (Qonciso)፣ (3) አብራሪ ድርሰት (Qaari beso)፣ (4) አመዛዛኝ/አከራካሪ ድርሰት (Palantho)።
- **English:** 1. Four Essay Genres (8.D Xaafo, p. 76): Narrative (Paatetha), Descriptive (Qonciso), Expository (Qaari beso), and Argumentative (Palantho).
- **Gofatho Latin:** 2. Wode Bessiya Peeshota (3 Verb Tenses, 8.F Qawosa, pp. 79–80): (1) Aadhode (Past: Si7is, Mis, Xaafis, Nabbabasu, Haasayis — Malla: 'Na7ay qumaa mis. Na7iyaa soo oosuwaa oothasu.'); (2) Ha7ode (Present: Si7ees, Mees, Xaafees, Nabbabaawusu, Haasayees — Malla: 'Orzaama miidiyaa kiitata nabbabaawusu.'); (3) Sinthode (Future: Si7ana, Maana, Xaafana, Nabbabana, Haasayana — Malla: 'Nuuni soo oosuwaa dogonna mela akeekana.').
- **Gofatho Fidel:** 2። ዎዴ ቤስሲየ ፔእሾተ (3 ቬርብ ቴንሴስ፣ 8።ፍ ቀዎሰ፣ ፕፕ። 79–80)፦ (1) አአꬎዴ (ፐስት፦ ሲዒስ፣ ሚስ፣ ጠአፊስ፣ ነብበበሱ፣ ሀአሰዪስ — መልለ፦ 'ነዓይ ቁመአ ሚስ። ነዒየአ ሶኦ ኦኦሱወአ ኦኦꬃሱ።')፤ (2) ሀዖዴ (ፕሬሴንት፦ ሲዔእስ፣ ሜእስ፣ ጠአፌእስ፣ ነብበበአዉሱ፣ ሀአሰዬእስ — መልለ፦ 'ኦርዘአመ ሚኢዲየአ ኪኢተተ ነብበበአዉሱ።')፤ (3) ሲንꬆዴ (ፉቱሬ፦ ሲዓነ፣ መአነ፣ ጠአፈነ፣ ነብበበነ፣ ሀአሰየነ — መልለ፦ 'ኑኡኒ ሶኦ ኦኦሱወአ ዶጎንነ ሜለ አኬእከነ።')።
- **Amharic:** 2. ጊዜ አመልካች ግሶች (8.F Qawosa፣ ገጽ 79–80)፦ (1) ኃላፊ ጊዜ (Aadhode፦ mis, oothasu)፤ (2) የአሁን ጊዜ (Ha7ode፦ mees, nabbabaawusu)፤ (3) የወደፊት ጊዜ (Sinthode፦ maana, akeekana)።
- **English:** 2. Tense-Aspect Verbal Paradigms (8.F Qawosa, pp. 79–80): Complete conjugation of Si7- ('hear'), M- ('eat'), Xaaf- ('write'), Nabbab- ('read'), and Haasay- ('speak') across Past (Aadhode), Present (Ha7ode), and Future (Sinthode).

#### E. Proverbs, Idioms, Riddles & Oral Literature (`Leemiso / Tosse / Aaqeta / Sedhemenkke / Qan77ishe`)
| Category | Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Tosse (Proverb, p. 77) | **Miday bidaa gakkees.** | ሚደይ ቢደአ ገክኬእስ። | የበላ የሄደውን ይደርሳል (ምግብ የበላ ሰው ብርታት ያገኛል)። | He who has eaten catches up with the one who has already set out. |
| Tosse (Proverb, p. 78) | **Haathi miiccishin mees.** | ሀአꬂ ሚኢጭጪሺን ሜእስ። | ውኃ እየሳቀ ይበላል (ይወስዳል)። | Water devours while smiling (calm appearances can hide danger, like alcohol). |
| Tosse (Proverb, p. 78) | **Uloy kallin pili cammees.** | ኡሎይ ከልሊን ፒሊ ጨምሜእስ። | ሆድ ሲጠግብ ቅልጥም ይመራል። | When the stomach is full, even bone marrow tastes bitter. |
| Tosse (Proverb, p. 78) | **Yaare muussi bini qaallis ulo daakkis.** | የአሬ ሙኡስሲ ቢኒ ቀአልሊስ ኡሎ ደአክኪስ። | የስስት (የድግስ) መብል ጥንትም የቃሊስን ሆድ ቀደደ። | Greedy overeating tore open Qaallis's stomach even in olden times. |

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 6, 7 & 8 (8.F Qawosa, pp. 79–80): Construct sentences in Aadhode (Si7is, Mis, Xaafis, Nabbabasu, Haasayis), Ha7ode (Si7ees, Mees, Xaafees, Nabbabaawusu, Haasayees), and Sinthode (Si7ana, Maana, Xaafana, Nabbabana, Haasayana).
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 6፣ 7 & 8 (8።ፍ ቀዎሰ፣ ፕፕ። 79–80)፦ ጮንስትሩጭት ሴንቴንጬስ ኢን አአꬎዴ (ሲዒስ፣ ሚስ፣ ጠአፊስ፣ ነብበበሱ፣ ሀአሰዪስ)፣ ሀዖዴ (ሲዔእስ፣ ሜእስ፣ ጠአፌእስ፣ ነብበበአዉሱ፣ ሀአሰዬእስ)፣ አንድ ሲንꬆዴ (ሲዓነ፣ መአነ፣ ጠአፈነ፣ ነብበበነ፣ ሀአሰየነ)።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 6፣ 7 እና 8 (ሰዋስው፣ ገጽ 79–80)፦ በኃላፊ ጊዜ (Aadhode)፣ በአሁን ጊዜ (Ha7ode) እና በወደፊት ጊዜ (Sinthode) ግሶች ዓረፍተ ነገር መመሥረት።
- **English:** Exercises 6, 7 & 8 (Verb Tense Practice, pp. 79–80): Compose sentences using Past, Present, and Future verb forms.

### Gr10_Ch9 — Shemppo 9: Ooso Medho (Entrepreneurship) | ሼምፕፖ 9፦ ኦኦሶ ሜꬎ | ምዕራፍ 9፦ ሥራ ፈጠራና የድርጊትና ተደራጊ ግሶች | Chapter 9: Entrepreneurship, Overcoming Poverty, and Active vs. Passive Voice (Os-meqo & Ost-meqo)
- **Pages:** Book pp. 76–83 (PDF pp. 81–88) • **Source:** [Grade 10 Student Book](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1mKIcfMoMgnZ9Pa657NWmE-OGND2wlveM/view?usp=drivesdk)
- **Reading Passage (`Nabbabo`):** *Ooso Medho (Entrepreneurship)* (ኦኦሶ ሜꬎ (እንትሬፕሬኔኡርሺፕ)) — ሥራ ፈጠራ (Entrepreneurship)፦ ታሪክ፣ ጥቅምና አራቱ የሥራ ፈጠራ ዘርፎች / Entrepreneurship: Definition, Prehistoric Obsidian Trade in New Guinea (17,000 BCE), Proverbs of Diligence, and 4 Types of Enterprises (pp. 82–84 (Book pp. 77–79))

#### A. Key Reading Passage Sentences (`Nabbabo` — 4-Way Alignment)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel (`ኦራꬃ ጫቁዋ`) | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Ooso medho guussay issi asi gidin cugan kase oosettiyaa oosoy demisiyaa demishay guuxxishin maanaw, uyanaw, ma77anawnne danccanaw qaammishin he pacciyaa kunthanaw medhdhiya ooso. | ኦኦሶ ሜꬎ ጉኡስሰይ ኢስሲ አሲ ጊዲን ጩገን ከሴ ኦኦሴትቲየአ ኦኦሶይ ዴሚሲየአ ዴሚሻይ ጉኡጥጢሺን መአነው፣ ኡየነው፣ መዕዓነውንኔ ደንጭጨነው ቀአምሚሺን ሄ ፐጭጪየአ ኩንꬃነው ሜꬍꬊየ ኦኦሶ። | ሥራ ፈጠራ ማለት አንድ ሰው ወይም ቡድን ቀደም ሲል የሚሠራው ሥራ የሚያስገኘው ገቢ ሲያንስና ለኑሮው ሲጎድልበት ያንን ክፍተት ለመሙላት የሚፈጥረው አዲስ ሥራ ነው። | Entrepreneurship (ooso medho) is the creation of new productive work by an individual or group to overcome income shortages and improve their standard of living. |
| Nu gade Goofa asay tossishe, 'Uttiday dulle pittees, qaaxxiday qanthe pittees' geyssa mela medhdhi oosoy bonchchisees, asaara erisees, dureyees. | ኑ ገዴ ጎኦፈ አሰይ ቶስሲሼ፣ 'ኡትቲደይ ዱልሌ ፒትቴእስ፣ ቀአጥጢደይ ቀንꬄ ፒትቴእስ' ጌይስሰ ሜለ ሜꬍꬊ ኦኦሶይ ቦንችቺሴእስ፣ አሰአረ እሪሴእስ፣ ዱሬዬእስ። | የአገራችን የጎፋ ሕዝብ 'የተቀመጠ መቀመጫውን ያራግፋል፣ የተንቀሳቀሰ ግን ቅቤ ይቀባል (ይበለጽጋል)' ብሎ እንደሚተርተው ሥራ ፈጠራ ያስከብራል፣ ከሰው ጋር ያስተዋውቃል፣ ያበለጽጋል። | As the Gofatho proverb says, 'He who sits brushes dust off his seat, while he who moves and works prospers,' entrepreneurship brings honor, connection, and wealth. |

#### B. Dialogues & Oral Discourse (`Haasaya`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 2 (9.B Haasaya, p. 82): Pair and group discussion on 6 questions: 1. Issi asi waani duretanaw dandda7iyaakko tobbite. 2. Wode suuren go7etetharanne ooso medhora waani gahettiyaako haasayite. 6. Tamaari wurssidi kawo ooso oothiko lo7ooye ooso medhdhi oothiko lo7oo?
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 2 (9።ብ ሀአሰየ፣ ፕ። 82)፦ ፐኢር አንድ ግሮኡፕ ዲስጩስሲኦን ኦን 6 ቁእስቲኦንስ፦ 1። ኢስሲ አሲ ወአኒ ዱሬተነው ደንድደዒየአክኮ ቶብቢቴ። 2። ዎዴ ሱኡሬን ጎዔቴꬃረንኔ ኦኦሶ ሜꬎረ ወአኒ ገሄትቲየአኮ ሀአሰዪቴ። 6። ተመአሪ ዉርስሲዲ ከዎ ኦኦሶ ኦኦꬂኮ ሎዖኦዬ ኦኦሶ ሜꬍꬊ ኦኦꬂኮ ሎዖኦ?
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 2 (9.B ንግግር፣ ገጽ 82)፦ 1. አንድ ሰው እንዴት ሊበለጽግ እንደሚችል ተወያዩ። 2. የጊዜ አጠቃቀምና ሥራ ፈጠራ እንዴት እንደሚገናኙ ተነጋገሩ። 6. ተምሮ ከጨረሱ በኋላ የመንግሥት ሥራ መጠበቅ ይሻላል ወይስ የራስ ሥራ መፍጠር?
- **English:** Exercise 2 (9.B Oral Discussion, p. 82): Discuss pathways to prosperity, time management in entrepreneurship, and self-employment vs. waiting for government employment after graduation.

#### C. Chapter Vocabulary Glossary (`Qabirshshe / Ooratha Qaala`)
| Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | POS | Gofatho Synonym / Note | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **ooso medho** | ኦኦሶ ሜꬎ | `NP` | ooratha ooso medhdhi ooso | ሥራ ፈጠራ (Entrepreneurship) | entrepreneurship / job creation |
| **tuutummees** | ቱኡቱምሜእስ | `V` | baaxettees / butettees | ይታገላል / ይጥራል | strives / works persistently |
| **kabbasha** | ከብበሻ | `N` | waaxethi / deexo tooho | ቀንበር / ሸክም (ለምሳሌ፦ የድህነት ቀንበር / manqqotetha kabbasha) | yoke / heavy burden (e.g., yoke of poverty) |
| **koxethi** | ኮጤꬂ | `N` | menthi holo | መስበር / አሽቀንጥሮ መጣል | breaking / casting off |
| **marcco** | መርጭጮ | `N` | dongge xeera biraata / hiilla | ብልሃት / የጦር ወይም የአደን ጫፍ (ኦብሲዲያን) | spearhead / obsidian blade / craft |
| **os-meqo** | ኦስ-ሜቆ | `N` | yohaaddey ooso poliya meega qofa | የድርጊት ግስ / ዓረፍተ ነገር (Active Voice) | active voice |
| **ost-meqo** | ኦስት-ሜቆ | `N` | kaaloddey yohaadde bessan eqqiya meega qofa | ተደራጊ ግስ / ዓረፍተ ነገር (Passive Voice) | passive voice |

#### D. Grammar & Writing Rules (`Qawosa & Xaafo`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Os-meqonne Ost-meqo (Active Voice and Passive Voice, 9.F Qawosa, pp. 86–87, cited from Beddilu Wozza 2014): In Os-meqo (Active), the agent subject (Yohaadde) performs the action: 'Wozaakkoy keethe keexxis' ('Wozaakko built a house'); 'Bunnaaroy muuze tokkis' ('Bunnaaro planted a banana tree'). In Ost-meqo (Passive), the object (Kaalodde) takes nominative '-y', the agent optionally takes instrumental/locative '-n' or ablative '-ppe', and the verb takes passive '-ett-': (1) 'Muuzey Bunnaaron tokettis' or 'Muuzey Bunnaaroppe tokettis' ('The banana tree was planted by Bunnaaro'); (2) Agentless passive: 'Muuzey tokettis' ('The banana tree was planted').
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ኦስ-ሜቆንኔ ኦስት-ሜቆ (አጭቲቬ ቮኢጬ አንድ ፐስሲቬ ቮኢጬ፣ 9።ፍ ቀዎሰ፣ ፕፕ። 86–87፣ ጪቴድ ፍሮም ቤድዲሉ ዎዝዘ 2014)፦ ኢን ኦስ-ሜቆ (አጭቲቬ)፣ ꬄ አጌንት ሱብጄጭት (ዮሀአድዴ) ፔርፎርምስ ꬄ አጭቲኦን፦ 'ዎዘአክኮይ ኬእꬄ ኬእጥጢስ' ('ዎዘአክኮ ቡኢልት አ ሆኡሴ')፤ 'ቡንነአሮይ ሙኡዜ ቶክኪስ' ('ቡንነአሮ ፕለንቴድ አ በነነ ትሬእ')። ኢን ኦስት-ሜቆ (ፐስሲቬ)፣ ꬄ ኦብጄጭት (ከአሎድዴ) ተኬስ ኖሚነቲቬ '-ይ'፣ ꬄ አጌንት ኦፕቲኦነልልይ ተኬስ ኢንስትሩሜንተል/ሎጨቲቬ '-ን' ኦር አብለቲቬ '-ፕፔ'፣ አንድ ꬄ ቬርብ ተኬስ ፐስሲቬ '-እትት-'፦ (1) 'ሙኡዜይ ቡንነአሮን ቶኬትቲስ' ኦር 'ሙኡዜይ ቡንነአሮፕፔ ቶኬትቲስ' ('ꬄ በነነ ትሬእ ወስ ፕለንቴድ ብይ ቡንነአሮ')፤ (2) አጌንትሌስስ ፐስሲቬ፦ 'ሙኡዜይ ቶኬትቲስ' ('ꬄ በነነ ትሬእ ወስ ፕለንቴድ')።
- **Amharic:** የድርጊት (Os-meqo) እና የተደራጊ (Ost-meqo) ዓረፍተ ነገሮች (9.F Qawosa፣ ገጽ 86–87)፦ በድርጊት ዓ.ነገር፦ 'ቡናሮ ሙዝ ተከለ (Bunnaaroy muuze tokkis)'። ወደ ተደራጊ ሲቀየር፦ (1) 'ሙዙ በቡናሮ ተተከለ (Muuzey Bunnaaron/Bunnaaroppe tokettis)' ወይም (2) 'ሙዙ ተተከለ (Muuzey tokettis)'።
- **English:** Active Voice (Os-meqo) and Passive Voice (Ost-meqo) Derivations (9.F Qawosa, pp. 86–87): Formalizes agentive passive ('-n' / '-ppe' on demoted agent + '-ett-' on verb) and agentless passive ('Muuzey tokettis').

#### E. Proverbs, Idioms, Riddles & Oral Literature (`Leemiso / Tosse / Aaqeta / Sedhemenkke / Qan77ishe`)
| Category | Gofatho Latin | Gofatho Fidel | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Tosse (Proverb, p. 83) | **Uttiday dulle pittees, qaaxxiday qanthe pittees.** | ኡትቲደይ ዱልሌ ፒትቴእስ፣ ቀአጥጢደይ ቀንꬄ ፒትቴእስ። | የተቀመጠ መቀመጫውን ያራግፋል፣ የተንቀሳቀሰ (የሠራ) ግን ቅቤ ይቀባል (ይበለጽጋል)። | He who sits idle brushes dust off his seat, while he who moves and works prospers. |
| Tosse (Proverb, p. 85) | **Maahiyaa goynaa oykkopa; oykkiko yeddopa.** | መአሂየአ ጎይነአ ኦይክኮፐ፤ ኦይክኪኮ ዬድዶፐ። | የነብር ጭራ አትያዝ፤ ከያዝክ ግን አትልቀቅ (የጀመርከውን ሥራ በጽናት ፈጽም)። | Do not grab a leopard by the tail; if you do, do not let go (finish what you start with unwavering resolve). |

#### F. Student Exercises & Verified Answer Keys (`Meeze`)
- **Gofatho Latin:** Meeze 6 (9.F Qawosa Os-meqo -> Ost-meqo, p. 87): Transform active sentences into passive voice: 1. Wolqqi tuussi qanxxis -> Tuussay Wolqqin (Wolqqippe) qanxxettis; 2. Woraabi malddo zeris -> Malddoy Woraabin zerettis; 3. Xawarqqa miizi maathasu -> Miiziya Xawarqqin maasettasu; 6. Barddimoy keethe keexxees -> Keethay Barddimon keexettees; 8. Ammanoy ooso medhdhis -> Oosoy Ammanon medhdhettis; 9. Kaalosey puuto tokkana -> Puutoy Kaalosen tokettana.
- **Gofatho Fidel:** ሜእዜ 6 (9።ፍ ቀዎሰ ኦስ-ሜቆ -> ኦስት-ሜቆ፣ ፕ። 87)፦ ትረንስፎርም አጭቲቬ ሴንቴንጬስ ኢንቶ ፐስሲቬ ቮኢጬ፦ 1። ዎልቅቂ ቱኡስሲ ቀንጥጢስ -> ቱኡስሰይ ዎልቅቂን (ዎልቅቂፕፔ) ቀንጥጤትቲስ፤ 2። ዎረአቢ መልድዶ ዜሪስ -> መልድዶይ ዎረአቢን ዜሬትቲስ፤ 3። ጠወርቅቀ ሚኢዚ መአꬃሱ -> ሚኢዚየ ጠወርቅቂን መአሴትተሱ፤ 6። በርድዲሞይ ኬእꬄ ኬእጥጤእስ -> ኬእꬃይ በርድዲሞን ኬእጤትቴእስ፤ 8። አምመኖይ ኦኦሶ ሜꬍꬊስ -> ኦኦሶይ አምመኖን ሜꬍꬌትቲስ፤ 9። ከአሎሴይ ፑኡቶ ቶክከነ -> ፑኡቶይ ከአሎሴን ቶኬትተነ።
- **Amharic:** መልመጃ 6 (ከድርጊት ወደ ተደራጊ መቀየር፣ ገጽ 87)፦ 1. Wolqqi tuussi qanxxis -> Tuussay Wolqqin qanxxettis፤ 2. Woraabi malddo zeris -> Malddoy Woraabin zerettis፤ 6. Barddimoy keethe keexxees -> Keethay Barddimon keexettees።
- **English:** Exercise 6 (Active to Passive Voice Transformation, p. 87): Convert all 10 active sentences into passive voice across past (-ettis), present (-ettees), and future (-ettana) tenses.

| **hayso / paatetha adarso** | ሀይሶ / ፐአቴꬃ አደርሶ | ተራኪ ድርሰት | narrative essay |

| **(wuda) cuga tobba** | (ዉደ) ጩገ ቶብበ | የቡድን ውይይት | group discussion |
| **xeelo** | ጤእሎ | ምልከታ | observation |
| **yara** | የረ | አካላይ/አጠቃላይ ተጠኚ | population (research target) |
| **zaari be7o** | ዘአሪ ቤዖ | መከለስ/ክለሳ | revising / review |

### 4.2 Grade 9 Official Glossary (`Qaalata Birshshetha`, PDF p. 83 / Book p. 79)
| Gofatho Headword | Gofatho Fidel | Gofatho Definition (`Birshshethi`) | Amharic (`በአማርኛ`) | English |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Aadhode** | አአꬎዴ | Kase haasayettida | ሃላፊ ጊዜ | Past tense (Gofatho def: Kase haasayettida) |
| **Abbe qosonna** | አብቤ ቆሶንነ | Minjjonna | ሳይለግሙ | Without holding back / wholeheartedly (Gofatho def: Minjjonna) |
| **Ashirtto** | አሺርትቶ | Guye atethi | መስነፍ | Laziness / lagging behind (Gofatho def: Guye atethi) |
| **Baaso dichcha** | በአሶ ዲችቻ | Eqota | መሠረተ ልማት | Infrastructure (Gofatho def: Eqota) |
| **Besode** | ቤሶዴ | Bessenne wode | መቸት | Setting of a story/play (Gofatho def: Bessenne wode) |
| **Cawa gate** | ጨወ ገቴ | Ooso ciga | ደመወዝ | Salary / wage (Gofatho def: Ooso ciga) |
| **Daassi** | ደአስሲ | Meega qofata shiiqo | አንቀጽ | Paragraph (Gofatho def: Meega qofata shiiqo) |
| **Dano** | ደኖ | Qopponna gakkiya meto | አደጋ | Accident / disaster (Gofatho def: Qopponna gakkiya meto) |
| **Dom77e** | ዶምዕዔ | Qofa zokkotho | ግጭት | Conflict (Gofatho def: Qofa zokkotho) |
| **Gaagla** | ገአግለ | Gallasa, ageena, laythi | ቀን | Date: day, month, year (Gofatho def: Gallasa, ageena, laythi) |
| **Gelosa** | ጌሎሰ | Gelida sohon | አገባብ | Contextual usage (Gofatho def: Gelida sohon) |
| **Giddotiyasa** | ጊድዶቲየሰ | Ubbay gahettiyasa | ማዕከል | Center / hub (Gofatho def: Ubbay gahettiyasa) |
| **Inddusttere** | ኢንድዱስትቴሬ | Maashinen muruutiso | ኢንዳስትሪ | Industry (Gofatho def: Maashinen muruutiso) |
| **Isikkirpptte** | ኢሲክኪርፕፕትቴ | Diraama xuufe | ኢስክርብት | Script (Gofatho def: Diraama xuufe) |
| **Moodethi** | ሞኦዴꬂ | Shaakki woso | መመደብ | Classification / categorization (Gofatho def: Shaakki woso) |
| **Mor77ethi** | ሞርዕዔꬂ | Nam77a qofo | ማመንታት | Hesitation / doubt (Gofatho def: Nam77a qofo) |
| **Odaawa** | ኦደአወ | Adarssanchchi medhdhida ase | ገጸ ባህሪ | Character (Gofatho def: Adarssanchchi medhdhida ase) |
| **Paabirkka** | ፐአቢርክከ | Miisheta muruutisiya eqota | ፋብርካ | Factory (Gofatho def: Miisheta muruutisiya eqota) |
| **Qaye bira** | ቀዬ ቢረ | Qoodettiya bira | ጥሬ ገንዘብ | Liquid cash (Gofatho def: Qoodettiya bira) |
| **Shica** | ሺጨ | Dure | ባለጸጋ | Wealthy / rich person (Gofatho def: Dure) |
| **Wallotethi** | ወልሎቴꬂ | Koyidaatho | ነጻነት | Freedom / liberty (Gofatho def: Koyidaatho) |
| **Xolqqethi** | ጦልቅቄꬂ | Zaari beso, odo | ግብረ-መልስ | Feedback / response (Gofatho def: Zaari beso, odo) |
| **Zedhe** | ዜꬌ | Hiithe | ከርሰ ምድር | Subsoil / underground (Gofatho def: Hiithe) |
| **Zeebulssi** | ዜእቡልስሲ | Carkko kokkorssi | የአየር ሞገድ | Airwave / broadcast wave (Gofatho def: Carkko kokkorssi) |

### 4.3 Grade 10 Official Idioms Glossary (`Aaqeta`, PDF p. 88 / Book p. 83)
| Idiom (`Aaqeta` Latin) | Gofatho Fidel | Gofatho Explanation | Amharic (`አማርኛ`) | English Meaning |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Aawu mithi** | አአዉ ሚꬂ | bare, attonnabaa | የማይቀር ነገር (የአባት ዕዳ) | Something inevitable / unavoidable duty (Gofatho def: bare, attonnabaa) |
| **Achche mittis** | አችቼ ሚትቲስ | Ceeggis, diccenna | አረጀ፣ አያድግም (ቀጨጨ) | Grew old without growing tall / stunted (Gofatho def: Ceeggis, diccenna) |
| **Aacis** | አአጪስ | yelettis, daris | ተወለደ፣ በዛ (ተራባ) | Multiplied, reproduced abundantly (Gofatho def: yelettis, daris) |
| **Ayfen gelis** | አይፌን ጌሊስ | bitettis | በዓይን ገባ (ቡዳ በላው/ተቀናበት) | Caught someone's eye / struck by envy or evil eye (Gofatho def: bitettis) |
| **Ayfe kumees** | አይፌ ኩሜእስ | Teematees, oyyanttees | ዓይን ይሞላል፣ ያስደስታል | Fills the eye / looks impressive and pleasing (Gofatho def: Teematees, oyyanttees) |
| **Asho suuthan yuuyis** | አሾ ሱኡꬃን ዩኡዪስ | mida uyidabay ashos giigis | የበላውና የጠጣው ተስማማው | Food and drink nourished the body well (Gofatho def: mida uyidabay ashos giigis) |
| **Biitta muzis** | ቢኢትተ ሙዚስ | baayisis, wadhdhis | መሬት አበላ፣ ጣለ (አሸነፈ) | Knocked to the ground / defeated (Gofatho def: baayisis, wadhdhis) |
| **Biitti ma77is (genthis)** | ቢኢትቲ መዕዒስ (ጌንꬂስ) | hayqqis | ሞተ (መሬት ለበሰው) | Passed away / died (Gofatho def: hayqqis) |
| **Biitti qaaxxis** | ቢኢትቲ ቀአጥጢስ | dere daganthis | አገር አስደነገጠ | Shocked the whole land (Gofatho def: dere daganthis) |
| **Coocen gelis** | ጮኦጬን ጌሊስ | manqqis | ደኸየ (በድህነት ወደቀ) | Fell into extreme poverty (Gofatho def: manqqis) |
| **Cuchcha mal77o** | ጩችቻ መልዕዖ | mala lo7o | ደስ የሚል፣ ተወዳጅ (ምራቁ ጣፋጭ) | Sweet-natured, charming person (Gofatho def: mala lo7o) |
| **Dada** | ደደ | zambbo pala | ነውር / ጸያፍ ድርጊት | Taboo transgression / reckless act (Gofatho def: zambbo pala) |
| **Daada yaagano** | ደአደ የአገኖ | duussas injje | ለኑሮ ምቹ / ስምምነት ያለው | Harmonious, comfortable for living together (Gofatho def: duussas injje) |
| **Dooni ace mo** | ዶኦኒ አጬ ሞ | dooni pgoppo | አንደበተ ርቱዕ | Eloquent speaker (Gofatho def: dooni poppo) |
| **Dubbushay lee7is** | ዱብቡሻይ ሌእዒስ | wogiaa asi dhayis | አደባባዩ ጎደለ (አዋቂ ሽማግሌ ጠፋ) | The assembly place became empty / loss of wise elders (Gofatho def: wogaa asi dhayis) |
| **Dhukettis** | ꬉኬትቲስ | dicci simmonna hayqqis | ሳያድግ በአጭሩ ተቀጨ (ሞተ) | Died prematurely before reaching maturity (Gofatho def: dicci simmonna hayqqis) |
| **Eesho ekkis** | እእሾ እክኪስ | daganthis | አስደነገጠ | Startled / terrified someone (Gofatho def: daganthis) |
| **Geda giddo gelis** | ጌደ ጊድዶ ጌሊስ | wodey matis | ጊዜው ተቃረበ (እግር ስር ደረሰ) | Time is running out / deadline approached (Gofatho def: wodey matis) |
| **Geda oykkis** | ጌደ ኦይክኪስ | Woossis | ለመነ፣ ተማጸነ (እግር ያዘ) | Begged / pleaded humbly (Gofatho def: Woossis) |
| **Gattanne hoota** | ገትተንኔ ሆኦተ | siga | ሰላምና እርቅ | Peaceful reconciliation and harmony (Gofatho def: siga) |
| **Gorddoma** | ጎርድዶመ | iita pala | ክፉ ደባ / ተንኮል | Malicious plot or curse (Gofatho def: iita pala) |
| **Haathan sussi yeggees** | ሀአꬃን ሱስሲ ዬግጌእስ | ooso achchees, ooso oykkees | ሥራ ይጀምራል | Starts working diligently (Gofatho def: ooso achchees, ooso oykkees) |
| **Issi gedan essis** | ኢስሲ ጌደን እስሲስ | un77ethis | አጣደፈ፣ አስጨነቀ (በአንድ እግር አቆመ) | Cornered / pressured someone relentlessly (Gofatho def: un77ethis) |
| **Kana siidhe** | ከነ ሲኢꬌ | irxxa | ቀዝቃዛና እርጥብ (የውሻ አፍንጫ) | Cold and wet (Gofatho def: irxxa) |
| **Maathara boye** | መአꬃረ ቦዬ | injje | ምቹ፣ ቀላል (ጎደሬ በወተት) | Easy and agreeable (Gofatho def: injje) |
| **Mana bangga** | መነ በንግገ | dharccettis, maara dhayis | ተበታተነ፣ ሥርዓት አጣ | Scattered in disorder (Gofatho def: dharccettis, maara dhayis) |
| **Ola gade** | ኦለ ገዴ | haaho, allaga gade | ሩቅ/ባዳ አገር | Distant, foreign, or hostile land (Gofatho def: haaho, allaga gade) |
